# === 模块：backend/app/core/judge_engine/base.py ===
# [功能] 评判引擎抽象接口，统一 evaluate 契约
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""打分引擎抽象基类 —— 定义 evaluate 接口契约。"""
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any
@dataclass
class JudgeResult:
    """评分结果数据类。"""
    scores: dict[str, float] = field(default_factory=dict)
    total: float = 0.0
    reason: str = ""
    token_cost: int = 0
class BaseJudge(ABC):
    """打分引擎抽象基类。
    所有打分引擎实现必须实现 evaluate 方法。
    """
    @abstractmethod
    async def evaluate(
        self,
        trace_steps: list[dict[str, Any]],
        expected_output: str,
        expected_tools: list[str],
    ) -> JudgeResult:
        """对一次执行轨迹进行多维度评分。
        Args:
            trace_steps: Agent 执行过程中的 ReAct 步骤数组。
            expected_output: 期望输出文本。
            expected_tools: 期望调用的工具名称列表。
        Returns:
            JudgeResult: 包含各维度分数、总分和扣分说明。
        """
        raise NotImplementedError
# === 模块：backend/app/core/judge_engine/llm_judge.py ===
# [功能] 混合评判：规则预打分 + 大模型精修，输出可解释指标
# [创新] 无密钥自动降级为纯规则；有密钥再精修；SHA256 结果缓存
# [算法] 工具准确率/答案正确性/推理连贯性三维加权汇总
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""LLM-as-Judge with hybrid rule-based + LLM scoring.
Architecture: 1) Rule-based pre-scoring always runs,
2) LLM-as-Judge refinement when API key is available.
3) Falls back to pure rule-based when no API key.
"""
import hashlib
import json
import logging
import os
from openai import AsyncOpenAI
from tenacity import before_sleep_log, retry, retry_if_exception_type
from tenacity import stop_after_attempt, wait_exponential
from app.core.judge_engine.base import BaseJudge
from app.core.judge_engine.metrics import calc_tool_accuracy, extract_answer_text
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
DIMENSION_WEIGHTS = {"tool_accuracy": 40.0, "answer_correctness": 40.0, "reasoning_coherence": 20.0}
SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are a rigorous AI Agent evaluation expert. Score across 3 dimensions.
1. Tool call accuracy (0-40) 2. Answer correctness (0-40) 3. Reasoning coherence (0-20)
Output ONLY valid JSON with fields: scores, total, reason."""
class LLMJudge(BaseJudge):
    """Hybrid scoring engine: rule-based + optional LLM refinement."""
    def __init__(self, api_key=[REDACTED_API_KEY], base_url=None, model="gpt-4o-mini"):
        api_key = [REDACTED_API_KEY] or os.environ.get("OPENAI_API_KEY", "")
        self.has_api_key = [REDACTED_API_KEY])
        if self.has_api_key:
            self.client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED_API_KEY], base_url=base_url)
        else:
            self.client = None
        self.model = model
    async def evaluate(self, trace_steps, expected_output, expected_tools=None):
        cache_key = self._make_cache_key(trace_steps, expected_output, expected_tools or [])
        return await self._cached_evaluate(cache_key, trace_steps, expected_output, tuple(expected_tools or...
    @staticmethod
    def _make_cache_key(trace_steps, expected_output, expected_tools):
        raw = json.dumps({'steps': trace_steps, 'output': expected_output, 'tools': sorted(expected_tools)}...
        return hashlib.sha256(raw.encode('utf-8')).hexdigest()
    _cache = {}
    async def _cached_evaluate(self, _cache_key, trace_steps, expected_output, expected_tools):
        if _cache_key in self._cache:
            return self._cache[_cache_key]
        result = await self._do_evaluate(trace_steps, expected_output, expected_tools or [])
        if len(LLMJudge._cache) > 128:
            LLMJudge._cache.clear()
        LLMJudge._cache[_cache_key] = result
        return result
    def _pre_score(self, steps, expected_output, expected_tools):
        actual_tools = self._extract_tool_names(steps)
        tool_pct, tool_reason = calc_tool_accuracy(actual_tools, expected_tools)
        tool_score = round(tool_pct * 0.4, 1)
        extracted = self._extract_final_answer(steps)
        answer_score, answer_reason = self._lexical_answer_score(extracted, expected_output)
        coh_score, coh_reason = self._heuristic_coherence_score(steps)
        step_analysis = self._analyze_steps(steps, expected_tools)
        pre_total = tool_score + answer_score + coh_score
        return {
            'scores': {'tool_accuracy': tool_score, 'answer_correctness': answer_score, 'reasoning_coherenc...
            'total': pre_total,
            'reason': f'[Rule-based] Tool: {tool_reason} | Answer: {answer_reason} | Coherence: {coh_reason}',
            'token_cost': 0,
            'details': {'tool_accuracy': {'score': tool_score, 'reason': tool_reason, 'expected': expected_...
                'answer_correctness': {'score': answer_score, 'reason': answer_reason, 'extracted_answer': ...
                'reasoning_coherence': {'score': coh_score, 'reason': coh_reason, 'iteration_count': len(st...
            'step_analysis': step_analysis,
        }
    @retry(stop=stop_after_attempt(3), wait=wait_exponential(multiplier=1, min=2, max=10),
            retry=retry_if_exception_type((TimeoutError, ConnectionError)),
            before_sleep=before_sleep_log(logger, logging.WARNING), reraise=True)
    async def _llm_refine(self, steps, expected_output, expected_tools, pre_score):
        prompt = self._build_prompt(steps, expected_output, expected_tools, pre_score)
        response = await self.client.chat.completions.create(model=self.model,
            messages=[{'role': 'system', 'content': SYSTEM_PROMPT}, {'role': 'user', 'content': prompt}],
            temperature=0.1, response_format={'type': 'json_object'})
        usage = response.usage
        token_cost = (usage.prompt_tokens if usage else 0) + (usage.completion_tokens if usage else 0)
        try:
            data = json.loads(response.choices[0].message.content or '{}')
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            result = dict(pre_score)
            result['token_cost'] = token_cost
            result['reason'] += ' [LLM refine failed]'
            result['mode'] = 'rule_only'
            return result
        scores = data.get('scores', {})
        for dim in ('tool_accuracy', 'answer_correctness', 'reasoning_coherence'):
            scores.setdefault(dim, pre_score['scores'].get(dim, 0.0))
        result = dict(pre_score)
        result['scores'] = scores
        result['total'] = float(data.get('total', pre_score['total']))
        result['reason'] = f'[LLM] {data.get("reason", pre_score["reason"])}'
        result['token_cost'] = token_cost
        result['mode'] = 'hybrid'
        return result
    async def _do_evaluate(self, steps, expected_output, expected_tools):
        pre = self._pre_score(steps, expected_output, expected_tools)
        if self.has_api_key and self.client:
            try:
                return await self._llm_refine(steps, expected_output, expected_tools, pre)
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.warning('LLM refine failed: %s', exc)
                pre['mode'] = 'rule_only'
                return pre
        pre['mode'] = 'rule_only'
        return pre
    @staticmethod
    def _extract_tool_names(steps):
        names, seen = [], set()
        for s in steps:
            n = s.get('action') or s.get('tool_name') or ''
            if n and n not in seen and n != 'final_answer':
                seen.add(n)
                names.append(n)
        return names
    @staticmethod
    def _extract_final_answer(steps):
        for s in reversed(steps):
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            if s.get('action') == 'final_answer':
                return s.get('action_input') or s.get('observation') or ''
            if s.get('type') == 'final_answer':
                return s.get('content') or s.get('observation') or ''
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            if s.get('thought') and not s.get('action'):
                return s['thought']
        return extract_answer_text(steps)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    @staticmethod
    def _lexical_answer_score(extracted, expected):
        if not expected:
            return 40.0, 'No expected output; full score.'
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        if not extracted:
            return 0.0, 'No answer found.'
        ex = set(expected.lower().split())
        ac = set(extracted.lower().split())
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        if not ex:
            return 40.0, 'No words; full score.'
        r = len(ex & ac) / len(ex)
        score = round(r * 40.0, 1)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        if r > 0.8:
            desc = 'High'
        elif r > 0.5:
            desc = 'Moderate'
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        elif r > 0.2:
            desc = 'Low'
        else:
            desc = 'Minimal'
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        return score, f'{desc} word overlap.'
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    @staticmethod
    def _heuristic_coherence_score(steps):
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        if not steps:
            return 20.0, 'No steps; default full score.'
        ded, reasons = 0.0, []
        seen = set()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        for s in steps:
            t = (s.get('thought') or s.get('content') or '').strip().lower()
            if t and t in seen and len(t) > 5:
                ded += 4.0
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                reasons.append(f'Repetition: {t[:40]}')
            elif t:
                seen.add(t)
        if len(steps) > 8:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            ded += min((len(steps) - 8) * 2, 6)
            reasons.append(f'High iteration count ({len(steps)}).')
        score = round(max(0.0, 20.0 - ded), 1)
        reason = '; '.join(reasons) if reasons else 'No issues.'
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        return score, reason
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    @staticmethod
    def _analyze_steps(steps, expected_tools):
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        result = []
        for i, s in enumerate(steps):
            e = {'index': i, 'has_thought': bool(s.get('thought') or s.get('content')),
                 'has_action': bool(s.get('action') or s.get('tool_name'))}
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            a = s.get('action') or s.get('tool_name') or ''
            if a:
                e['action_name'] = a
                e['is_expected'] = a in expected_tools if expected_tools else None
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            result.append(e)
        return result
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    @staticmethod
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    def _build_prompt(steps, expected_output, expected_tools, pre_score):
        import textwrap
        steps_str = json.dumps(steps, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
        pre_str = json.dumps({'scores': pre_score['scores'], 'total': pre_score['total']}, ensure_ascii=False)
        return textwrap.dedent(f'''
            ## Expected Output
            {expected_output}
            ## Expected Tools
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            {json.dumps(expected_tools, ensure_ascii=False)}
            ## Agent Steps
            {steps_str}
            ## Pre-Score (for reference)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            {pre_str}
            Please output refined score as JSON.
        ''').strip()
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    @staticmethod
    def _deep_tuple_to_list(obj):
        if isinstance(obj, tuple):
            return [LLMJudge._deep_tuple_to_list(item) for item in obj]
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        if isinstance(obj, dict):
            return {k: LLMJudge._deep_tuple_to_list(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
        if isinstance(obj, list):
            return [LLMJudge._deep_tuple_to_list(item) for item in obj]
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        return obj
# === 模块：backend/app/core/judge_engine/metrics.py ===
# [功能] 无外部模型调用的规则指标计算
# [算法] 期望工具集合差集惩罚：漏调与多余调用分别计罚
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""指标计算工具函数 —— 提供无 LLM 调用的轻量级辅助评分。"""
import re
from typing import Any
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def calc_tool_accuracy(
    actual_tool_names: list[str],
    expected_tools: list[str],
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
) -> tuple[float, str]:
    """计算工具调用准确率（0-100）。
    基于集合的精确匹配和召回率计算。
    Args:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        actual_tool_names: 实际调用的工具名称列表。
        expected_tools: 期望调用的工具名称列表。
    Returns:
        (score, reason): 分数（0-100）和说明文字。
    """
    actual_set = set(actual_tool_names)
    expected_set = set(expected_tools)
    if not expected_set:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        return 100.0, "无预期工具要求，默认满分。"
    missing = expected_set - actual_set
    extra = actual_set - expected_set
    penalty = 0.0
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    reasons = []
    if missing:
        penalty += len(missing) * 10.0
        reasons.append(f"缺少工具: {', '.join(sorted(missing))}")
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if extra:
        penalty += len(extra) * 10.0
        reasons.append(f"多余工具: {', '.join(sorted(extra))}")
    score = max(0.0, 100.0 - penalty)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    reason = "; ".join(reasons) if reasons else "全部预期工具均已调用，无多余调用。"
    return score, reason
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def extract_answer_text(steps: list[dict[str, Any]]) -> str:
    """从 ReAct 步骤数组中提取最终的答案文本。
    遍历步骤，取最后一个 role='assistant' 的内容作为最终答案。
    Args:
        steps: ReAct 步骤数组。
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    Returns:
        最终答案文本，未找到时返回空字符串。
    """
    for step in reversed(steps):
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        if step.get("role") == "assistant":
            content = step.get("content", "")
            if content:
                return content
        # 某些格式使用 type='final_answer'
        if step.get("type") == "final_answer":
            return step.get("content", "")
    return ""
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def _normalize(text: str) -> str:
    """归一化文本用于模糊比较。"""
    text = re.sub(r"\s+", " ", text).strip().lower()
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    return re.sub(r"[^\w\u4e00-\u9fff]", "", text)

# [功能] Runner 基类与结果结构，保证异步/同步路径一致
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Agent 执行器抽象基类。"""
from abc import ABC, abstractmethod
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Any
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
@dataclass
class AgentResult:
    """Agent 执行结果数据类。"""
    steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = field(default_factory=list)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    total_tokens: int = 0
    response_time_ms: int = 0
    status: str = "success"
    error_message: str = ""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    finished_at: datetime | None = None
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
class BaseAgentRunner(ABC):
    """Agent 执行器抽象基类。
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    所有 Agent 实现（OpenAI、Claude 等）必须实现 run 方法，
    返回统一的 AgentResult 数据结构。
    """
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    @abstractmethod
    async def run(
        self,
        user_query: str,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        agent_config: dict[str, Any],
    ) -> AgentResult:
        """执行一次 Agent 调用。
        Args:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            user_query: 用户输入指令。
            agent_config: Agent 配置参数（模型名称、温度等）。
        Returns:
            AgentResult: 包含执行步骤、Token 消耗和状态。
        """
        raise NotImplementedError
# === 模块：backend/app/core/agent_runner/openai_runner.py ===
# [功能] ReAct 执行器：Thought-Action-Observation 循环与轨迹落库

import json
import logging
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
import time
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from typing import Any
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
from openai import AsyncOpenAI
from app.core.agent_runner.base import AgentResult, BaseAgentRunner
from app.core.agent_runner.tool_sandbox import (

# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
@dataclass
class ToolDefinition:
    name: str
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    description: str
    parameters: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    fn: Callable[..., str] | None = None
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def to_openai_tool(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        params = self.parameters
        if params.get("type") == "object" and "properties" in params:
            schema = params
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        else:
            schema = {
                "type": "object",
                "properties": params,
                **({"required": list(params.keys())} if params else {}),
            }
        return {
            "type": "function",
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            "function": {
                "name": self.name,
                "description": self.description,
                "parameters": schema,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            },
        }
REACT_SYSTEM_PROMPT = """You are an AI assistant that follows the ReAct pattern.
For each step:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
1. **Thought**: Reason about what to do next.
2. **Action**: Choose an available tool, or write "final_answer".
3. **Action Input**: Provide parameters for the chosen action.
4. **Observation**: You will receive the result of your action.
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
Available tools:
"""
REACT_SINGLE_TURN_PROMPT = """Output in this format:
Thought: your reasoning
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
Action: tool_name or final_answer
Action Input: arguments
Final Answer: your response
Use Chinese or English as appropriate."""
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
@dataclass
class ReActStep:
    iteration: int
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    thought: str = ""
    action: str = ""
    action_input: str = ""
    observation: str = ""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    tokens: int = 0
    timestamp: float = 0.0
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        return {
            "iteration": self.iteration,
            "thought": self.thought,
            "action": self.action,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            "action_input": self.action_input,
            "observation": self.observation,
            "tokens": self.tokens,
        }
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
DEFAULT_TOOL_FUNCTIONS: dict[str, Callable[..., str]] = {
    name: meta["fn"] for name, meta in BUILTIN_TOOLS.items()
}
# Keep DEFAULT_TOOL_DEFS in sync with sandbox registry
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
DEFAULT_TOOL_DEFS = get_openai_tool_defs(["web_search", "calculator"])
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
class OpenAIReActRunner(BaseAgentRunner):
    """ReAct loop agent using OpenAI.
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    Implements: Thought -> Action -> Observation -> repeat -> Final Answer.
    Supports both function-calling mode and text-parsing mode.
    """
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def __init__(
        self,
        api_key: [REDACTED_API_KEY] | None = None,
        base_url: str | None = None,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        model: str = "gpt-4o-mini",
        max_iterations: int = 5,
    ) -> None:
        self.client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED_API_KEY], base_url=base_url)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        self.model = model
        self.max_iterations = max_iterations
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    async def run(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        self,
        query: str,
        tools: list[dict[str, Any]] | None = None,
    ) -> dict[str, Any]:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        tool_defs, tool_map = self._parse_tools(tools or DEFAULT_TOOL_DEFS)
        openai_tools = [t.to_openai_tool() for t in tool_defs]
        tool_descriptions = "\n".join(f"  - {t.name}: {t.description}" for t in tool_defs)
        system_prompt = REACT_SYSTEM_PROMPT + tool_descriptions + "\n\n" + REACT_SINGLE_TURN_PROMPT
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        messages: list[dict[str, Any]] = [
            {"role": "system", "content": system_prompt},
            {"role": "user", "content": query},
        ]
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        steps: list[ReActStep] = []
        total_tokens = 0
        final_answer: str | None = None
        status = "success"
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        error_message = ""
        start_time = time.monotonic()
        for iteration in range(self.max_iterations):
            step = ReActStep(iteration=iteration, timestamp=time.time())
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            try:
                response = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
                    model=self.model,
                    messages=messages,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                    tools=openai_tools if tool_defs else None,
                    tool_choice="auto" if tool_defs else None,
                    temperature=0,
                )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                choice = response.choices[0]
                msg = choice.message
                usage = response.usage
                step.tokens = (usage.total_tokens if usage else 0)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                total_tokens += step.tokens
                if msg.tool_calls:
                    for tc in msg.tool_calls:
                        fn_name = tc.function.name
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                        try:
                            fn_args = json.loads(tc.function.arguments)
                        except json.JSONDecodeError:
                            fn_args = {}
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                        step.action = fn_name
                        step.action_input = json.dumps(fn_args, ensure_ascii=False)
                        step.thought = msg.content or ""
                        step.observation = self._execute_tool(fn_name, fn_args, tool_map)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                        messages.append({
                            "role": "assistant",
                            "content": msg.content or "",
                            "tool_calls": [{
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                                "id": tc.id,
                                "type": "function",
                                "function": {"name": fn_name, "arguments": tc.function.arguments},
                            }],
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                        })
                        messages.append({
                            "role": "tool",
                            "tool_call_id": tc.id,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                            "content": step.observation,
                        })
                else:
                    content = msg.content or ""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    parsed = self._parse_step(content)
                    step.thought = parsed.get("thought", content)
                    step.action = parsed.get("action", "")
                    step.action_input = parsed.get("action_input", "")
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                    if step.action == "final_answer" or "final answer" in content.lower():
                        final_answer = parsed.get("action_input") or parsed.get("thought", content)
                        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
                        break
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    if step.action and step.action != "final_answer":
                        step.observation = self._execute_tool(
                            step.action,
                            self._parse_action_input(step.action_input),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                            tool_map,
                        )
                        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
                        messages.append({"role": "user", "content": f"Observation: {step.observation}"})
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                    else:
                        final_answer = content
                        messages.append({"role": "assistant", "content": content})
                        break
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            except Exception as exc:
                logger.exception("ReAct iteration %d failed: %s", iteration, exc)
                step.observation = f"[Error: {exc}]"
                if iteration == 0:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    status = "failed"
                    error_message = str(exc)
            steps.append(step)
        if final_answer is None and steps:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            last_step = steps[-1]
            final_answer = last_step.thought or last_step.observation or "[No answer]"
            if status == "success":
                status = "max_iterations_reached"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - start_time) * 1000)
        return {
            "steps": [s.to_dict() for s in steps],
            "total_tokens": total_tokens,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            "iterations": len(steps),
            "final_answer": final_answer,
            "status": status,
            "error_message": error_message,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            "response_time_ms": elapsed_ms,
        }
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def _parse_tools(self, raw_tools=None):
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        if raw_tools is None:
            return [], {}
        defs: list[ToolDefinition] = []
        for t in raw_tools:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            if isinstance(t, ToolDefinition):
                defs.append(t)
            elif isinstance(t, dict):
                fn_info = t.get("function", t)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                if isinstance(fn_info, dict):
                    defs.append(ToolDefinition(
                        name=fn_info.get("name", t.get("name", "unknown")),
                        description=fn_info.get("description", t.get("description", "")),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                        parameters=fn_info.get("parameters", {}).get("properties", fn_info.get("parameters"...
                        fn=t.get("fn") or fn_info.get("fn"),
                    ))
        return defs, {d.name: d for d in defs}
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    def _execute_tool(self, name: str, args: dict[str, Any], tool_map: dict[str, ToolDefinition]) -> str:
        """Execute tools via sandbox (timeout + truncation + allowlist)."""
        tool = tool_map.get(name)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        custom_fn = tool.fn if tool and tool.fn else DEFAULT_TOOL_FUNCTIONS.get(name)
        return run_tool_sandboxed(name, args or {}, custom_fn=custom_fn)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    @staticmethod
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def _parse_step(text: str) -> dict[str, str]:
        result: dict[str, str] = {}
        lines = text.strip().split("\n")
        prefixes = [
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            ("Thought:", "thought"), ("Thought：", "thought"),
            ("Action:", "action"), ("Action：", "action"),
            ("Action Input:", "action_input"), ("Action Input：", "action_input"),
            ("Observation:", "observation"), ("Observation：", "observation"),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            ("Final Answer:", "final_answer"), ("Final Answer：", "final_answer"),
        ]
        for line in lines:
            s = line.strip()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            for prefix, key in prefixes:
                if s.lower().startswith(prefix.lower()):
                    value = s[len(prefix):].strip()
                    if value:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                        result[key] = value
        return result
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    @staticmethod
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    def _parse_action_input(raw: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        if not raw:
            return {}
        try:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            return json.loads(raw)
        except (json.JSONDecodeError, TypeError):
            pass
        if "=" in raw:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            result = {}
            for part in raw.split(","):
                if "=" in part:
                    k, v = part.split("=", 1)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    result[k.strip()] = v.strip().strip("\"'")
            if result:
                return result
        return {"input": raw}
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
class OpenAIRunner(BaseAgentRunner):
    """Legacy single-turn executor. Retained for backward compatibility."""
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    def __init__(self, api_key: [REDACTED_API_KEY] | None = None, base_url: str | None = None) -> None:
        self.client = AsyncOpenAI(api_key=[REDACTED_API_KEY], base_url=base_url)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    async def run(self, user_query: str, agent_config: dict[str, Any]) -> AgentResult:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        model = agent_config.get("model", "gpt-4o")
        temperature = agent_config.get("temperature", 0)
        max_tokens = agent_config.get("max_tokens", 4096)
        start_time = time.monotonic()
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        try:
            response = await self.client.chat.completions.create(
                model=model,
                messages=[
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                    {"role": "system", "content": REACT_SYSTEM_PROMPT + "\n" + REACT_SINGLE_TURN_PROMPT},
                    {"role": "user", "content": user_query},
                ],
                temperature=temperature,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                max_tokens=max_tokens,
            )
            elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - start_time) * 1000)
            content = response.choices[0].message.content or ""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            total_tokens = (response.usage.total_tokens if response.usage else 0)
            steps = self._parse_react_steps(content)
            return AgentResult(steps=steps, total_tokens=total_tokens, response_time_ms=elapsed_ms, status=...
        except Exception as exc:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            elapsed_ms = int((time.monotonic() - start_time) * 1000)
            logger.exception("Agent execution failed: %s", exc)
            return AgentResult(steps=[{"role": "error", "content": str(exc)}], total_tokens=0, response_tim...
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    @staticmethod
    def _parse_react_steps(content: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
        steps: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
        lines = content.split("\n")
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        current_step: dict[str, Any] = {}
        step_role: str | None = None
        prefixes = [
            ("Thought:", "thought"), ("Thought：", "thought"),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            ("Action:", "action"), ("Action：", "action"),
            ("Action Input:", "action_input"), ("Action Input：", "action_input"),
            ("Observation:", "observation"), ("Observation：", "observation"),
            ("Final Answer:", "final_answer"), ("Final Answer：", "final_answer"),
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        ]
        for line in lines:
            s = line.strip()
            matched = False
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            for prefix, ptype in prefixes:
                if s.lower().startswith(prefix.lower()):
                    if current_step and step_role:
                        current_step["content"] = current_step.get("content", "").strip()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                        steps.append(current_step)
                    current_step = {"role": "tool" if ptype == "observation" else "assistant", "type": ptype}
                    step_role = ptype
                    value = s[len(prefix):].strip()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                    if ptype in ("thought", "final_answer", "observation"):
                        current_step["content"] = value
                    elif ptype == "action":
                        current_step["tool_name"] = value
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    elif ptype == "action_input":
                        current_step["tool_input"] = value
                    matched = True
                    break
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            if not matched and current_step and s:
                prev = current_step.get("content", "")
                current_step["content"] = (prev + "\n" + s).strip()
        if current_step and step_role:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            current_step["content"] = current_step.get("content", "").strip()
            steps.append(current_step)
        if not steps:
            steps.append({"role": "assistant", "type": "final_answer", "content": content.strip()})
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        return steps
# === 模块：backend/app/core/agent_runner/parser.py ===
# [功能] 从模型自由文本解析 Action / Action Input / Final Answer
# [创新] 多模式正则容错，兼容中英文混排
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Agent 输出解析器 —— 从 Agent 响应中提取结构化信息。"""
from typing import Any
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def parse_steps_from_response(response: str) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """从 Agent 原始响应文本中解析 ReAct 步骤。
    作为 OpenAIRunner._parse_react_steps 的独立封装，
    可在需要轻量级解析的场景下直接使用。
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    Args:
        response: Agent 返回的原始文本。
    Returns:
        结构化的步骤列表。
    """
    from app.core.agent_runner.openai_runner import OpenAIRunner
    return OpenAIRunner._parse_react_steps(response)
# === 模块：backend/app/core/agent_runner/tool_sandbox.py ===
# [功能] 工具沙箱：受限执行内置工具，防止任意代码执行
# [创新] AST 白名单 + 线程超时 + 输出截断，兼容 Windows
# [算法] 拒绝 Name/Call/Attribute，仅允许数值常量与四则运算
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Safe built-in tool registry and sandboxed execution."""
from __future__ import annotations
import ast
import json
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
import logging
import operator as op
import re
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor, TimeoutError as FuturesTimeout
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Callable
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# Global executor for tool timeouts (Windows-friendly vs signals)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
_EXECUTOR = ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=4, thread_name_prefix="tool-sandbox")
DEFAULT_TOOL_TIMEOUT_SEC = 3.0
MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS = 4000
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class ToolSandboxError(Exception):
    """Raised when tool execution is rejected or fails safely."""
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Safe built-in implementations (no network, no filesystem, no eval)
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
def tool_calculator(expression: str = "", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """Evaluate a restricted arithmetic expression."""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    expr = (expression or kwargs.get("expr") or "").strip()
    if not expr:
        return "Error: empty expression"
    if len(expr) > 200:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        return "Error: expression too long"
    allowed_ops = {
        ast.Add: op.add,
        ast.Sub: op.sub,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        ast.Mult: op.mul,
        ast.Div: op.truediv,
        ast.Pow: op.pow,
        ast.USub: op.neg,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        ast.UAdd: op.pos,
        ast.Mod: op.mod,
    }
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def _eval(node: ast.AST) -> float:
        if isinstance(node, ast.Expression):
            return _eval(node.body)
        if isinstance(node, ast.Constant):
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            if isinstance(node.value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(node.value, bool):
                return float(node.value)
            raise ValueError(f"Unsupported constant: {node.value!r}")
        if isinstance(node, ast.BinOp):
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            fn = allowed_ops.get(type(node.op))
            if not fn:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported operator: {type(node.op).__name__}")
            return fn(_eval(node.left), _eval(node.right))
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        if isinstance(node, ast.UnaryOp):
            fn = allowed_ops.get(type(node.op))
            if not fn:
                raise ValueError(f"Unsupported unary: {type(node.op).__name__}")
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            return fn(_eval(node.operand))
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported syntax: {type(node).__name__}")
    try:
        tree = ast.parse(expr, mode="eval")
        # Reject names/calls/attributes entirely
        for n in ast.walk(tree):
            if isinstance(n, (ast.Name, ast.Call, ast.Attribute, ast.Subscript)):
                raise ValueError("Only numeric expressions are allowed")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        result = _eval(tree)
        return f"Result: {result}"
    except Exception as exc:
        return f"Error: {exc}"
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def tool_web_search(query: str = "", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """Deterministic simulated search (no external network)."""
    q = (query or kwargs.get("q") or "").strip()
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    if not q:
        return "Error: empty query"
    return (
        f"[Sandbox web_search] query={q!r}\n"
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        f"Top result: Simulated document about '{q[:80]}'. "
        f"Note: network is disabled in the evaluation sandbox."
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def tool_current_datetime(timezone_name: str = "UTC", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """Return current UTC time (sandbox-safe)."""
    _ = timezone_name or kwargs.get("tz")
    now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    return now.strftime("%Y-%m-%dT%H:%M:%SZ")
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def tool_json_get(data: str = "", path: str = "", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """Get a dotted path from a JSON string, e.g. path='user.name'."""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    raw = data or kwargs.get("json") or "{}"
    path = path or kwargs.get("key") or ""
    try:
        obj = json.loads(raw) if isinstance(raw, str) else raw
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
        return f"Error: invalid JSON ({exc})"
    if not path:
        return json.dumps(obj, ensure_ascii=False)[:MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS]
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    cur: Any = obj
    for part in path.split("."):
        if isinstance(cur, dict) and part in cur:
            cur = cur[part]
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        elif isinstance(cur, list) and part.isdigit() and int(part) < len(cur):
            cur = cur[int(part)]
        else:
            return f"Error: path not found: {path}"
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    if isinstance(cur, (dict, list)):
        return json.dumps(cur, ensure_ascii=False)[:MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS]
    return str(cur)[:MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS]
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
def tool_regex_extract(text: str = "", pattern: str = "", **kwargs: Any) -> str:
    """Extract first regex match (timeout-bound, no catastrophic patterns enforced lightly)."""
    text = text or kwargs.get("input") or ""
    pattern = pattern or kwargs.get("regex") or ""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    if not pattern:
        return "Error: empty pattern"
    if len(pattern) > 200 or len(text) > 50_000:
        return "Error: input too large"
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    try:
        m = re.search(pattern, text, flags=re.MULTILINE)
        if not m:
            return "No match"
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        return m.group(0)[:MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS]
    except re.error as exc:
        return f"Error: invalid regex ({exc})"

# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def get_openai_tool_defs(names: list[str] | None = None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Return OpenAI-format tool definitions for the given names (or all)."""
    selected = names or list(BUILTIN_TOOLS.keys())
    out: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    for name in selected:
        meta = BUILTIN_TOOLS.get(name)
        if not meta:
            # Unknown expected tool: still expose a stub definition for the model
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            out.append({
                "type": "function",
                "function": {
                    "name": name,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                    "description": f"Tool: {name}",
                    "parameters": {"type": "object", "properties": {}, "required": []},
                },
            })
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            continue
        out.append({
            "type": "function",
            "function": {
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                "name": name,
                "description": meta["description"],
                "parameters": {
                    "type": "object",
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                    "properties": meta["parameters"],
                    "required": meta.get("required") or [],
                },
            },
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        })
    return out
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def get_tool_function(name: str) -> Callable[..., str] | None:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    meta = BUILTIN_TOOLS.get(name)
    return meta["fn"] if meta else None
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def run_tool_sandboxed(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    name: str,
    args: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    *,
    timeout_sec: float = DEFAULT_TOOL_TIMEOUT_SEC,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    custom_fn: Callable[..., str] | None = None,
) -> str:
    """Execute a tool with timeout and output truncation.
    Unknown tools without custom_fn return a sandbox denial message
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    (never executes arbitrary code).
    """
    args = args or {}
    fn = custom_fn or get_tool_function(name)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    if fn is None:
        return (
            f"[Sandbox] Tool '{name}' is not registered. "
            f"Available: {', '.join(sorted(BUILTIN_TOOLS))}."
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    def _call() -> str:
        try:
            # Prefer kwargs; fall back to single positional if needed
            return str(fn(**args))
        except TypeError:
            # Map common single-arg tools
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            if len(args) == 1:
                return str(fn(next(iter(args.values()))))
            raise
    try:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        future = _EXECUTOR.submit(_call)
        result = future.result(timeout=timeout_sec)
    except FuturesTimeout:
        return f"[Sandbox] Tool '{name}' timed out after {timeout_sec}s"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.warning("Tool %s failed: %s", name, exc)
        return f"[Sandbox] Tool '{name}' error: {exc}"
    text = str(result)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    if len(text) > MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS:
        return text[:MAX_OUTPUT_CHARS] + "…[truncated]"
    return text
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def resolve_tools_for_suite(expected_tools: list[str] | None) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    """Build tool defs for a test suite: expected tools + always-safe defaults."""
    names = list(expected_tools or [])
    # Always include calculator & web_search as common defaults when empty
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if not names:
        names = ["web_search", "calculator"]
    # Deduplicate preserving order
    seen: set[str] = set()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    ordered: list[str] = []
    for n in names:
        if n and n not in seen:
            seen.add(n)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            ordered.append(n)
    defs = get_openai_tool_defs(ordered)
    # Attach sandbox fn handles for runner map
    for d in defs:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        name = d["function"]["name"]
        d["fn"] = get_tool_function(name)
    return defs

"""Celery async evaluation pipeline.
Architecture:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
  Connects OpenAIReActRunner and LLMJudge into an automated pipeline:
    run_full_evaluation / evaluate_pipeline_task(task_id)
         │
         ├── 1. Load Task + TestSuites from DB
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
         ├── 2. Update status QUEUED → RUNNING
         ├── 3. For each TestSuite (parallel via Celery group):
         │       └── run_single_test_suite
         │            ├── OpenAIReActRunner.run(query, tools)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
         │            └── Persist Trace to DB
         ├── 4. For each Trace (parallel via Celery group):
         │       └── run_judge_evaluation
         │            ├── LLMJudge.evaluate(trace_steps, expected)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
         │            └── Persist MetricScore to DB
         ├── 5. Aggregate results
         └── 6. Update status to "completed" or "failed"
"""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出

def _emit_status(task: Task, status: TaskStatus | str, prev: str | None = None) -> None:
    """Best-effort live event for WebSocket clients."""
    try:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        from app.core.events import publish_task_status
        st = status.value if isinstance(status, TaskStatus) else str(status)
        publish_task_status(
            task.id,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            task.name or task.id,
            st,
            prev_status=prev,
            actor=getattr(task, "created_by", None),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        )
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("publish_task_status skipped: %s", exc)

# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def _run_async(async_factory):
    """Run an async factory (zero-arg → coroutine) from a sync Celery task.
    Safe when called from a worker thread *or* from pytest-asyncio (nested loop):
    if a loop is already running, the coroutine is executed in a fresh thread.
    """
    import asyncio
    import concurrent.futures
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def _runner():
        return asyncio.run(async_factory())
    try:
        asyncio.get_running_loop()
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        nested = True
    except RuntimeError:
        nested = False
    if nested:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        with concurrent.futures.ThreadPoolExecutor(max_workers=1) as pool:
            return pool.submit(_runner).result()
    return _runner()
