# Sub-task 1: Execute a single test suite via OpenAIReActRunner
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
@celery_app.task(
    bind=True,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    max_retries=3,
    default_retry_delay=60,
    soft_time_limit=300,
    time_limit=320,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    acks_late=True,
    autoretry_for=(ConnectionError, TimeoutError, OSError),
    retry_backoff=True,
    retry_backoff_max=600,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def run_single_test_suite(self, test_suite_id: str, agent_config: dict) -> dict:
    """Execute one test case using OpenAIReActRunner and persist the Trace."""
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    logger.info("[Suite %s] Starting execution", test_suite_id)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    async def _execute():
        async with async_session_factory() as session:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            result = await session.execute(
                select(TestSuite).where(TestSuite.id == test_suite_id)
            )
            suite = result.scalar_one_or_none()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            if not suite:
                raise ValueError(f"TestSuite not found: {test_suite_id}")
            from app.core.agent_runner.tool_sandbox import resolve_tools_for_suite
            runner = build_agent_runner(agent_config or {})
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            tools = resolve_tools_for_suite(suite.expected_tools)
            agent_result = await runner.run(
                query=suite.user_query,
                tools=tools,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            )
            status_map = {
                "success": TraceStatus.SUCCESS,
                "max_iterations_reached": TraceStatus.FAILED,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                "failed": TraceStatus.FAILED,
            }
            trace_status = status_map.get(
                agent_result.get("status", ""), TraceStatus.FAILED
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            )
            trace = Trace(
                test_suite_id=test_suite_id,
                user_query=suite.user_query,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                steps=agent_result.get("steps", []),
                total_tokens=agent_result.get("total_tokens", 0),
                response_time_ms=agent_result.get("response_time_ms", 0),
                status=trace_status,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            )
            session.add(trace)
            await session.commit()
            await session.refresh(trace)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            return {
                "trace_id": trace.id,
                "status": trace.status.value,
                "total_tokens": trace.total_tokens,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                "response_time_ms": trace.response_time_ms,
                "step_count": len(trace.steps),
                "iterations": agent_result.get("iterations", 0),
                "final_answer": agent_result.get("final_answer", ""),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                "error_message": agent_result.get("error_message", ""),
            }
    try:
        result = _run_async(_execute)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        logger.info(
            "[Suite %s] Completed: trace=%s status=%s tokens=%d",
            test_suite_id,
            result.get("trace_id"),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            result.get("status"),
            result.get("total_tokens", 0),
        )
        return result
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("[Suite %s] Execution failed: %s", test_suite_id, exc)
        # Retry on transient network errors if retries remain
        return {
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            "trace_id": None,
            "status": "failed",
            "total_tokens": 0,
            "response_time_ms": 0,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            "step_count": 0,
            "iterations": 0,
            "final_answer": "",
            "error_message": str(exc),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        }
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Sub-task 2: Judge a single trace via LLMJudge
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
@celery_app.task(
    bind=True,
    max_retries=3,
    default_retry_delay=60,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    soft_time_limit=300,
    time_limit=320,
    acks_late=True,
    autoretry_for=(ConnectionError, TimeoutError, OSError),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    retry_backoff=True,
    retry_backoff_max=600,
)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def run_judge_evaluation(
    self, trace_id: str, expected_output: str, expected_tools: list
) -> dict:
    """Score one Trace using LLMJudge and persist the MetricScores."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    logger.info("[Judge %s] Starting evaluation", trace_id)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    async def _judge():
        async with async_session_factory() as session:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            result = await session.execute(select(Trace).where(Trace.id == trace_id))
            trace = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            if not trace:
                raise ValueError(f"Trace not found: {trace_id}")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            judge = build_llm_judge()
            judge_result = await judge.evaluate(
                trace_steps=trace.steps,
                expected_output=expected_output,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                expected_tools=expected_tools or [],
            )
            # Support both dict and object-style results
            if isinstance(judge_result, dict):
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                scores = judge_result.get("scores", {})
                total = float(judge_result.get("total", 0.0))
                reason = judge_result.get("reason", "")
                token_cost = int(judge_result.get("token_cost", 0) or 0)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            else:
                scores = getattr(judge_result, "scores", {}) or {}
                total = float(getattr(judge_result, "total", 0.0) or 0.0)
                reason = getattr(judge_result, "reason", "") or ""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                token_cost = int(getattr(judge_result, "token_cost", 0) or 0)
            for metric_name, score in scores.items():
                ms = MetricScore(
                    trace_id=trace_id,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    metric_name=metric_name,
                    score=float(score),
                    reason=reason,
                    extra_metadata={"token_cost": token_cost},
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                )
                session.add(ms)
            await session.commit()
            return {
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "scores": scores,
                "total": total,
                "reason": reason,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                "token_cost": token_cost,
            }
    try:
        result = _run_async(_judge)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        logger.info(
            "[Judge %s] Completed: total=%.1f token_cost=%d",
            trace_id,
            result.get("total", 0),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            result.get("token_cost", 0),
        )
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                scores = judge_result.get("scores", {})
                total = float(judge_result.get("total", 0.0))
                reason = judge_result.get("reason", "")
                token_cost = int(judge_result.get("token_cost", 0) or 0)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            else:
                scores = getattr(judge_result, "scores", {}) or {}
                total = float(getattr(judge_result, "total", 0.0) or 0.0)
                reason = getattr(judge_result, "reason", "") or ""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                token_cost = int(getattr(judge_result, "token_cost", 0) or 0)
            for metric_name, score in scores.items():
                ms = MetricScore(
                    trace_id=trace_id,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    metric_name=metric_name,
                    score=float(score),
                    reason=reason,
                    extra_metadata={"token_cost": token_cost},
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                )
                session.add(ms)
            await session.commit()
            return {
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                "trace_id": trace_id,
                "scores": scores,
                "total": total,
                "reason": reason,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                "token_cost": token_cost,
            }
    try:
        result = _run_async(_judge)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        logger.info(
            "[Judge %s] Completed: total=%.1f token_cost=%d",
            trace_id,
            result.get("total", 0),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            result.get("token_cost", 0),
        )
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        logger.exception("[Judge %s] Evaluation failed: %s", trace_id, exc)
        return {
            "trace_id": trace_id,
            "scores": {},
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            "total": 0.0,
            "reason": f"Evaluation failed: {exc}",
            "token_cost": 0,
            "error_message": str(exc),
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        }
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Orchestrator: Full evaluation pipeline
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
@celery_app.task(bind=True)
def run_full_evaluation(self, task_id: str) -> dict:
    """Orchestrate the full evaluation pipeline for a task.
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    Pipeline:
      1. Load task + test suites from DB, set status to running
      2. Execute all suites in parallel (Celery group)
      3. Judge all traces in parallel
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
      4. Aggregate results
      5. Persist final status
    """
    logger.info("[Task %s] Full evaluation started", task_id)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    async def _orchestrate():
        async with async_session_factory() as session:
            result = await session.execute(select(Task).where(Task.id == task_id))
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            task = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            if not task:
                raise ValueError(f"Task not found: {task_id}")
            # Status machine: allow QUEUED or CREATED → RUNNING
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            prev = task.status.value
            if task.status in (TaskStatus.CREATED, TaskStatus.QUEUED):
                task.status = TaskStatus.RUNNING
            else:
                # Already running/judging — continue idempotently
                if task.status not in (
                    TaskStatus.RUNNING,
                    TaskStatus.JUDGING,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                ):
                    task.status = TaskStatus.RUNNING
            await session.commit()
            if prev != task.status.value:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                _emit_status(task, task.status, prev)
            suite_result = await session.execute(
                select(TestSuite).where(TestSuite.task_id == task_id)
            )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            suites = list(suite_result.scalars().all())
            if not suites:
                prev = task.status.value
                task.status = TaskStatus.COMPLETED
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                await session.commit()
                _emit_status(task, task.status, prev)
                return {
                    "task_id": task_id,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                    "status": "completed",
                    "total_suites": 0,
                    "completed_suites": 0,
                    "failed_suites": 0,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    "average_score": 0.0,
                    "message": "No test suites found.",
                }
            total = len(suites)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            try:
                self.update_state(
                    state="RUNNING",
                    meta={
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                        "task_id": task_id,
                        "phase": "executing",
                        "total_suites": total,
                        "completed": 0,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
                # Eager mode / tests may not support update_state fully
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                pass
            # Execute suites (group with eager mode runs inline)
            suite_jobs = [
                run_single_test_suite.s(suite.id, task.agent_config or {})
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                for suite in suites
            ]
            suite_group = group(suite_jobs)
            suite_async = suite_group.apply_async()
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            suite_results = suite_async.get(disable_sync_subtasks=False)
            # Normalize results list
            if not isinstance(suite_results, (list, tuple)):
                suite_results = [suite_results]
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            successful_suites = [
                r
                for r in suite_results
                if r and r.get("trace_id") and r.get("status") != "failed"
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            ]
            failed_suites = [
                r
                for r in suite_results
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                if not r or not r.get("trace_id") or r.get("status") == "failed"
            ]
            try:
                self.update_state(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    state="RUNNING",
                    meta={
                        "task_id": task_id,
                        "phase": "judging",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                        "total_suites": total,
                        "completed_suites": len(successful_suites),
                        "failed_suites": len(failed_suites),
                    },
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                )
            except Exception:
                pass
            prev = task.status.value
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            task.status = TaskStatus.JUDGING
            await session.commit()
            _emit_status(task, task.status, prev)
            judge_jobs = []
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            for suite, s_result in zip(suites, suite_results):
                if not s_result:
                    continue
                tid = s_result.get("trace_id")
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                if tid:
                    judge_jobs.append(
                        run_judge_evaluation.s(
                            tid,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                            suite.expected_output or "",
                            suite.expected_tools or [],
                        )
                    )
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            if judge_jobs:
                judge_async = group(judge_jobs).apply_async()
                judge_results = judge_async.get(disable_sync_subtasks=False)
                if not isinstance(judge_results, (list, tuple)):
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    judge_results = [judge_results]
            else:
                judge_results = []
            total_score = 0.0
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            score_count = 0
            dimension_scores: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
            total_tokens = 0
            total_time_ms = 0
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            for jr in judge_results:
                if not jr:
                    continue
                total_score += float(jr.get("total", 0.0) or 0.0)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                score_count += 1
                total_tokens += int(jr.get("token_cost", 0) or 0)
                for dim, val in (jr.get("scores") or {}).items():
                    dimension_scores.setdefault(dim, []).append(float(val))
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            for s_result in suite_results:
                if not s_result:
                    continue
                total_tokens += int(s_result.get("total_tokens", 0) or 0)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                total_time_ms += int(s_result.get("response_time_ms", 0) or 0)
            avg_score = round(total_score / score_count, 1) if score_count else 0.0
            avg_dim_scores = (
                {
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                    dim: round(sum(vals) / len(vals), 1)
                    for dim, vals in dimension_scores.items()
                }
                if dimension_scores
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                else {}
            )
            overall_status = "completed"
            if failed_suites and not successful_suites:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                overall_status = "failed"
            elif failed_suites:
                overall_status = "partial"
            prev = task.status.value
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            task.status = (
                TaskStatus.FAILED
                if overall_status == "failed"
                else TaskStatus.COMPLETED
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            )
            await session.commit()
            _emit_status(task, task.status, prev)
            try:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                self.update_state(
                    state=overall_status.upper(),
                    meta={
                        "task_id": task_id,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
                        "phase": "done",
                        "total_suites": total,
                        "completed_suites": len(successful_suites),
                        "failed_suites": len(failed_suites),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                        "average_score": avg_score,
                    },
                )
            except Exception:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                pass
            return {
                "task_id": task_id,
                "status": overall_status,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                "total_suites": total,
                "completed_suites": len(successful_suites),
                "failed_suites": len(failed_suites),
                "average_score": avg_score,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                "dimension_scores": avg_dim_scores,
                "total_tokens": total_tokens,
                "total_time_ms": total_time_ms,
                "suites": list(suite_results),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                "judgments": list(judge_results),
            }
    try:
        result = _run_async(_orchestrate)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        logger.info(
            "[Task %s] Completed: status=%s suites=%d/%d score=%.1f",
            task_id,
            result.get("status"),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            result.get("completed_suites", 0),
            result.get("total_suites", 0),
            result.get("average_score", 0.0),
        )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        return result
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.exception("[Task %s] Orchestration failed: %s", task_id, exc)
        _run_async(lambda: _mark_task_failed(task_id, str(exc)))
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        return {
            "task_id": task_id,
            "status": "failed",
            "error_message": str(exc),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            "total_suites": 0,
            "completed_suites": 0,
            "failed_suites": 0,
            "average_score": 0.0,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        }
# Public alias used by docs / external callers
evaluate_pipeline_task = run_full_evaluation
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
async def _mark_task_failed(task_id: str, error: str) -> None:
    """Mark a task as failed in the database."""
    try:
        async with async_session_factory() as session:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            result = await session.execute(select(Task).where(Task.id == task_id))
            task = result.scalar_one_or_none()
            if task:
                prev = task.status.value
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                task.status = TaskStatus.FAILED
                await session.commit()
                _emit_status(task, task.status, prev)
                logger.info("[Task %s] marked FAILED: %s", task_id, error)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.error("Failed to mark task %s as failed: %s", task_id, exc)
# === 模块：backend/app/core/security.py ===
# [功能] API 密钥鉴权与 secret:actor 主体映射
# [安全] 常量时间比较，避免时序旁路
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""API Key authentication helpers."""
from __future__ import annotations
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
import hmac
import secrets
from dataclasses import dataclass
from fastapi import Header, Request
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
from fastapi.security.utils import get_authorization_scheme_param
from app.config import settings
from app.utils.exceptions import AgentFlowError
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
class UnauthorizedError(AgentFlowError):
    """Raised when authentication fails."""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    def __init__(self, message: str = "Unauthorized") -> None:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        super().__init__(message=message, status_code=401, detail=None)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class AuthIdentity:
    """Authenticated principal derived from API key."""
    key_id: str
    actor: str
    raw_key_prefix: str
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def parse_api_keys(raw: str | list[str] | None) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Parse API keys config into {secret: actor_name}.
    Supported formats:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    - "secret1,secret2"  -> actors key_1, key_2
    - "secret1:alice,secret2:bob"
    - JSON list via pydantic already as list[str]
    """
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if not raw:
        return {}
    items: list[str]
    if isinstance(raw, list):
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        items = [str(x).strip() for x in raw if str(x).strip()]
    else:
        items = [p.strip() for p in str(raw).split(",") if p.strip()]
    mapping: dict[str, str] = {}
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    for idx, item in enumerate(items, start=1):
        if ":" in item:
            secret, actor = item.split(":", 1)
            secret, actor = secret.strip(), actor.strip() or f"key_{idx}"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        else:
            secret, actor = item, f"key_{idx}"
        if secret:
            mapping[secret] = actor
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    return mapping
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def extract_api_key(request: Request, authorization: str | None = None, x_api_key: [REDACTED_API_KEY] | Non...
    """Extract API key from X-API-Key or Authorization: Bearer."""
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    key = (x_api_key or request.headers.get("X-API-Key") or "").strip()
    if key:
        return key
    header = authorization or request.headers.get("Authorization")
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if header:
        scheme, param = get_authorization_scheme_param(header)
        if scheme.lower() == "bearer" and param:
            return param.strip()
        # Also accept raw "ApiKey <token>"
        if scheme.lower() in {"apikey", "api-key"} and param:
            return param.strip()
    return None
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def authenticate_api_key(api_key: [REDACTED_API_KEY] | None) -> AuthIdentity | None:
    """Validate API key against configured secrets (constant-time compare)."""
    if not api_key:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        return None
    keys = parse_api_keys(settings.API_KEYS)
    for secret, actor in keys.items():
        if hmac.compare_digest(api_key, secret):
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            return AuthIdentity(
                key_id=secrets.token_hex(4),
                actor=actor,
                raw_key_prefix=api_key[:4] + "***",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            )
    return None
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
def require_auth_if_enabled(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    request: Request,
    authorization: str | None = Header(default=None),
    x_api_key: [REDACTED_API_KEY] | None = Header(default=None, alias="X-API-Key"),
) -> AuthIdentity | None:
    """FastAPI dependency: enforce API key when AUTH_ENABLED is true."""
    if not settings.AUTH_ENABLED:
        # Anonymous dev identity
        return AuthIdentity(key_id="dev", actor="anonymous", raw_key_prefix="dev")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    api_key = [REDACTED_API_KEY], authorization, x_api_key)
    identity = authenticate_api_key(api_key)
    if identity is None:
        raise UnauthorizedError("Invalid or missing API key. Provide X-API-Key or Authorization: Bearer <ke...
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    request.state.actor = identity.actor
    request.state.auth = identity
    return identity
# Paths that skip auth even when enabled
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
AUTH_PUBLIC_PATHS = frozenset({
    "/health",
    "/docs",
    "/redoc",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    "/openapi.json",
})
# === 模块：backend/app/core/tenancy.py ===
# [功能] 多租户过滤：按主体隔离任务与轨迹
# [创新] 依赖注入层统一注入租户上下文
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Lightweight multi-tenant helpers keyed by API-key actor."""
from __future__ import annotations
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from sqlalchemy import Select, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload
from app.config import settings
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
from app.models.task import Task
from app.models.test_suite import TestSuite
from app.models.trace import Trace
from app.utils.exceptions import NotFoundError
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
def tenancy_enforced() -> bool:
    """Isolation is on when auth is enabled, or TENANCY_ENABLED is explicit."""
    return bool(settings.AUTH_ENABLED or settings.TENANCY_ENABLED)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def admin_actors() -> set[str]:
    raw = settings.ADMIN_ACTORS or ""
    return {p.strip() for p in raw.split(",") if p.strip()}
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def is_admin(actor: str | None) -> bool:
    if not actor:
        return False
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    return actor in admin_actors()
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def can_access_task(task: Task, actor: str | None) -> bool:
    """Return True if actor may read/mutate the task."""
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if not tenancy_enforced():
        return True
    if is_admin(actor):
        return True
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    owner = getattr(task, "created_by", None) or "anonymous"
    return owner == (actor or "anonymous")
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def ensure_task_access(task: Task | None, actor: str | None, task_id: str = "") -> Task:
    """Raise NotFoundError if missing or not accessible (no existence leak)."""
    if task is None or not can_access_task(task, actor):
        raise NotFoundError("Task", task_id or (task.id if task else ""))
    return task
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
def apply_owner_filter(query: Select, actor: str | None) -> Select:
    """Restrict Task list queries to the current actor when tenancy is on."""
    if not tenancy_enforced():
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        return query
    if is_admin(actor):
        return query
    owner = actor or "anonymous"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    return query.where(Task.created_by == owner)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
def apply_trace_owner_filter(query: Select, actor: str | None) -> Select:
    """Restrict Trace queries to suites belonging to actor-owned tasks."""
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    if not tenancy_enforced():
        return query
    if is_admin(actor):
        return query
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    owner = actor or "anonymous"
    return (
        query.join(TestSuite, Trace.test_suite_id == TestSuite.id)
        .join(Task, TestSuite.task_id == Task.id)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        .where(Task.created_by == owner)
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
async def load_task_for_suite(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    session: AsyncSession,
    suite_id: str,
    actor: str | None,
) -> Task:
    """Load parent Task for a test suite; enforce ownership."""
    result = await session.execute(
        select(Task)
        .join(TestSuite, TestSuite.task_id == Task.id)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        .where(TestSuite.id == suite_id)
    )
    task = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    if task is None or not can_access_task(task, actor):
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        raise NotFoundError("测试用例", suite_id)
    return task
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def load_trace_with_access(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    session: AsyncSession,
    trace_id: str,
    actor: str | None,
    *,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    with_scores: bool = True,
) -> Trace:
    """Load a Trace and ensure the caller owns the parent Task."""
    opts = [selectinload(Trace.metric_scores)] if with_scores else []
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    result = await session.execute(
        select(Trace).options(*opts).where(Trace.id == trace_id)
    )
    trace = result.scalar_one_or_none()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    if trace is None:
        raise NotFoundError("执行轨迹", trace_id)
    # Resolve parent task via suite
    suite_result = await session.execute(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        select(TestSuite).where(TestSuite.id == trace.test_suite_id)
    )
    suite = suite_result.scalar_one_or_none()
    if suite is None:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        raise NotFoundError("执行轨迹", trace_id)
    task_result = await session.execute(select(Task).where(Task.id == suite.task_id))
    task = task_result.scalar_one_or_none()
    if task is None or not can_access_task(task, actor):
        # Hide existence of foreign traces
        raise NotFoundError("执行轨迹", trace_id)
    return trace
# === 模块：backend/app/core/middleware.py ===
# [功能] 请求 ID、限流与安全响应头中间件
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Request tracing and optional API-key auth middleware."""
import logging
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
import uuid
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from starlette.middleware.base import BaseHTTPMiddleware, RequestResponseEndpoint
from starlette.requests import Request
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from starlette.responses import JSONResponse, Response
from app.config import settings
from app.core.security import AUTH_PUBLIC_PATHS, authenticate_api_key, extract_api_key
from app.utils.exceptions import error_response
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class RequestIDMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    """
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    Middleware that:
      1. Reads X-Request-ID from request headers or generates a UUID.
      2. Injects request_id into the request state for logging.
      3. Adds X-Request-ID to the response headers.
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
      4. Logs each request with method, path, status, and duration.
    """
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: RequestResponseEndpoint) -> Response:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        request_id = request.headers.get("X-Request-ID") or str(uuid.uuid4())
        request.state.request_id = request_id
        start_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        response = await call_next(request)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        response.headers["X-Request-ID"] = request_id
        duration_ms = int((datetime.now(timezone.utc) - start_time).total_seconds() * 1000)
        logger.info(
            "%s %s -> %s [%dms] request_id=%s",
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            request.method,
            request.url.path,
            response.status_code,
            duration_ms,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            request_id,
        )
        return response
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
class APIKeyAuthMiddleware(BaseHTTPMiddleware):
    """Enforce API key auth for /api/v1 when AUTH_ENABLED=true."""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    async def dispatch(self, request: Request, call_next: RequestResponseEndpoint) -> Response:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        path = request.url.path
        if not settings.AUTH_ENABLED:
            request.state.actor = "anonymous"
            return await call_next(request)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        if path in AUTH_PUBLIC_PATHS or not path.startswith("/api/"):
            request.state.actor = "public"
            return await call_next(request)
        # CORS preflight
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        if request.method == "OPTIONS":
            return await call_next(request)
        api_key = [REDACTED_API_KEY])
        identity = authenticate_api_key(api_key)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        if identity is None:
            rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)
            return JSONResponse(
                status_code=401,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                content=error_response(
                    401,
                    "Unauthorized",
                    "Provide a valid X-API-Key or Authorization: Bearer <key>",
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    request_id=rid,
                ),
            )
        request.state.actor = identity.actor
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        request.state.auth = identity
        return await call_next(request)

# [功能] 数据库会话、鉴权主体与租户依赖装配
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Dependency injection with database pool and Redis cache."""
from __future__ import annotations
import json
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from collections.abc import AsyncGenerator
from typing import Any
import redis.asyncio as aioredis
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import (
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    AsyncSession,
    async_sessionmaker,
    create_async_engine,
)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from app.config import settings
# ---- Database engine with connection pool ----
_engine_kw: dict = {
    "echo": settings.DB_ECHO,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    "future": True,
}
if "postgresql" in settings.DATABASE_URL:
    _engine_kw.update({
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        "pool_size": settings.DB_POOL_SIZE,
        "max_overflow": settings.DB_MAX_OVERFLOW,
        "pool_pre_ping": True,
        "pool_recycle": 3600,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    })
engine = create_async_engine(settings.DATABASE_URL, **_engine_kw)
async_session_factory = async_sessionmaker(
    engine,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    class_=AsyncSession,
    expire_on_commit=False,
)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
async def get_db() -> AsyncGenerator[AsyncSession, Any]:
    """Provide an async database session with auto-commit/rollback."""
    async with async_session_factory() as session:
        try:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            yield session
            await session.commit()
        except Exception:
            await session.rollback()
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            raise
        finally:
            await session.close()
# ---- Redis cache ----
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
_redis: aioredis.Redis | None = None
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def get_redis() -> aioredis.Redis:
    """Lazy-init Redis connection pool."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    global _redis
    if _redis is None:
        _redis = aioredis.from_url(
            settings.REDIS_URL,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            encoding="utf-8",
            decode_responses=True,
            # Fail fast when Redis is down (local eager mode / offline)
            socket_timeout=0.8,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            socket_connect_timeout=0.5,
        )
    return _redis
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def close_redis() -> None:
    """Close the Redis connection pool gracefully."""
    global _redis
    if _redis:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        await _redis.close()
        _redis = None
# ---- Cache helpers ----
CACHE_TTL_DEFAULT = 300  # 5 minutes
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
CACHE_TTL_LIST = 60       # 1 minute for lists
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
async def cache_get(key: str) -> Any | None:
    """Get value from Redis cache."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    try:
        r = await get_redis()
        val = await r.get(key)
        return json.loads(val) if val else None
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    except Exception:
        return None
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
async def cache_set(key: str, value: Any, ttl: int = CACHE_TTL_DEFAULT) -> None:
    """Set value in Redis cache with TTL."""
    try:
        r = await get_redis()
        await r.setex(key, ttl, json.dumps(value, default=str))
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    except Exception:
        pass
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
async def cache_invalidate(pattern: str) -> None:
    """Invalidate all keys matching a pattern."""
    try:
        r = await get_redis()
        keys = await r.keys(pattern)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        if keys:
            await r.delete(*keys)
    except Exception:
        pass
# === 模块：backend/app/core/events.py ===
# [功能] 领域事件总线：状态变更与评分完成发布订阅
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Task activity events — Redis pub/sub + local callback bridge for WebSocket.
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
Celery workers publish here; the API process subscribes and fans out to WS clients.
When Redis is unavailable, in-process listeners still receive local publishes.
"""
from __future__ import annotations
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
import json
import logging
from datetime import datetime, timezone
from typing import Any, Awaitable, Callable, Optional
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
CHANNEL = "agentflow:task_events"
# Local async listeners (API process only)
_local_listeners: list[Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[None]]] = []
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def register_local_listener(cb: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[None]]) -> None:
    if cb not in _local_listeners:
        _local_listeners.append(cb)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def unregister_local_listener(cb: Callable[[dict[str, Any]], Awaitable[None]]) -> None:
    if cb in _local_listeners:
        _local_listeners.remove(cb)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def build_task_event(
    *,
    task_id: str,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    task_name: str,
    status: str,
    prev_status: str | None = None,
    actor: str | None = None,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    extra: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    payload: dict[str, Any] = {
        "type": "task_status",
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        "task_id": task_id,
        "task_name": task_name,
        "status": status,
        "prev_status": prev_status,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        "actor": actor or "anonymous",
        "at": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
    }
    if extra:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        payload["extra"] = extra
    return payload
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def publish_task_event(event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Sync-safe publish for Celery workers and FastAPI endpoints.
    1) Publish to Redis (best-effort)
    2) Schedule local async listeners if a loop is running
    """
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    body = json.dumps(event, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
    # Redis fan-out (works across processes)
    try:
        import redis as sync_redis
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        from app.config import settings
        client = sync_redis.from_url(settings.REDIS_URL, socket_connect_timeout=0.5)
        try:
            client.publish(CHANNEL, body)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        finally:
            client.close()
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("Redis publish skipped: %s", exc)
    # In-process async listeners (API only)
    try:
        import asyncio
        loop = asyncio.get_running_loop()
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    except RuntimeError:
        return
    for cb in list(_local_listeners):
        try:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            loop.create_task(cb(event))
        except Exception as exc:
            logger.debug("Local listener schedule failed: %s", exc)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def publish_task_status(
    task_id: str,
    task_name: str,
    status: str,
    *,
    prev_status: str | None = None,
    actor: str | None = None,
) -> None:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    publish_task_event(
        build_task_event(
            task_id=task_id,
            task_name=task_name,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            status=status if isinstance(status, str) else str(status),
            prev_status=prev_status,
            actor=actor,
        )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    )
# === 模块：backend/app/core/ws_hub.py ===
# [功能] 按任务维度管理实时连接并广播进度
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""WebSocket connection hub + Redis subscriber for live task events."""
from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
import json
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
import logging
from typing import Any
from fastapi import WebSocket
from app.core.events import CHANNEL, register_local_listener, unregister_local_listener
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
class ConnectionManager:
    """In-memory WebSocket fan-out."""
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self._clients: set[WebSocket] = set()
        self._lock = asyncio.Lock()
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    async def connect(self, ws: WebSocket) -> None:
        await ws.accept()
        async with self._lock:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            self._clients.add(ws)
        logger.info("WS client connected (%d total)", len(self._clients))
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    async def disconnect(self, ws: WebSocket) -> None:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        async with self._lock:
            self._clients.discard(ws)
        logger.info("WS client disconnected (%d total)", len(self._clients))
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    async def broadcast(self, message: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        if not self._clients:
            return
        data = json.dumps(message, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        dead: list[WebSocket] = []
        async with self._lock:
            clients = list(self._clients)
        for ws in clients:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            try:
                await ws.send_text(data)
            except Exception:
                dead.append(ws)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        for ws in dead:
            await self.disconnect(ws)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    @property
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def count(self) -> int:
        return len(self._clients)
manager = ConnectionManager()
_subscriber_task: asyncio.Task | None = None
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
async def _on_local_event(event: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    await manager.broadcast(event)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
async def _redis_subscriber_loop() -> None:
    """Subscribe to Redis channel and rebroadcast to local WS clients."""
    while True:
        try:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            from app.core.dependencies import get_redis
            r = await get_redis()
            pubsub = r.pubsub()
            await pubsub.subscribe(CHANNEL)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            logger.info("Redis subscriber listening on %s", CHANNEL)
            async for msg in pubsub.listen():
                if msg is None:
                    continue
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                if msg.get("type") != "message":
                    continue
                raw = msg.get("data")
                if raw is None:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                    continue
                if isinstance(raw, bytes):
                    raw = raw.decode("utf-8", errors="replace")
                try:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                    event = json.loads(raw)
                except json.JSONDecodeError:
                    continue
                await manager.broadcast(event)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            raise
        except Exception as exc:
            # Back off harder so local/dev without Redis stays quiet
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            logger.warning("Redis subscriber error, retry in 30s: %s", exc)
            await asyncio.sleep(30)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def start_ws_hub() -> None:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    global _subscriber_task
    register_local_listener(_on_local_event)
    from app.config import settings
    # Eager / no-worker local mode: in-process listeners only (no Redis retry spam)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    if settings.CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER:
        logger.info("WS hub started (local events only, Redis subscriber skipped)")
        return
    if _subscriber_task is None or _subscriber_task.done():
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        _subscriber_task = asyncio.create_task(_redis_subscriber_loop())
        logger.info("WS hub started (Redis pub/sub enabled)")
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
async def stop_ws_hub() -> None:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    global _subscriber_task
    unregister_local_listener(_on_local_event)
    if _subscriber_task and not _subscriber_task.done():
        _subscriber_task.cancel()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        try:
            await _subscriber_task
        except asyncio.CancelledError:
            pass
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    _subscriber_task = None
    logger.info("WS hub stopped")
# === 模块：backend/app/core/audit.py ===
# [功能] 关键操作审计：动作、对象、主体、客户端
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Lightweight audit trail for mutating operations."""
from __future__ import annotations
import logging
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
from typing import Any
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from app.models.audit_log import AuditLog
logger = logging.getLogger("app.audit")
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
async def write_audit(
    session: AsyncSession,
    *,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    action: str,
    resource_type: str,
    resource_id: str | None = None,
    actor: str = "anonymous",
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    detail: dict[str, Any] | None = None,
    request_id: str | None = None,
    ip: str | None = None,
) -> AuditLog:
    """Persist an audit log row and emit structured log line."""
    entry = AuditLog(
        actor=actor or "anonymous",
        action=action,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        resource_type=resource_type,
        resource_id=resource_id,
        detail=detail or {},
        request_id=request_id,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        ip_address=ip,
    )
    session.add(entry)
    # Caller is responsible for commit (endpoint / get_db)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    logger.info(
        "audit action=%s resource=%s/%s actor=%s request_id=%s",
        action,
        resource_type,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        resource_id,
        actor,
        request_id,
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    return entry

async def seed_database(async_session_factory, *, force: bool = False) -> None:
    """Insert seed data into the database.
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    Args:
        async_session_factory: SQLAlchemy async session factory.
        force: If True, insert demo task even when other tasks already exist
            (skips only when a demo-named task is present).
    """
    from sqlalchemy import select, func
    from app.models.task import Task, TaskStatus
    from app.models.test_suite import TestSuite
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    async with async_session_factory() as session:
        if not force:
            result = await session.execute(select(func.count(Task.id)))
            count = result.scalar() or 0
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            if count > 0:
                return
        else:
            existing = await session.execute(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                select(Task).where(Task.name == SAMPLE_TASK["name"]).limit(1)
            )
            if existing.scalar_one_or_none() is not None:
                return
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        task = Task(
            id=str(uuid4()),
            name=SAMPLE_TASK["name"],
            description=SAMPLE_TASK["description"],
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            agent_config=SAMPLE_TASK["agent_config"],
            status=TaskStatus.CREATED,
            created_by="demo",
        )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        session.add(task)
        await session.flush()
        for suite_data in SAMPLE_TEST_SUITES:
            suite = TestSuite(
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                task_id=task.id,
                user_query=suite_data["user_query"],
                expected_output=suite_data["expected_output"],
                expected_tools=suite_data["expected_tools"],
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            )
            session.add(suite)
        await session.commit()
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
def main() -> None:
    import argparse
    import asyncio
    import sys
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Seed AgentFlow-Eval demo data")
    parser.add_argument(
        "--force",
        action="store_true",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        help="Insert demo task even if other tasks already exist",
    )
    args = parser.parse_args()
    try:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        from app.core.dependencies import async_session_factory
    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover
        sys.exit(1)
    asyncio.run(seed_database(async_session_factory, force=args.force))
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

"""Audit log model for security-relevant actions."""

# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column
from app.models.base import Base, PKMixin, TimestampMixin
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
class AuditLog(PKMixin, TimestampMixin, Base):
    """Immutable-style audit record (no update API)."""
    __tablename__ = "audit_logs"
    actor: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=False, default="anonymous", comment="操作者标识",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    )
    action: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=False, comment="动作，如 task.create / task.execute",
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    resource_type: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=False, comment="资源类型",
    )
    resource_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        String(64), nullable=True, default=None, comment="资源 ID",
    )
    detail: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(
        JSON, nullable=True, default=None, comment="附加详情",
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    )
    request_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, default=None, comment="请求追踪 ID",
    )
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    ip_address: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(64), nullable=True, default=None, comment="客户端 IP",
    )
    note: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        Text, nullable=False, default="", comment="备注",
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def __repr__(self) -> str:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        return f"<AuditLog {self.action} {self.resource_type}/{self.resource_id}>"

# [功能] backend/app/models/base.py — 评测工作台业务模块

"""基础 ORM 模型类，提供 UUID 主键与时间戳字段的公共基类。"""
from datetime import datetime
from uuid import uuid4
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from sqlalchemy import DateTime, String, func
from sqlalchemy.orm import DeclarativeBase, Mapped, mapped_column
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class Base(DeclarativeBase):
    """所有 ORM 模型的抽象基类。"""
    pass
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
class TimestampMixin:
    """混入类，自动添加 created_at / updated_at 时间戳字段。
    所有业务模型通过多重继承获得时间戳能力。
    """
    created_at: Mapped[datetime] = mapped_column(
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        server_default=func.now(),
        comment="记录创建时间",
    )
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    updated_at: Mapped[datetime | None] = mapped_column(
        DateTime(timezone=True),
        onupdate=func.now(),
        nullable=True,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        comment="记录最后更新时间",
    )
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
class PKMixin:
    """混入类，提供 UUID 字符串主键。
    兼容 SQLite（开发）和 PostgreSQL（生产）两种后端。
    """
    id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        String(36),
        primary_key=True,
        default=lambda: str(uuid4()),
        comment="UUID 主键",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    )
# === 模块：backend/app/models/metric_score.py ===
# [功能] 指标分模型：自动分与人工复核有效分优先
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""MetricScore model —— 单项评分指标记录。"""
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, Float, ForeignKey, JSON, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from app.models.base import Base, PKMixin, TimestampMixin
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.models.trace import Trace
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class MetricScore(PKMixin, TimestampMixin, Base):
    """评测指标得分。
    记录每个 Trace 在某个指标维度（如准确率、工具调用正确性等）的评分，
    支持 LLM 自动评分与人工审核覆盖。
    """
    __tablename__ = "metric_scores"
    trace_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        ForeignKey("traces.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        nullable=False,
        comment="FK to trace",
    )
    metric_name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        String(100), nullable=False, comment="metric name",
    )
    score: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(
        Float, nullable=False, comment="score 0-100",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    )
    reason: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=False, default="", comment="deduction reason",
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    extra_metadata: Mapped[dict | None] = mapped_column(
        JSON, nullable=True, default=None, comment="extra metadata",
    )
    # ---- v1.0 新增：LLM 评分置信度 ----
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    confidence: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(
        Float, nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="LLM 评分置信度 0.0 ~ 1.0",
    )
    # ---- v1.0 新增：人工审核覆盖 ----
    is_human_reviewed: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False,
        comment="是否经过人工审核",
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    )
    human_score: Mapped[float | None] = mapped_column(
        Float, nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="人工审核给出的分数（覆盖 LLM 评分）",
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    )
    reviewer: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="人工审核人员标识",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    )
    # ---- 关系 ----
    trace: Mapped["Trace"] = relationship(back_populates="metric_scores")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    @property
    def effective_score(self) -> float:
        """返回最终有效分数：优先使用人工审核分数。"""
        if self.is_human_reviewed and self.human_score is not None:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            return self.human_score
        return self.score
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def __repr__(self) -> str:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        return f"<MetricScore name={self.metric_name!r} score={self.score} reviewed={self.is_human_reviewed}>"
# === 模块：backend/app/models/task.py ===
# [功能] 评测任务领域模型：状态、配置、归档、创建者
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Task 任务模型 —— 表示一次评测任务的顶层聚合根。"""
import enum
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
from sqlalchemy import Boolean, JSON, Enum, String, Text
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.models.base import Base, PKMixin, TimestampMixin
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.models.test_suite import TestSuite
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class TaskStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    """任务状态枚举 —— 生产级状态机。
    状态流转:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        CREATED ─► QUEUED ─► RUNNING ─► JUDGING ─► COMPLETED
                                │          │
                                ▼          ▼
                             FAILED     FAILED
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                                │
                                ▼
                           CANCELLED / TIMEOUT
    """
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    CREATED = "created"
    QUEUED = "queued"
    RUNNING = "running"
    WAITING_TOOL = "waiting_tool"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    JUDGING = "judging"
    COMPLETED = "completed"
    FAILED = "failed"
    CANCELLED = "cancelled"
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    TIMEOUT = "timeout"
    # ---- 合法流转矩阵 ----
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    @classmethod
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def allowed_transitions(cls) -> dict["TaskStatus", set["TaskStatus"]]:
        """返回每个状态允许流转的目标状态集合。"""
        return {
            cls.CREATED: {cls.QUEUED, cls.CANCELLED},
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            cls.QUEUED: {cls.RUNNING, cls.CANCELLED, cls.TIMEOUT},
            cls.RUNNING: {cls.WAITING_TOOL, cls.JUDGING, cls.FAILED, cls.CANCELLED, cls.TIMEOUT},
            cls.WAITING_TOOL: {cls.RUNNING, cls.FAILED, cls.CANCELLED, cls.TIMEOUT},
            cls.JUDGING: {cls.COMPLETED, cls.FAILED, cls.CANCELLED},
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            cls.COMPLETED: set(),
            cls.FAILED: set(),
            cls.CANCELLED: set(),
            cls.TIMEOUT: set(),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        }
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    def can_transition_to(self, target: "TaskStatus") -> bool:
        """检查是否允许从当前状态流转到目标状态。"""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        return target in self.allowed_transitions().get(self, set())
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
class Task(PKMixin, TimestampMixin, Base):
    """评测任务。
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    一个 Task 包含多个 TestSuite 测试用例，执行时逐条运行并汇总评分。
    """
    __tablename__ = "tasks"
    name: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        String(255), nullable=False, comment="任务名称",
    )
    description: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=False, default="", comment="任务描述",
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    )
    status: Mapped[TaskStatus] = mapped_column(
        Enum(
            TaskStatus,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            name="task_status",
            native_enum=False,
            length=20,
            values_callable=lambda enum_cls: [e.value for e in enum_cls],
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        ),
        nullable=False,
        default=TaskStatus.CREATED,
        comment="任务状态",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    )
    agent_config: Mapped[dict] = mapped_column(
        JSON, nullable=False, default=dict, comment="Agent 配置参数（JSON 对象）",
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    celery_task_id: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(255), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="Celery 异步任务 ID，用于取消和状态追踪",
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    is_archived: Mapped[bool] = mapped_column(
        Boolean, nullable=False, default=False,
        comment="是否已归档（软归档，列表默认隐藏）",
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    created_by: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=False, default="anonymous",
        comment="创建者 actor（API Key 映射名），用于轻量多租户隔离",
    )
    # ---- 关系 ----
    test_suites: Mapped[list["TestSuite"]] = relationship(
        back_populates="task", cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    def __repr__(self) -> str:
        return f"<Task id={self.id} name={self.name!r} status={self.status.value}>"
# === 模块：backend/app/models/trace.py ===
# [功能] 执行轨迹模型：步骤、Token、耗时、费用
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Trace 模型 —— 记录 Agent 执行一次测试用例的完整轨迹。"""
import enum
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING
from sqlalchemy import Enum, Float, ForeignKey, Integer, JSON, String, Text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Mapped, mapped_column, relationship
from app.models.base import Base, PKMixin, TimestampMixin
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from app.models.metric_score import MetricScore
    from app.models.test_suite import TestSuite
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
class TraceStatus(str, enum.Enum):
    """轨迹执行状态。"""
    SUCCESS = "success"
    FAILED = "failed"
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class Trace(PKMixin, TimestampMixin, Base):
    """执行轨迹。
    保存 Agent 运行一次测试用例的完整 ReAct 步骤、Token 消耗和响应时间，
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    是评分和链路可视化的核心数据源。
    """
    __tablename__ = "traces"
    test_suite_id: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        ForeignKey("test_suites.id", ondelete="CASCADE"),
        nullable=False,
        comment="所属测试用例 ID",
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    user_query: Mapped[str] = mapped_column(
        Text, nullable=False, comment="本次执行使用的用户指令（快照）",
    )
    steps: Mapped[list] = mapped_column(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        JSON, nullable=False, default=list,
        comment="ReAct 步骤数组，每步包含 thought/action/observation 等字段",
    )
    total_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, comment="本次执行消耗的总 Token 数",
    )
    response_time_ms: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0, comment="执行耗时（毫秒）",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    )
    status: Mapped[TraceStatus] = mapped_column(
        Enum(TraceStatus, name="trace_status"),
        nullable=False,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        default=TraceStatus.SUCCESS,
        comment="执行状态：success / failed",
    )
    # ---- v1.0 新增：Token 分拆与成本追踪 ----
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    prompt_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0,
        comment="输入 Prompt Token 数",
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    completion_tokens: Mapped[int] = mapped_column(
        Integer, nullable=False, default=0,
        comment="输出 Completion Token 数",
    )
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    cost: Mapped[float] = mapped_column(
        Float, nullable=False, default=0.0,
        comment="本次执行费用（USD）",
    )
    # ---- v1.0 新增：版本追踪，确保可复现 ----
    agent_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="Agent 实现版本号",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    )
    prompt_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="Prompt 模板版本号",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    )
    model_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="LLM 模型版本（如 gpt-4o-2024-05-13）",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    )
    tool_version: Mapped[str | None] = mapped_column(
        String(100), nullable=True, default=None,
        comment="工具集版本号",
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    )
    # ---- 关系 ----
    test_suite: Mapped["TestSuite"] = relationship(back_populates="traces")
    metric_scores: Mapped[list["MetricScore"]] = relationship(
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        back_populates="trace", cascade="all, delete-orphan",
    )
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def __repr__(self) -> str:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        return f"<Trace id={self.id} status={self.status.value} tokens={self.total_tokens}>"
# === 模块：backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/audit.py ===
# [功能] backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/audit.py — 评测工作台业务模块
# [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑

"""Audit log query API."""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy import func, select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from app.core.dependencies import get_db
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from app.models.audit_log import AuditLog
router = APIRouter()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
@router.get("")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
async def list_audit_logs(
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100),
    action: str | None = Query(None, description="Filter by action"),
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    resource_type: str | None = Query(None),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """List recent audit events (newest first)."""
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    query = select(AuditLog)
    count_query = select(func.count(AuditLog.id))
    if action:
        query = query.where(AuditLog.action == action)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        count_query = count_query.where(AuditLog.action == action)
    if resource_type:
        query = query.where(AuditLog.resource_type == resource_type)
        count_query = count_query.where(AuditLog.resource_type == resource_type)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    total = (await session.execute(count_query)).scalar() or 0
    result = await session.execute(
        query.order_by(AuditLog.created_at.desc())
        .offset((page - 1) * page_size)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        .limit(page_size)
    )
    rows = result.scalars().all()
    items = [
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        {
            "id": r.id,
            "actor": r.actor,
            "action": r.action,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            "resource_type": r.resource_type,
            "resource_id": r.resource_id,
            "detail": r.detail,
            "request_id": r.request_id,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            "ip_address": r.ip_address,
            "created_at": r.created_at.isoformat() if r.created_at else None,
        }
        for r in rows
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    ]
    return {"items": items, "total": total, "page": page, "page_size": page_size}
# === 模块：backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/reports.py ===
# [功能] 报告聚合：指标分布、通过率、费用估算
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""评测报告导出接口 —— 提供任务级别的汇总报告生成功能。"""
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Request
from sqlalchemy import select
from sqlalchemy.ext.asyncio import AsyncSession
from sqlalchemy.orm import selectinload
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from app.core.dependencies import get_db
from app.core.tenancy import ensure_task_access
from app.models.task import Task
from app.models.test_suite import TestSuite
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
from app.models.trace import Trace
router = APIRouter()
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@router.get("/{task_id}")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def get_task_report(
    task_id: str,
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """生成指定任务的完整评测报告。
    报告包含：
    - 任务基本信息
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    - 测试用例总数与执行概况
    - 各维度平均分
    - 每个测试用例的详细评分
    - 耗时、Token 消耗统计
    """
    actor = getattr(request.state, "actor", None) or "anonymous"
    result = await session.execute(select(Task).where(Task.id == task_id))
    task = ensure_task_access(result.scalar_one_or_none(), actor, task_id)
    # 获取所有测试用例及其轨迹和评分
    suite_result = await session.execute(
        select(TestSuite)
        .options(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            selectinload(TestSuite.traces).selectinload(Trace.metric_scores),
        )
        .where(TestSuite.task_id == task_id)
    )
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    suites = suite_result.scalars().all()
    # 统计汇总
    total_suites = len(suites)
    total_traces = 0
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    total_tokens = 0
    total_time_ms = 0
    success_count = 0
    failed_count = 0
    # 维度汇总
    dimension_scores: dict[str, list[float]] = {}
    suite_details = []
    for suite in suites:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        traces = suite.traces or []
        trace_details = []
        for trace in traces:
            total_traces += 1
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            total_tokens += trace.total_tokens
            total_time_ms += trace.response_time_ms
            if trace.status.value == "success":
                success_count += 1
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            else:
                failed_count += 1
            scores_dict: dict[str, float] = {}
            for ms in trace.metric_scores or []:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                scores_dict[ms.metric_name] = ms.score
                if ms.metric_name not in dimension_scores:
                    dimension_scores[ms.metric_name] = []
                dimension_scores[ms.metric_name].append(ms.score)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            trace_details.append({
                "trace_id": trace.id,
                "status": trace.status.value,
                "total_tokens": trace.total_tokens,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                "response_time_ms": trace.response_time_ms,
                "scores": scores_dict,
                "created_at": trace.created_at.isoformat() if trace.created_at else None,
            })
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        suite_details.append({
            "suite_id": suite.id,
            "user_query": suite.user_query,
            "expected_output": suite.expected_output,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            "expected_tools": suite.expected_tools,
            "traces": trace_details,
        })
    # 计算各维度平均分
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    avg_dimension_scores: dict[str, float] = {}
    for dim, scores in dimension_scores.items():
        avg_dimension_scores[dim] = round(sum(scores) / len(scores), 1) if scores else 0.0
    # 总平均分
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    all_scores = [s for scores in dimension_scores.values() for s in scores]
    overall_avg = round(sum(all_scores) / len(all_scores), 1) if all_scores else 0.0
    return {
        "task": {
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            "id": task.id,
            "name": task.name,
            "description": task.description,
            "status": task.status.value,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            "created_at": task.created_at.isoformat() if task.created_at else None,
        },
        "summary": {
            "total_suites": total_suites,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            "total_traces": total_traces,
            "success_count": success_count,
            "failed_count": failed_count,
            "total_tokens": total_tokens,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            "total_time_ms": total_time_ms,
            "avg_time_per_trace_ms": round(total_time_ms / total_traces, 1) if total_traces else 0,
            "overall_score": overall_avg,
            "dimension_scores": avg_dimension_scores,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        },
        "details": suite_details,
    }
# === 模块：backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/settings.py ===
# [功能] backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/settings.py — 评测工作台业务模块

"""Settings / identity endpoints for the frontend Settings page."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
from __future__ import annotations
from typing import Any
from fastapi import APIRouter, Request
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from app.config import settings
from app.core.security import authenticate_api_key, extract_api_key, parse_api_keys
from app.core.tenancy import admin_actors, is_admin, tenancy_enforced
router = APIRouter()
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
class ActorInfoResponse(BaseModel):
    """Current authenticated actor and admin flag."""
    current_actor: str = Field(..., description="Resolved actor name")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    is_admin: bool = Field(..., description="Whether actor is in ADMIN_ACTORS")
    auth_enabled: bool = Field(..., description="Whether API key auth is enforced")
    tenancy_enabled: bool = Field(..., description="Whether task isolation is active")
    available_actors: list[str] = Field(
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        default_factory=list,
        description="Known actors from API_KEYS config (names only, not secrets)",
    )
    admin_actors: list[str] = Field(default_factory=list)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    api_key_configured: bool = Field(
        False, description="Whether a key was present on this request"
    )
    key_prefix: str | None = Field(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        None, description="Masked prefix of the presented key"
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
@router.get("/actor", response_model=ActorInfoResponse)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
async def get_current_actor(request: Request) -> Any:
    """Return identity derived from X-API-Key / Bearer token + settings.
    When AUTH_ENABLED is false, actor defaults to request.state.actor or 'anonymous'.
    """
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    raw_key = extract_api_key(request)
    identity = authenticate_api_key(raw_key) if raw_key else None
    if identity is not None:
        actor = identity.actor
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        key_prefix = identity.raw_key_prefix
        key_configured = True
    else:
        actor = getattr(request.state, "actor", None) or "anonymous"
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        key_prefix = None
        key_configured = bool(raw_key)
    # Known actor labels from API_KEYS (never expose secrets)
    known = sorted(set(parse_api_keys(settings.API_KEYS).values()))
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    admins = sorted(admin_actors())
    return ActorInfoResponse(
        current_actor=actor,
        is_admin=is_admin(actor),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        auth_enabled=bool(settings.AUTH_ENABLED),
        tenancy_enabled=tenancy_enforced(),
        available_actors=known,
        admin_actors=admins,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        api_key_configured=key_configured,
        key_prefix=key_prefix,
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构

    """Non-secret public settings snapshot for UI."""

# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    auth_enabled: bool
    tenancy_enabled: bool
    admin_actors: list[str]
    available_actors: list[str]
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
@router.get("", response_model=SettingsPublicResponse)
async def get_public_settings() -> Any:
    """Public settings (no secrets)."""
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    return SettingsPublicResponse(
        app_name=settings.APP_NAME,

        auth_enabled=bool(settings.AUTH_ENABLED),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        tenancy_enabled=tenancy_enforced(),

        available_actors=sorted(set(parse_api_keys(settings.API_KEYS).values())),
    )

# [功能] 任务 REST：创建/列表/导入/执行/取消

"""Task CRUD API endpoints with optional actor tenancy."""
from __future__ import annotations

# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
import json
from typing import Any
from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, File, HTTPException, Query, Request, UploadFile

# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
