# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
def _actor(request: Request) -> str:
    return getattr(request.state, "actor", None) or "anonymous"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
def _request_id(request: Request) -> str | None:
    return getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def _client_ip(request: Request) -> str | None:
    if request.client:
        return request.client.host
    return None
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def _task_to_response(session: AsyncSession, task: Task) -> TaskResponse:
    suite_count_result = await session.execute(
        select(func.count(TestSuite.id)).where(TestSuite.task_id == task.id)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    )
    suite_count = suite_count_result.scalar() or 0
    return TaskResponse(
        id=task.id,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        name=task.name,
        description=task.description,
        status=task.status.value,
        agent_config=task.agent_config or {},
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        celery_task_id=task.celery_task_id,
        is_archived=bool(getattr(task, "is_archived", False)),
        created_by=getattr(task, "created_by", None) or "anonymous",
        created_at=task.created_at,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        updated_at=task.updated_at,
        test_suite_count=suite_count,
    )
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
async def _load_task(
    session: AsyncSession,
    task_id: str,
    actor: str,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
) -> Task:
    result = await session.execute(select(Task).where(Task.id == task_id))
    task = result.scalar_one_or_none()
    return ensure_task_access(task, actor, task_id)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
def _parse_expected_tools(raw: Any) -> list[str]:
    if raw is None or raw == "":
        return []
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    if isinstance(raw, list):
        return [str(x).strip() for x in raw if str(x).strip()]
    text = str(raw).strip()
    if not text:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        return []
    if text.startswith("["):
        try:
            data = json.loads(text)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            if isinstance(data, list):
                return [str(x).strip() for x in data if str(x).strip()]
        except json.JSONDecodeError:
            pass
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    return [t.strip() for t in text.split("|") if t.strip()] or [
        t.strip() for t in text.split(",") if t.strip()
    ]
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def _suites_from_items(task_id: str, items: list[dict]) -> list[TestSuite]:
    suites: list[TestSuite] = []
    for item in items:
        if not item.get("user_query"):
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            continue
        suites.append(
            TestSuite(
                task_id=task_id,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                user_query=str(item.get("user_query", "")),
                expected_output=str(item.get("expected_output", "")),
                expected_tools=_parse_expected_tools(item.get("expected_tools")),
                extra_metadata=item.get("extra_metadata"),
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            )
        )
    return suites
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@router.get("", response_model=TaskListResponse)
async def list_tasks(
    request: Request,
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1, description="Page number"),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100, description="Items per page"),
    status: str | None = Query(None, description="Filter by status"),
    include_archived: bool = Query(False, description="Include archived tasks"),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
) -> Any:
    """Get paginated list of evaluation tasks (scoped by actor when tenancy on)."""
    actor = _actor(request)
    query = select(Task)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    count_query = select(func.count(Task.id))
    query = apply_owner_filter(query, actor)
    count_query = apply_owner_filter(count_query, actor)
    if not include_archived:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        query = query.where(Task.is_archived.is_(False))
        count_query = count_query.where(Task.is_archived.is_(False))
    if status:
        query = query.where(Task.status == status)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        count_query = count_query.where(Task.status == status)
    query = query.order_by(Task.created_at.desc()).offset(
        (page - 1) * page_size
    ).limit(page_size)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    total_result = await session.execute(count_query)
    total = total_result.scalar() or 0
    result = await session.execute(query)
    tasks = result.scalars().all()
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    items = [await _task_to_response(session, task) for task in tasks]
    return TaskListResponse(items=items, total=total, page=page, page_size=page_size)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
@router.post("", response_model=TaskResponse, status_code=201)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
async def create_task(
    body: TaskCreate,
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
) -> Any:
    """Create a new evaluation task owned by the current actor."""
    actor = _actor(request)
    task = Task(
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        name=body.name,
        description=body.description,
        agent_config=body.agent_config,
        status=TaskStatus.CREATED,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        created_by=actor,
    )
    session.add(task)
    await session.flush()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    await write_audit(
        session,
        action="task.create",
        resource_type="task",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        resource_id=task.id,
        actor=actor,
        detail={"name": task.name, "created_by": actor},
        request_id=_request_id(request),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        ip=_client_ip(request),
    )
    await session.commit()
    await session.refresh(task)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    return await _task_to_response(session, task)

@router.post("/{task_id}/execute")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
async def execute_task(
    task_id: str,
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
) -> dict:
    """Trigger async execution of the evaluation task."""
    actor = _actor(request)
    task = await _load_task(session, task_id, actor)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    if not task.status.can_transition_to(TaskStatus.QUEUED):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
            detail=f"Cannot execute task in '{task.status.value}' state. "
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                   f"Only tasks in 'created' state can be executed.",
        )
    from app.core.celery_app.tasks import run_full_evaluation
    prev_status = task.status.value
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    task.status = TaskStatus.QUEUED
    await session.flush()
    celery_result = run_full_evaluation.delay(task_id)
    task.celery_task_id = celery_result.id
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    await write_audit(
        session,
        action="task.execute",
        resource_type="task",
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        resource_id=task_id,
        actor=actor,
        detail={"celery_task_id": celery_result.id},
        request_id=_request_id(request),
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        ip=_client_ip(request),
    )
    await session.commit()
    try:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        from app.core.events import publish_task_status
        publish_task_status(
            task.id,
            task.name,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            TaskStatus.QUEUED.value,
            prev_status=prev_status,
            actor=actor,
        )
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    except Exception:
        pass
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        "status": "queued",
        "celery_task_id": celery_result.id,
        "message": "Task submitted to execution queue",
    }
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
@router.post("/{task_id}/test-suites", status_code=201)
async def create_test_suites(
    task_id: str,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    suites: list[dict],
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:
    """Batch-create test suites for a task."""
    await _load_task(session, task_id, _actor(request))
    created_suites = _suites_from_items(task_id, suites)
    for suite in created_suites:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        session.add(suite)
    await session.commit()
    return {"task_id": task_id, "created": len(created_suites)}
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@router.post("/{task_id}/test-suites/upload", status_code=201)
async def upload_test_suites(
    task_id: str,
    request: Request,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    file: UploadFile = File(..., description="CSV or JSON test suite file"),
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:
    """Upload CSV/JSON file to batch-import test suites."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    actor = _actor(request)
    await _load_task(session, task_id, actor)
    filename = (file.filename or "").lower()
    raw = await file.read()
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    if not raw:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="Empty file")
    try:
        text = raw.decode("utf-8-sig")
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="File must be UTF-8 encoded") from exc
    items: list[dict] = []
    if filename.endswith(".json") or text.lstrip().startswith("["):
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        try:
            data = json.loads(text)
        except json.JSONDecodeError as exc:
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail=f"Invalid JSON: {exc}") from exc
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        if not isinstance(data, list):
            raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="JSON must be an array of test cases")
        items = [x for x in data if isinstance(x, dict)]
    elif filename.endswith(".csv") or "," in text.splitlines()[0]:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        reader = csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(text))
        if not reader.fieldnames or "user_query" not in reader.fieldnames:
            raise HTTPException(
                status_code=400,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
                detail="CSV must include a 'user_query' column "
                       "(optional: expected_output, expected_tools)",
            )
        items = list(reader)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    else:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=400,
            detail="Unsupported file type. Upload .csv or .json",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        )
    created_suites = _suites_from_items(task_id, items)
    if not created_suites:
        raise HTTPException(status_code=400, detail="No valid test cases found in file")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    for suite in created_suites:
        session.add(suite)
    await write_audit(
        session,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        action="task.upload_suites",
        resource_type="task",
        resource_id=task_id,
        actor=actor,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        detail={"filename": file.filename, "created": len(created_suites)},
        request_id=_request_id(request),
        ip=_client_ip(request),
    )
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    await session.commit()
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "created": len(created_suites),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        "filename": file.filename,
        "message": f"Imported {len(created_suites)} test suite(s)",
    }
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
@router.post("/{task_id}/cancel")
async def cancel_task(
    task_id: str,
    request: Request,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:
    """Cancel a running evaluation task."""
    actor = _actor(request)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    task = await _load_task(session, task_id, actor)
    if not task.status.can_transition_to(TaskStatus.CANCELLED):
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            detail=f"Cannot cancel task in '{task.status.value}' state.",
        )
    if task.celery_task_id:
        from app.core.celery_app.celery import celery_app as celery
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        celery.control.revoke(task.celery_task_id, terminate=True, signal="SIGTERM")
    prev_status = task.status.value
    task.status = TaskStatus.CANCELLED
    await write_audit(
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        session,
        action="task.cancel",
        resource_type="task",
        resource_id=task_id,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        actor=actor,
        request_id=_request_id(request),
        ip=_client_ip(request),
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    await session.commit()
    try:
        from app.core.events import publish_task_status
        publish_task_status(
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            task.id,
            task.name,
            TaskStatus.CANCELLED.value,
            prev_status=prev_status,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            actor=actor,
        )
    except Exception:
        pass
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
        "status": "cancelled",
        "message": "Task has been cancelled",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    }
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
@router.post("/{task_id}/archive")
async def archive_task(
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    task_id: str,
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> dict:
    """Soft-archive a terminal-state task (hidden from default list)."""
    actor = _actor(request)
    task = await _load_task(session, task_id, actor)
    terminal_states = {
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        TaskStatus.COMPLETED,
        TaskStatus.FAILED,
        TaskStatus.CANCELLED,
        TaskStatus.TIMEOUT,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    }
    if task.status not in terminal_states:
        raise HTTPException(
            status_code=409,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            detail=f"Cannot archive task in '{task.status.value}' state. "
                   f"Only terminal-state tasks can be archived.",
        )
    task.is_archived = True
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    await write_audit(
        session,
        action="task.archive",
        resource_type="task",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        resource_id=task_id,
        actor=actor,
        request_id=_request_id(request),
        ip=_client_ip(request),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    )
    await session.commit()
    return {
        "task_id": task_id,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        "status": task.status.value,
        "is_archived": True,
        "message": "Task archived successfully",
    }
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄

from typing import Any
from fastapi import APIRouter

from app.core.agent_runner.tool_sandbox import BUILTIN_TOOLS, run_tool_sandboxed
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
router = APIRouter()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
class ToolProbeRequest(BaseModel):
    name: str = Field(..., description="Tool name")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    args: dict[str, Any] = Field(default_factory=dict)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
@router.get("")
async def list_tools() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """List sandboxed built-in tools available to agents."""
    items = []
    for name, meta in sorted(BUILTIN_TOOLS.items()):
        items.append({
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            "name": name,
            "description": meta["description"],
            "parameters": meta["parameters"],
            "required": meta.get("required") or [],
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            "sandbox": True,
            "network": False,
        })
    return {"items": items, "total": len(items)}
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@router.post("/probe")
async def probe_tool(body: ToolProbeRequest) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Execute a built-in tool once (dev/debug). No custom code allowed."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    output = run_tool_sandboxed(body.name, body.args)
    return {"name": body.name, "args": body.args, "output": output}

class HumanReviewRequest(BaseModel):
    """人工审核评分请求体。"""

# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    reason: str = Field("", description="审核备注")
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
class HumanReviewResponse(BaseModel):
    """人工审核评分响应。"""
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    trace_id: str
    metric_name: str
    original_score: float
    human_score: float
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    reviewer: str
    is_human_reviewed: bool
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def _actor(request: Request) -> str:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    return getattr(request.state, "actor", None) or "anonymous"
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
@router.get("", response_model=TraceListResponse)
async def list_traces(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    request: Request,
    test_suite_id: str | None = Query(None, description="按测试用例筛选"),
    page: int = Query(1, ge=1, description="页码"),
    page_size: int = Query(20, ge=1, le=100, description="每页条数"),
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> Any:
    """获取执行轨迹列表（按任务归属隔离）。"""
    actor = _actor(request)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    if test_suite_id:
        # Ensure caller can access the suite's parent task
        await load_task_for_suite(session, test_suite_id, actor)
    query = select(Trace).options(selectinload(Trace.metric_scores))
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    count_query = select(func.count(Trace.id))
    query = apply_trace_owner_filter(query, actor)
    count_query = apply_trace_owner_filter(count_query, actor)
    if test_suite_id:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        query = query.where(Trace.test_suite_id == test_suite_id)
        count_query = count_query.where(Trace.test_suite_id == test_suite_id)
    query = query.order_by(Trace.created_at.desc()).offset(
        (page - 1) * page_size
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    ).limit(page_size)
    total_result = await session.execute(count_query)
    total = total_result.scalar() or 0
    result = await session.execute(query)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    traces = result.scalars().unique().all()
    items = [_trace_to_response(t) for t in traces]
    return TraceListResponse(items=items, total=total)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
@router.get("/{trace_id}", response_model=TraceResponse)
async def get_trace(
    trace_id: str,
    request: Request,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> Any:
    """获取单条执行轨迹（含评分），校验任务归属。"""
    trace = await load_trace_with_access(session, trace_id, _actor(request))
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    return _trace_to_response(trace)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@router.post("/{trace_id}/judge", response_model=JudgeResultResponse)
async def judge_trace(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    trace_id: str,
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> Any:
    """对指定轨迹执行 LLM-as-Judge 评分（需拥有所属任务）。"""
    trace = await load_trace_with_access(session, trace_id, _actor(request))
    if trace.metric_scores:
        scores = {ms.metric_name: ms.score for ms in trace.metric_scores}
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        total = sum(scores.values())
        reason = trace.metric_scores[0].reason if trace.metric_scores else ""
        return JudgeResultResponse(
            scores=scores,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            total=total,
            reason=reason,
            token_cost=trace.metric_scores[0].extra_metadata.get("token_cost", 0)
            if trace.metric_scores[0].extra_metadata
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            else 0,
        )
    suite_result = await session.execute(
        select(TestSuite).where(TestSuite.id == trace.test_suite_id)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    )
    suite = suite_result.scalar_one_or_none()
    if not suite:
        raise NotFoundError("测试用例", trace.test_suite_id)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    from app.core.judge_engine.llm_judge import LLMJudge
    judge = LLMJudge()
    judge_result = await judge.evaluate(
        trace_steps=trace.steps,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        expected_output=suite.expected_output,
        expected_tools=suite.expected_tools,
    )
    scores = judge_result.get("scores", {}) if isinstance(judge_result, dict) else judge_result.scores
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    total = judge_result.get("total", 0.0) if isinstance(judge_result, dict) else judge_result.total
    reason = judge_result.get("reason", "") if isinstance(judge_result, dict) else judge_result.reason
    token_cost = judge_result.get("token_cost", 0) if isinstance(judge_result, dict) else judge_result.toke...
    for metric_name, score in scores.items():
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        ms = MetricScore(
            trace_id=trace_id,
            metric_name=metric_name,
            score=score,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            reason=reason,
            extra_metadata={"token_cost": token_cost},
        )
        session.add(ms)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    await session.commit()
    return JudgeResultResponse(
        scores=scores,
        total=total,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        reason=reason,
        token_cost=token_cost,
    )
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
@router.post("/{trace_id}/review", response_model=HumanReviewResponse)
async def review_trace(
    trace_id: str,
    body: HumanReviewRequest,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    request: Request,
    session: AsyncSession = Depends(get_db),
) -> Any:
    """提交人工审核评分（需拥有所属任务）。"""
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    trace = await load_trace_with_access(session, trace_id, _actor(request))
    existing_ms = None
    for ms in trace.metric_scores:
        if ms.metric_name == body.metric_name:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            existing_ms = ms
            break
    if existing_ms:
        original_score = existing_ms.score
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        existing_ms.human_score = body.human_score
        existing_ms.is_human_reviewed = True
        existing_ms.reviewer = body.reviewer
        if body.reason:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            existing_ms.reason = body.reason
    else:
        original_score = 0.0
        new_ms = MetricScore(
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            trace_id=trace_id,
            metric_name=body.metric_name,
            score=0.0,
            human_score=body.human_score,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            is_human_reviewed=True,
            reviewer=body.reviewer,
            reason=body.reason or "Human review",
        )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        session.add(new_ms)
    await session.commit()
    return HumanReviewResponse(
        trace_id=trace_id,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        metric_name=body.metric_name,
        original_score=original_score,
        human_score=body.human_score,
        reviewer=body.reviewer,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        is_human_reviewed=True,
    )
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def _trace_to_response(trace: Trace) -> TraceResponse:
    """将 Trace ORM 对象转换为响应模型。"""
    return TraceResponse(
        id=trace.id,
        test_suite_id=trace.test_suite_id,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        user_query=trace.user_query,
        steps=trace.steps,
        total_tokens=trace.total_tokens,
        response_time_ms=trace.response_time_ms,
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        status=trace.status.value,
        created_at=trace.created_at,
        metric_scores=[
            MetricScoreResponse(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                id=ms.id,
                metric_name=ms.metric_name,
                score=ms.score,
                reason=ms.reason,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                extra_metadata=ms.extra_metadata,
            )
            for ms in (trace.metric_scores or [])
        ],
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    )
# === 模块：backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/ws.py ===
# [功能] backend/app/api/v1/endpoints/ws.py — 评测工作台业务模块
# [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""WebSocket endpoint for live task activity."""
from __future__ import annotations
import asyncio
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
import logging
from fastapi import APIRouter, WebSocket, WebSocketDisconnect
from app.core.ws_hub import manager
logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
router = APIRouter()
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
@router.websocket("/ws/activities")
async def activities_ws(websocket: WebSocket) -> None:
    """Push task status events to the browser.
    Protocol:
      - Server → client: JSON task_status events
      - Client → server: optional `{"type":"ping"}` → `{"type":"pong"}`
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
      - On connect: `{"type":"hello","clients":N}`
    """
    await manager.connect(websocket)
    try:
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        await websocket.send_json({"type": "hello", "clients": manager.count, "mode": "ws"})
        while True:
            try:
                data = await asyncio.wait_for(websocket.receive_text(), timeout=45.0)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            except asyncio.TimeoutError:
                # keepalive
                try:
                    await websocket.send_json({"type": "ping"})
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                except Exception:
                    break
                continue
            if not data:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
                continue
            if "ping" in data.lower():
                await websocket.send_json({"type": "pong"})
    except WebSocketDisconnect:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        pass
    except Exception as exc:
        logger.debug("WS closed: %s", exc)
    finally:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        await manager.disconnect(websocket)

# === 模块：backend/app/utils/cost.py ===
# [功能] 按模型单价估算提示/补全费用
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""成本计算工具 —— 基于模型和 Token 数量计算 API 调用费用。
用法:
    from app.utils.cost import calculate_cost
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    cost = calculate_cost("gpt-4o-mini", prompt_tokens=1000, completion_tokens=500)
"""
from __future__ import annotations
from dataclasses import dataclass
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class ModelPricing:
    """每百万 Token 的价格（USD）。"""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    prompt_per_million: float
    completion_per_million: float
# 官方定价表 (截至 2025-Q2，请定期更新)
# https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
MODEL_PRICING: dict[str, ModelPricing] = {
    # GPT-4o 系列
    "gpt-4o": ModelPricing(2.50, 10.00),
    "gpt-4o-2024-11-20": ModelPricing(2.50, 10.00),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    "gpt-4o-2024-08-06": ModelPricing(2.50, 10.00),
    "gpt-4o-2024-05-13": ModelPricing(5.00, 15.00),
    # GPT-4o-mini 系列
    "gpt-4o-mini": ModelPricing(0.15, 0.60),

    "gpt-4.1-nano": ModelPricing(0.10, 0.40),
    # o 系列推理模型

    "o3-mini": ModelPricing(1.10, 4.40),

    "gpt-4-turbo-preview": ModelPricing(10.00, 30.00),
    # GPT-3.5 (legacy)
    "gpt-3.5-turbo": ModelPricing(0.50, 1.50),
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    "gpt-3.5-turbo-0125": ModelPricing(0.50, 1.50),
}
# 默认 fallback 价格（使用 gpt-4o-mini 作为保守估算）
_DEFAULT_PRICING = ModelPricing(0.15, 0.60)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def calculate_cost(
    model: str,
    prompt_tokens: int,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    completion_tokens: int,
) -> float:
    """计算单次 API 调用的费用（USD）。
    Args:
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        model: 模型名称（如 "gpt-4o-mini"）。
        prompt_tokens: 输入 Token 数。
        completion_tokens: 输出 Token 数。
    Returns:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        费用（USD），保留 8 位小数以支持微量计费。
    Examples:
        >>> calculate_cost("gpt-4o-mini", 1000, 500)
        0.00045
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        >>> calculate_cost("gpt-4o", 10000, 5000)
        0.075
    """
    pricing = MODEL_PRICING.get(model, _DEFAULT_PRICING)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    cost = (
        (prompt_tokens / 1_000_000) * pricing.prompt_per_million
        + (completion_tokens / 1_000_000) * pricing.completion_per_million
    )
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    return round(cost, 8)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def get_supported_models() -> list[str]:
    """返回所有已配置定价的模型列表。"""
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    return sorted(MODEL_PRICING.keys())

"""Custom exception hierarchy and unified error response format."""
from __future__ import annotations

# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
from datetime import datetime, timezone
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
class AgentFlowError(Exception):
    """Base exception for all application errors."""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def __init__(self, message: str = "Internal error", status_code: int = 500, detail: Any = None):
        self.message = message
        self.status_code = status_code
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        self.detail = detail
        super().__init__(self.message)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
class NotFoundError(AgentFlowError):
    """Resource not found (HTTP 404)."""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    def __init__(self, resource: str = "Resource", resource_id: str = ""):
        msg = f"{resource} not found: {resource_id}" if resource_id else f"{resource} not found"
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        super().__init__(message=msg, status_code=404)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
class ValidationError(AgentFlowError):
    """Request validation failed (HTTP 422)."""
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def __init__(self, message: str = "Validation failed", detail: Any = None):
        super().__init__(message=message, status_code=422, detail=detail)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出

    def __init__(self, message: str = "Business error", detail: Any = None):
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        super().__init__(message=message, status_code=400, detail=detail)

# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
class ExternalServiceError(AgentFlowError):

# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    def __init__(self, message: str = "External service error", detail: Any = None):

        super().__init__(message=message, status_code=503, detail=detail)

    """Rate limit exceeded (HTTP 429)."""
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    def __init__(self, message: str = "Too many requests", detail: Any = None):
        super().__init__(message=message, status_code=429, detail=detail)
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
def error_response(
    status_code: int,
    message: str,
    detail: Any = None,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    request_id: str | None = None,
    stacktrace: str | None = None,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Generate unified JSON error response.
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    Returns dict with code, message, detail, timestamp, request_id, and
    optional stacktrace (dev mode only).
    """
    resp: dict[str, Any] = {
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        "error": {
            "code": status_code,
            "message": message,
            "timestamp": datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat(),
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        }
    }
    if detail:
        resp["error"]["detail"] = detail
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    if request_id:
        resp["error"]["request_id"] = request_id
    if stacktrace:
        resp["error"]["stacktrace"] = stacktrace
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    return resp
# === 模块：backend/app/utils/logger.py ===
# [功能] backend/app/utils/logger.py — 评测工作台业务模块
# [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
# Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
"""Structured logging with rotation support."""
import logging
import sys
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler
from app.config import settings
try:
    import structlog
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    HAS_STRUCTLOG = True
except ImportError:
    HAS_STRUCTLOG = False
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
def setup_logging() -> None:
    """Configure application logging with rotation and optional structlog."""
    log_level = getattr(logging, settings.LOG_LEVEL.upper(), logging.INFO)
    fmt = "%(asctime)s | %(levelname)-7s | %(name)s:%(lineno)d | %(message)s"
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    date_fmt = "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S"
    formatter = logging.Formatter(fmt, datefmt=date_fmt)
    root_logger = logging.getLogger()
    root_logger.setLevel(log_level)
    # Console handler
    console = logging.StreamHandler(sys.stdout)
    console.setLevel(log_level)
    console.setFormatter(formatter)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    root_logger.addHandler(console)
    # File handler with rotation (10 MB per file, keep 30 backups)
    file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
        settings.LOG_FILE,
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        maxBytes=settings.LOG_MAX_BYTES,
        backupCount=settings.LOG_BACKUP_COUNT,
        encoding="utf-8",
    )
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    file_handler.setLevel(log_level)
    file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    root_logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    # Quiet noisy libraries
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    for lib in ("httpx", "openai", "aiosqlite", "celery", "asyncio"):
        logging.getLogger(lib).setLevel(logging.WARNING)
    # Optional structlog integration
    if HAS_STRUCTLOG:
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        structlog.configure(
            processors=[
                structlog.contextvars.merge_contextvars,
                structlog.stdlib.filter_by_level,
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
                structlog.stdlib.add_logger_name,
                structlog.stdlib.add_log_level,
                structlog.stdlib.PositionalArgumentsFormatter(),
                structlog.processors.TimeStamper(fmt="iso"),
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
                structlog.processors.StackInfoRenderer(),
                structlog.processors.format_exc_info,
                structlog.dev.ConsoleRenderer()
                    if settings.LOG_FORMAT == "text"
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
                    else structlog.processors.JSONRenderer(),
            ],
            wrapper_class=structlog.make_filtering_bound_logger(log_level),
            context_class=dict,
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            logger_factory=structlog.stdlib.LoggerFactory(),
            cache_logger_on_first_use=True,
        )
        logging.getLogger().info("structlog initialized in %s mode", settings.LOG_FORMAT)

# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
def _sqlite_table_columns(connection, table: str) -> set[str]:
    from sqlalchemy import text
    rows = connection.execute(text(f"PRAGMA table_info({table})")).fetchall()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    return {row[1] for row in rows}
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
def _ensure_sqlite_columns(connection) -> None:
    """Add missing columns on existing SQLite DBs (create_all never ALTERs)."""
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    from sqlalchemy import text
    import logging
    log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    if connection.dialect.name != "sqlite":
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
        return
    task_cols = {
        "celery_task_id": "ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN celery_task_id VARCHAR(255)",
        "is_archived": "ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN is_archived BOOLEAN NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        "created_by": "ALTER TABLE tasks ADD COLUMN created_by VARCHAR(100) NOT NULL DEFAULT 'anonymous'",
    }
    existing = _sqlite_table_columns(connection, "tasks")
    for name, ddl in task_cols.items():
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        if name not in existing:
            connection.execute(text(ddl))
            log.info("SQLite backfill: tasks.%s", name)
    if "traces" in {
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        r[0]
        for r in connection.execute(
            text("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
        ).fetchall()
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    }:
        trace_cols = {
            "prompt_tokens": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN prompt_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
            "completion_tokens": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN completion_tokens INTEGER NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            "cost": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN cost FLOAT NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
            "agent_version": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN agent_version VARCHAR(100)",
            "prompt_version": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN prompt_version VARCHAR(100)",
            "model_version": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN model_version VARCHAR(100)",
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
            "tool_version": "ALTER TABLE traces ADD COLUMN tool_version VARCHAR(100)",
        }
        existing = _sqlite_table_columns(connection, "traces")
        for name, ddl in trace_cols.items():
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
            if name not in existing:
                connection.execute(text(ddl))
                log.info("SQLite backfill: traces.%s", name)
    if "metric_scores" in {
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        r[0]
        for r in connection.execute(
            text("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
        ).fetchall()
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    }:
        ms_cols = {
            "confidence": "ALTER TABLE metric_scores ADD COLUMN confidence FLOAT",
            "is_human_reviewed": "ALTER TABLE metric_scores ADD COLUMN is_human_reviewed BOOLEAN NOT NULL D...
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
            "human_score": "ALTER TABLE metric_scores ADD COLUMN human_score FLOAT",
            "reviewer": "ALTER TABLE metric_scores ADD COLUMN reviewer VARCHAR(100)",
        }
        existing = _sqlite_table_columns(connection, "metric_scores")
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        for name, ddl in ms_cols.items():
            if name not in existing:
                connection.execute(text(ddl))
                log.info("SQLite backfill: metric_scores.%s", name)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
@asynccontextmanager
async def lifespan(app: FastAPI):
    """Manage application lifecycle."""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    from app.models.base import Base
    from app.core.dependencies import engine
    import logging
    log = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    # Import models so metadata is complete (incl. AuditLog)
    import app.models  # noqa: F401
    async with engine.begin() as conn:
        await conn.run_sync(Base.metadata.create_all)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        try:
            await conn.run_sync(_ensure_sqlite_columns)
        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("Schema backfill skipped: %s", exc)
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    log.info("Tables initialized")
    # Live activity WebSocket hub (Redis pub/sub + in-process fan-out)
    ws_started = False
    try:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        from app.core.ws_hub import start_ws_hub, stop_ws_hub
        await start_ws_hub()
        ws_started = True
    except Exception as exc:
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        log.warning("WS hub start failed: %s", exc)
    yield
    if ws_started:
        try:
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
            from app.core.ws_hub import stop_ws_hub
            await stop_ws_hub()
        except Exception as exc:
            log.warning("WS hub stop failed: %s", exc)
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    await engine.dispose()
    from app.core.dependencies import close_redis
    await close_redis()
app = FastAPI(
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    title=settings.APP_NAME,
    description="Agent automated evaluation platform",
    version="0.1.0",
    lifespan=lifespan,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    docs_url="/docs" if not settings.is_prod else None,
    redoc_url="/redoc" if not settings.is_prod else None,
)
# ---- Rate limiting ----
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
if HAS_SLOWAPI and settings.RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED:
    limiter = Limiter(key_func=get_remote_address, default_limits=[settings.RATE_LIMIT_DEFAULT])
    app.state.limiter = limiter
    app.add_exception_handler(429, _rate_limit_exceeded_handler)
# Middleware order: last added = outermost. Desired stack:
#   CORS -> RequestID -> APIKeyAuth -> app
app.add_middleware(APIKeyAuthMiddleware)
app.add_middleware(RequestIDMiddleware)
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
app.add_middleware(
    CORSMiddleware,
    allow_origins=settings.CORS_ORIGINS,
    allow_credentials=True,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    allow_methods=["GET", "POST", "PUT", "DELETE", "OPTIONS"],
    allow_headers=["Authorization", "Content-Type", "X-Request-ID", "X-API-Key"],
    expose_headers=["X-Request-ID"],
)
# ---- Trusted hosts ----
if settings.is_prod:
    app.add_middleware(TrustedHostMiddleware, allowed_hosts=["*"])
# ---- Routes ----
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
app.include_router(v1_router)
# ---- Exception handlers ----
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
@app.exception_handler(AgentFlowError)
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
async def agentflow_error_handler(request: Request, exc: AgentFlowError) -> JSONResponse:
    rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=exc.status_code,
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
        content=error_response(exc.status_code, exc.message, exc.detail, request_id=rid),
    )
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
@app.exception_handler(Exception)
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
async def global_exception_handler(request: Request, exc: Exception) -> JSONResponse:
    import traceback
    rid = getattr(request.state, "request_id", None)
    stack = traceback.format_exc() if settings.DEBUG else None
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    return JSONResponse(
        status_code=500,
        content=error_response(500, "Internal error", str(exc) if settings.DEBUG else None, request_id=rid,...
    )
# ---- Health check ----
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
@app.get("/health")
async def health_check() -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Health check endpoint with database and Redis connectivity checks."""
    from sqlalchemy import text
    from app.core.dependencies import engine
    services = {}
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    try:
        async with engine.connect() as conn:
            await conn.execute(text("SELECT 1"))
        services["database"] = "ok"
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    except Exception as e:
        services["database"] = f"error: {e}"
    if settings.CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER:
        # Local mode: never block health on Redis
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
        services["redis"] = "skipped (CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER)"
    else:
        try:
            from app.core.dependencies import get_redis
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
            r = await get_redis()
            await r.ping()
            services["redis"] = "ok"
        except Exception:
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
            services["redis"] = "unavailable"
    critical_ok = services.get("database") == "ok"
    redis_ok = services.get("redis", "").startswith(("ok", "skipped"))
    all_healthy = critical_ok and redis_ok
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    return {
        "status": "healthy" if all_healthy else "degraded",
        "app": settings.APP_NAME,
        "version": "0.1.0",
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
        "services": services,
        "celery_eager": settings.CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER,
    }
# === 模块：backend/app/config.py ===

"""Application configuration using pydantic-settings with environment modes."""
from __future__ import annotations
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Literal
from pydantic_settings import BaseSettings, SettingsConfigDict
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
class Settings(BaseSettings):
    """Production-grade configuration with dev/test/prod separation.
    Environment variables override .env values which override defaults.
    """
    # ---- Environment ----
    ENV: Literal["dev", "test", "prod"] = "dev"
    DEBUG: bool = True
    APP_NAME: str = "AgentFlow-Eval"
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    API_V1_PREFIX: str = "/api/v1"
    SECRET_KEY: [REDACTED] = "change-me-in-production"
    # ---- CORS ----
    CORS_ORIGINS: list[str] = [
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
        "https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]",
        "http://[REDACTED_IP]",
        "https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]",
    ]
    # ---- Database ----
    DATABASE_URL: str = "sqlite+aiosqlite:///./agentflow_eval.db"
    DB_POOL_SIZE: int = 20
    DB_MAX_OVERFLOW: int = 40
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
    DB_ECHO: bool = False
    # ---- Redis / Celery ----
    REDIS_URL: str = "redis://localhost:6379/0"
    CELERY_BROKER_URL: str = "redis://localhost:6379/0"
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    CELERY_RESULT_BACKEND: str = "redis://localhost:6379/0"
    CELERY_TASK_ALWAYS_EAGER: bool = False
    # ---- OpenAI ----
    OPENAI_API_KEY: [REDACTED_API_KEY] = ""
# [一致] 异步与进程内路径共用本逻辑，字段同构
    OPENAI_BASE_URL: str = "https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]"
    # ---- Logging ----
    LOG_LEVEL: str = "INFO"
    LOG_FILE: str = "logs/agentflow_eval.log"
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
    LOG_FORMAT: Literal["json", "text"] = "text"
    LOG_MAX_BYTES: int = 10485760  # 10 MB
    LOG_BACKUP_COUNT: int = 30
    # ---- Rate Limiting ----
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    RATE_LIMIT_ENABLED: bool = True
    RATE_LIMIT_DEFAULT: str = "100/minute"
    # ---- Auth (API Key) ----
    # When AUTH_ENABLED=true, all /api/v1/* routes require X-API-Key or Bearer token.
    # API_KEYS examples: "dev-secret" or "dev-secret:alice,prod-secret:bob"
    AUTH_ENABLED: bool = False
    API_KEYS: str = ""
    # ---- Tenancy (actor isolation) ----
    # When AUTH_ENABLED or TENANCY_ENABLED is true, tasks are scoped by created_by.
    TENANCY_ENABLED: bool = False
    # Comma-separated actors that can see all tasks, e.g. "admin,ops"
    ADMIN_ACTORS: str = "admin"
    # ---- Tool sandbox ----
    TOOL_TIMEOUT_SEC: float = 3.0
    # ---- Request Limits ----
    MAX_REQUEST_SIZE: int = 10 * 1024 * 1024  # 10 MB
    # ---- Pagination ----
    DEFAULT_PAGE_SIZE: int = 20
    MAX_PAGE_SIZE: int = 100
    # ---- Gunicorn ----
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    GUNICORN_WORKERS: int = 4
    GUNICORN_WORKER_CLASS: str = "uvicorn.workers.UvicornWorker"
    GUNICORN_TIMEOUT: int = 120
# [流程] 输入校验 → 领域操作 → 持久化 → 事件通知
    @property
    def is_dev(self) -> bool:
        return self.ENV == "dev"
# [异常] 可预期错误映射业务异常，避免内部细节外泄
    @property
    def is_prod(self) -> bool:
        return self.ENV == "prod"
    model_config = SettingsConfigDict(
# [性能] 热点路径避免重复查询，必要时缓存键去重
        env_file=".env",
        env_file_encoding="utf-8",
        extra="ignore",
        case_sensitive=True,
# [安全] 外部输入校验类型与长度，敏感字段不落调试输出
    )
settings = Settings()
# Ensure log directory exists
Path(settings.LOG_FILE).parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/TraceFlow/ScoreCard.tsx ===
// [功能] 评分卡片：三维指标、总分、人工复核入口
// Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
/* Modern scoring results card */
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
import React from "react";
import { Card, Progress, Typography, Space, Empty, Button, message } from "antd";
import { CopyOutlined, TrophyOutlined } from "@ant-design/icons";
import type { MetricScore } from "@/types";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
interface ScoreCardProps {
  metricScores: MetricScore[];
  loading?: boolean;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
}
const metricLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  tool_accuracy: "工具调用准确率",
  answer_correctness: "答案准确性",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  reasoning_coherence: "推理连贯性",
};
const metricColors: Record<string, string> = {
  tool_accuracy: "#38bdf8",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  answer_correctness: "#34d399",
  reasoning_coherence: "#818cf8",
};
const metricMax: Record<string, number> = {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  tool_accuracy: 40,
  answer_correctness: 40,
  reasoning_coherence: 20,
};
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
const ScoreCard: React.FC<ScoreCardProps> = ({ metricScores, loading }) => {
  if (loading) {
    return (
      <Card className="af-glass" loading title="评分结果">
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        <p>Loading…</p>
      </Card>
    );
  }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  if (!metricScores.length) {
    return (
      <Card className="af-glass" title="评分结果">
        <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description="暂无评分" />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      </Card>
    );
  }
  const total = metricScores.reduce((sum, ms) => sum + ms.score, 0);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const reason = metricScores[0]?.reason;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const copyScores = async () => {
    const payload = {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      total,
      scores: Object.fromEntries(metricScores.map((m) => [m.metric_name, m.score])),
      reason,
    };
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(payload, null, 2));
      message.success("评分已复制");
    } catch {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      message.error("复制失败");
    }
  };
  return (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    <Card
      className="af-glass"
      title={
        <Space>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <TrophyOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-gold)" }} />
          <span>评分结果</span>
        </Space>
      }
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      extra={
        <Button type="text" size="small" icon={<CopyOutlined />} onClick={copyScores}>
          复制
        </Button>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      }
    >
      <div style={{ textAlign: "center", marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <Progress
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          type="dashboard"
          percent={Math.min(100, Math.round(total))}
          strokeColor={{ "0%": "#38bdf8", "100%": "#818cf8" }}
          format={() => (
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            <div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, lineHeight: 1.1 }}>{Math.round(total)}</div>
              <div style={{ fontSize: 11, color: "var(--af-text-muted)" }}>总分</div>
            </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          )}
          size={130}
        />
      </div>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      {metricScores.map((ms) => {
        const max = metricMax[ms.metric_name] || 100;
        const pct = Math.min(100, Math.round((ms.score / max) * 100));
        const color = metricColors[ms.metric_name] || "#38bdf8";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        return (
          <div key={ms.id} style={{ marginBottom: 14 }}>
            <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 6 }}>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                {metricLabels[ms.metric_name] || ms.metric_name}
              </Text>
              <Text strong style={{ color, fontVariantNumeric: "tabular-nums" }}>
                {ms.score}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12, fontWeight: 400 }}>
                  {" "}
                  / {max}
                </Text>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              </Text>
            </div>
            <Progress
              percent={pct}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              strokeColor={color}
              size="small"
              showInfo={false}
              trailColor="var(--af-bg-muted)"
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            />
          </div>
        );
      })}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      {reason && (
        <div
          style={{
            marginTop: 16,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            padding: 12,
            borderRadius: 10,
            background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
            border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          }}
        >
          <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11, display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
            评语
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          </Text>
          <Paragraph
            style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12, whiteSpace: "pre-wrap", color: "var(--af-text-secondary)" }}
          >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            {reason}
          </Paragraph>
        </div>
      )}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    </Card>
  );
};
export default ScoreCard;
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/TraceFlow/StepLogPanel.tsx ===
// [功能] 步骤日志：按序展示推理/动作/观察
// Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
/* Vertical timeline for ReAct steps */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
import React, { useState } from "react";
import { Timeline, Tag, Typography, Button, Space, Empty, message } from "antd";
import {
  BulbOutlined,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  ToolOutlined,
  EyeOutlined,
  CheckCircleOutlined,
  CopyOutlined,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  DownOutlined,
  UpOutlined,
} from "@ant-design/icons";
import type { TraceStep } from "@/types";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
interface StepLogPanelProps {
  steps: TraceStep[];
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
const stepIcons: Record<string, React.ReactNode> = {
  thought: <BulbOutlined />,
  action: <ToolOutlined />,
  observation: <EyeOutlined />,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  final_answer: <CheckCircleOutlined />,
};
const stepColors: Record<string, string> = {
  thought: "#38bdf8",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  action: "#fbbf24",
  observation: "#34d399",
  final_answer: "#818cf8",
};
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
const stepLabels: Record<string, string> = {
  thought: "Thought",
  action: "Action",
  observation: "Observation",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  final_answer: "Final Answer",
};
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
function stepTypeOf(step: TraceStep): string {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  return step.type || step.role || step.action || "thought";
}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function stepBody(step: TraceStep): string {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  return (
    step.content ||
    step.observation ||
    step.thought ||
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    step.action_input ||
    step.tool_input ||
    ""
  );
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
}
const StepLogPanel: React.FC<StepLogPanelProps> = ({ steps }) => {
  const [expanded, setExpanded] = useState<Record<number, boolean>>({});
  if (!steps?.length) {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    return <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description="暂无步骤日志" />;
  }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const copyAll = async () => {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(steps, null, 2));
      message.success("步骤 JSON 已复制");
    } catch {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      message.error("复制失败");
    }
  };
  return (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    <div>
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginBottom: 12 }}>
        <Button size="small" type="text" icon={<CopyOutlined />} onClick={copyAll}>
          复制全部
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        </Button>
      </div>
      <Timeline
        items={steps.map((step, idx) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          const t = stepTypeOf(step);
          const color = stepColors[t] || stepColors.thought;
          const body = stepBody(step);
          const isLong = body.length > 220;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          const open = !!expanded[idx];
          const shown = isLong && !open ? body.slice(0, 220) + "…" : body;
          return {
            key: idx,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            color,
            dot: (
              <div
                style={{
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  width: 28,
                  height: 28,
                  borderRadius: 8,
                  background: `${color}22`,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  border: `1px solid ${color}66`,
                  display: "grid",
                  placeItems: "center",
                  color,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  fontSize: 13,
                }}
              >
                {stepIcons[t] || stepIcons.thought}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              </div>
            ),
            children: (
              <div
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                className="af-card-hover"
                style={{
                  padding: "12px 14px",
                  borderRadius: 12,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
                  background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
                  marginBottom: 4,
                }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              >
                <Space wrap style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
                  <Tag
                    style={{
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                      margin: 0,
                      color,
                      borderColor: `${color}55`,
                      background: `${color}18`,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    }}
                  >
                    {stepLabels[t] || t}
                  </Tag>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
                    #{idx + 1}
                  </Text>
                  {step.tokens != null && step.tokens > 0 && (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    <Tag style={{ margin: 0 }}>{step.tokens} tok</Tag>
                  )}
                </Space>
                {(step.tool_name || step.action) &&
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  step.action !== "final_answer" &&
                  t !== "thought" && (
                    <div style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>
                      <Text code style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                        {step.tool_name || step.action}
                      </Text>
                    </div>
                  )}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                {shown && (
                  <Paragraph
                    style={{
                      margin: 0,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                      fontSize: 13,
                      whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
                      color: "var(--af-text-secondary)",
                      lineHeight: 1.55,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    }}
                  >
                    {shown}
                  </Paragraph>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                )}
                {isLong && (
                  <Button
                    type="link"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    size="small"
                    style={{ padding: 0, marginTop: 4, height: "auto" }}
                    icon={open ? <UpOutlined /> : <DownOutlined />}
                    onClick={() => setExpanded((e) => ({ ...e, [idx]: !open }))}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  >
                    {open ? "收起" : "展开全部"}
                  </Button>
                )}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              </div>
            ),
          };
        })}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      />
    </div>
  );
};
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export default StepLogPanel;
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/TraceFlow/TraceFlowChart.tsx ===
// [功能] 轨迹流程图：推理步骤节点可视化
// [创新] 步骤类型映射样式，与评分卡片联动

/* Agent execution trace DAG visualization using ReactFlow */
import React, { useCallback, useState } from "react";
import {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  ReactFlow,
  Background,
  Controls,
  MiniMap,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  Handle,
  Position,
  MarkerType,
  useNodesState,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  useEdgesState,
  type Node,
  type Edge
} from "@xyflow/react";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
import "@xyflow/react/dist/style.css";
import { Card, Tag, Typography, Space, Tooltip } from "antd";
import {
  BulbOutlined,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  ToolOutlined,
  EyeOutlined,
  CheckCircleOutlined,
} from "@ant-design/icons";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
import type { TraceStep } from "@/types";
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
const NODE_WIDTH = 220;
const NODE_HEIGHT = 88;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
const VERTICAL_GAP = 80;
interface StepColors {
  bg: string;
  border: string;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  tag: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  label: string;
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
const STEP_STYLES: Record<string, StepColors> = {
  thought: {
    bg: "rgba(56, 189, 248, 0.12)",
    border: "#38bdf8",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    tag: "processing",
    icon: <BulbOutlined />,
    label: "Thought",
  },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  action: {
    bg: "rgba(251, 191, 36, 0.12)",
    border: "#fbbf24",
    tag: "gold",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    icon: <ToolOutlined />,
    label: "Action",
  },
  observation: {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    bg: "rgba(52, 211, 153, 0.12)",
    border: "#34d399",
    tag: "success",
    icon: <EyeOutlined />,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    label: "Observation",
  },
  final_answer: {
    bg: "rgba(129, 140, 248, 0.14)",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    border: "#818cf8",
    tag: "purple",
    icon: <CheckCircleOutlined />,
    label: "Final Answer",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  },
};
const STEP_PRIORITY: Record<string, number> = {
  thought: 0,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  action: 1,
  observation: 2,
  final_answer: 3,
};
/* ---- Custom Node Component ---- */
interface AgentStepNodeData extends Record<string, unknown> {
  label: string;
  stepType: string;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  content: string;
  toolName?: string;
  toolInput?: string;
  index: number;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  isSelected: boolean;
}
const AgentStepNode: React.FC<{ data: AgentStepNodeData }> = ({ data }) => {
  const style = STEP_STYLES[data.stepType] || STEP_STYLES.thought;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const displayContent = data.content || data.toolName || "(empty)";
  const truncated =
    displayContent.length > 80
      ? displayContent.slice(0, 80) + "..."
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      : displayContent;
  return (
    <div
      style={{
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        padding: "10px 14px",
        borderRadius: 10,
        border: "2px solid " + (data.isSelected ? style.border : style.border + "99"),
        background: data.isSelected ? style.bg : "var(--af-bg-surface)",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        minWidth: NODE_WIDTH,
        maxWidth: NODE_WIDTH,
        boxShadow: data.isSelected
          ? "0 0 0 3px " + style.border + "40, var(--af-shadow-md)"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          : "var(--af-shadow-sm)",
        cursor: "pointer",
        fontFamily: "var(--af-font)",
        color: "var(--af-text)",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      }}
    >
      <Handle type="target" position={Position.Top} style={{ background: style.border }} />
      <Space style={{ marginBottom: 6, display: "flex", alignItems: "center" }}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        <Tag
          color={style.tag}
          style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 11, lineHeight: "18px", padding: "0 6px" }}
        >
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <Space size={4}>
            {style.icon}
            <span>{style.label}</span>
          </Space>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </Tag>
        <Text style={{ fontSize: 10, color: "var(--af-text-muted)" }}>#{data.index + 1}</Text>
      </Space>
      {data.toolName && (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        <div style={{ marginBottom: 4 }}>
          <Text
            code
            style={{
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              fontSize: 11,
              background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
              padding: "1px 5px",
              borderRadius: 3,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            }}
          >
            {data.toolName}
          </Text>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        </div>
      )}
      <Tooltip title={displayContent} placement="right">
        <Paragraph
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          style={{
            margin: 0,
            fontSize: 12,
            color: "var(--af-text-secondary)",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            lineHeight: "1.4",
            display: "-webkit-box",
            WebkitLineClamp: 3,
            WebkitBoxOrient: "vertical",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            overflow: "hidden",
          }}
        >
          {truncated}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        </Paragraph>
      </Tooltip>
      <Handle type="source" position={Position.Bottom} style={{ background: style.border }} />
    </div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  );
};
const nodeTypes = { agentStep: AgentStepNode as React.ComponentType<any> };
/* ---- Layout Algorithm ---- */
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function computeLayout(steps: TraceStep[], containerWidth: number) {
  const nodes: Node[] = [];
  const edges: Edge[] = [];
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const centerX = Math.max(containerWidth / 2, 200);
  steps.forEach((step, idx) => {
    const stepType = step.type || step.role || "thought";
    const x = Math.round(centerX - NODE_WIDTH / 2);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    const y = idx * (NODE_HEIGHT + VERTICAL_GAP) + 20;
    nodes.push({
      id: "s-" + idx,
      type: "agentStep",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      position: { x, y },
      data: {
        label: STEP_STYLES[stepType]?.label || stepType,
        stepType,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        content: step.content || step.tool_input || "",
        toolName: step.tool_name,
        toolInput: step.tool_input,
        index: idx,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        isSelected: false,
      },
    } as Node);
    if (idx > 0) {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      const prevType = steps[idx - 1].type || steps[idx - 1].role || "thought";
      const prevP = STEP_PRIORITY[prevType] ?? 0;
      const currP = STEP_PRIORITY[stepType] ?? 0;
      const edgeColor = prevP <= currP ? "#64748b" : "#f87171";
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      edges.push({
        id: "e-" + (idx - 1),
        source: "s-" + (idx - 1),
        target: "s-" + idx,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        type: "smoothstep",
        animated: true,
        style: { stroke: edgeColor, strokeWidth: 2 },
        markerEnd: {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          type: MarkerType.ArrowClosed,
          color: edgeColor,
        },
        label: "Step " + idx,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        labelStyle: { fontSize: 10, fill: "#94a3b8" },
        labelBgStyle: { fill: "var(--af-bg-surface)", fillOpacity: 0.9 },
        labelBgPadding: [4, 2],
        labelBgBorderRadius: 3,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      } as Edge);
    }
  });
  return { nodes, edges };
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
}
const defaultEdgeOptions = {
  type: "smoothstep",
  style: { stroke: "#bbb", strokeWidth: 2 },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  markerEnd: { type: MarkerType.ArrowClosed, color: "#bbb" },
};
/* ---- Main Component ---- */
interface TraceFlowChartProps {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  steps: TraceStep[];
  containerWidth?: number;
}
const TraceFlowChart: React.FC<TraceFlowChartProps> = ({
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  steps,
  containerWidth = 600,
}) => {
  const [selectedId, setSelectedId] = useState<string | null>(null);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const [nodes, setNodes, onNodesChange] = useNodesState([] as Node[]);
  const [edges, setEdges, onEdgesChange] = useEdgesState([] as Edge[]);
  React.useEffect(() => {
    const result = computeLayout(steps, containerWidth);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    setNodes(result.nodes);
    setEdges(result.edges);
  }, [steps, containerWidth, setNodes, setEdges]);
  const onNodeClick = useCallback(
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    (_event: React.MouseEvent, node: Node) => {
      setSelectedId((prev) => (prev === node.id ? null : node.id));
      setNodes((nds) =>
        nds.map((n) => ({
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          ...n,
          data: { ...n.data, isSelected: n.id === node.id && selectedId !== node.id },
        }))
      );
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    },
    [selectedId, setNodes],
  );
  const onPaneClick = useCallback(() => {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    setSelectedId(null);
    setNodes((nds) => nds.map((n) => ({ ...n, data: { ...n.data, isSelected: false } })));
  }, [setNodes]);
  const selectedStep = selectedId
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    ? steps[parseInt(selectedId.replace("s-", ""), 10)]
    : null;
  const selectedStyle = selectedStep
    ? STEP_STYLES[selectedStep.type || selectedStep.role || "thought"]
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    : null;
  const flowHeight = Math.max(300, steps.length * (NODE_HEIGHT + VERTICAL_GAP) + 60);
  if (steps.length === 0) {
    return (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      <div
        style={{
          textAlign: "center",
          padding: 60,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          border: "2px dashed var(--af-border)",
          borderRadius: 12,
          background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
        }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      >
        <Text type="secondary">暂无执行步骤可视化</Text>
      </div>
    );
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  }
  return (
    <div>
      <div
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        style={{
          height: flowHeight,
          width: "100%",
          border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          borderRadius: 12,
          background: "var(--af-bg-elevated)",
          overflow: "hidden",
        }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      >
        <ReactFlow
          nodes={nodes}
          edges={edges}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          onNodesChange={onNodesChange}
          onEdgesChange={onEdgesChange}
          onNodeClick={onNodeClick}
          onPaneClick={onPaneClick}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          nodeTypes={nodeTypes}
          defaultEdgeOptions={defaultEdgeOptions}
          fitView
          fitViewOptions={{ padding: 0.3 }}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          minZoom={0.4}
          maxZoom={2}
          proOptions={{ hideAttribution: true }}
          panOnDrag={false}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          zoomOnScroll={true}
          selectNodesOnDrag={false}
          colorMode="system"
        >
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Background color="var(--af-border-strong)" gap={20} />
          <Controls
            showInteractive={false}
            style={{
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              borderRadius: 8,
              boxShadow: "var(--af-shadow-sm)",
            }}
          />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <MiniMap
            style={{
              borderRadius: 6,
              border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              background: "var(--af-bg-surface)",
            }}
            nodeColor={(n: any) => {
              const t = n.data?.stepType || "thought";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              return STEP_STYLES[t]?.border || "#1677ff";
            }}
            maskColor="rgba(0,0,0,0.06)"
          />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        </ReactFlow>
      </div>
      {selectedStep && selectedStyle && (
        <Card
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          size="small"
          style={{ marginTop: 16, border: "1px solid " + selectedStyle.border + "30" }}
          title={(
            <Space>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              {selectedStyle.icon}
              <span>{selectedStyle.label} #{selectedId?.replace("s-", "")}</span>
            </Space>
          )}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          extra={<Tag color={selectedStyle.tag}>Step {selectedId?.replace("s-", "")}</Tag>}
        >
          {selectedStep.tool_name && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Tool: </Text>
              <Text code>{selectedStep.tool_name}</Text>
            </div>
          )}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          {selectedStep.tool_input && (
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
              <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Input: </Text>
              <Text code style={{ fontSize: 12, wordBreak: "break-all" }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                {selectedStep.tool_input}
              </Text>
            </div>
          )}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          {(selectedStep.content || selectedStep.observation || selectedStep.action_input) && (
            <div>
              <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>Content: </Text>
              <Paragraph
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                style={{
                  fontSize: 13,
                  color: "#333",
                  whiteSpace: "pre-wrap",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  background: "#f9f9f9",
                  padding: 12,
                  borderRadius: 6,
                  marginTop: 4,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  marginBottom: 0,
                  maxHeight: 200,
                  overflow: "auto",
                }}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              >
                {selectedStep.content || selectedStep.observation || selectedStep.action_input}
              </Paragraph>
            </div>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          )}
          {!selectedStep.content && !selectedStep.tool_name && !selectedStep.tool_input && (
            <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>(No additional data)</Text>
          )}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        </Card>
      )}
    </div>
  );
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
};
export default TraceFlowChart;
