// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
import { PageSkeleton } from "@/components/ui/PageSkeleton";
import { useI18nStore } from "@/i18n";
const { Text } = Typography;
const PIE_COLORS = ["#38bdf8", "#818cf8", "#34d399", "#f87171", "#fbbf24", "#94a3b8"];
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
const FILTER_PREF_KEY = "agentflow_dashboard_filter";
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
function goTasks(navigate: ReturnType<typeof useNavigate>, status?: string) {
  const params = new URLSearchParams();
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  if (status) params.set("status", status);
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(
      FILTER_PREF_KEY,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      JSON.stringify({ status: status || "", at: Date.now() })
    );
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  }
  navigate(status ? `/tasks?status=${encodeURIComponent(status)}` : "/tasks");
}
const Dashboard: React.FC = () => {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useTasks({ page: 1, page_size: 50 });
  const tasks = data?.items ?? [];
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const total = data?.total ?? 0;
  const stats = useMemo(() => {
    const running = tasks.filter((t) =>
      ["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(t.status)
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    ).length;
    const completed = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === "completed").length;
    const failed = tasks.filter((t) => ["failed", "timeout"].includes(t.status)).length;
    const created = tasks.filter((t) => t.status === "created").length;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    return { total, running, completed, failed, created };
  }, [tasks, total]);
  const pieData = useMemo(() => {
    const map: Record<string, number> = {};
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    tasks.forEach((t) => {
      map[t.status] = (map[t.status] || 0) + 1;
    });
    return Object.entries(map).map(([name, value]) => ({ name, value }));
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  }, [tasks]);
  const barData = useMemo(() => {
    // Last 7 buckets by day label of created_at
    const buckets: Record<string, number> = {};
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    tasks.forEach((t) => {
      if (!t.created_at) return;
      const d = new Date(t.created_at);
      const key = `${d.getMonth() + 1}/${d.getDate()}`;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      buckets[key] = (buckets[key] || 0) + 1;
    });
    return Object.entries(buckets)
      .slice(-7)
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      .map(([day, count]) => ({ day, count }));
  }, [tasks]);
  const completionRate =
    stats.total > 0 ? Math.round((stats.completed / Math.max(stats.total, 1)) * 100) : 0;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageSkeleton variant="dashboard" />;
  }
  if (error) {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    const errMsg = (error as Error)?.message || "未知错误";
    return (
      <Alert
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        type="error"
        message="加载总览失败"
        description={
          <div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            <div>{errMsg}</div>
            <div style={{ marginTop: 8, opacity: 0.75, fontSize: 12 }}>
              本地开发请先启动后端（端口 8000），再刷新本页。可运行 scripts/start-local.ps1
            </div>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          </div>
        }
        showIcon
        action={
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Button size="small" onClick={() => refetch()}>
            重试
          </Button>
        }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      />
    );
  }
  const statCards = [
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    {
      title: t("dashboard.total"),
      value: stats.total,
      icon: <ExperimentOutlined />,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      color: "var(--af-primary)",
      status: "",
    },
    {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      title: t("dashboard.running"),
      value: stats.running,
      icon: <ClockCircleOutlined />,
      color: "#818cf8",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      status: "running",
    },
    {
      title: t("dashboard.completed"),
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      value: stats.completed,
      icon: <CheckCircleOutlined />,
      color: "var(--af-success)",
      status: "completed",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    },
    {
      title: t("dashboard.failed"),
      value: stats.failed,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      icon: <CloseCircleOutlined />,
      color: "var(--af-danger)",
      status: "failed",
    },
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  ];
  return (
    <div className="af-page">
      <PageHeader
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        title={t("dashboard.title")}
        subtitle={t("dashboard.subtitle")}
        icon={<DashboardOutlined />}
        extra={
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <Button
            type="primary"
            icon={<PlusOutlined />}
            onClick={() => navigate("/tasks/create")}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
          >
            {t("dashboard.create")}
          </Button>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        }
      />
      <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        {statCards.map((s) => (
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <Col xs={12} lg={6} key={s.title}>
            <Tooltip title={t("dashboard.clickHint")}>
              <Card
                className="af-glass af-card-hover"
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                styles={{ body: { padding: 18 } }}
                style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
                onClick={() => goTasks(navigate, s.status || undefined)}
              >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", alignItems: "flex-start" }}>
                  <div>
                    <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
                      {s.title}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    </Text>
                    <div style={{ fontSize: 28, fontWeight: 800, letterSpacing: "-0.03em", marginTop: 4 }}>
                      {s.value}
                    </div>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  </div>
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: 40,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                      height: 40,
                      borderRadius: 12,
                      background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
                      display: "grid",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      placeItems: "center",
                      color: s.color,
                      fontSize: 18,
                    }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  >
                    {s.icon}
                  </div>
                </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              </Card>
            </Tooltip>
          </Col>
        ))}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      </Row>
      <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
        <Col xs={24} lg={8}>
          <Card
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            className="af-glass af-card-hover"
            title={t("dashboard.rate")}
            styles={{ body: { padding: 20, textAlign: "center" } }}
            style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            onClick={() => goTasks(navigate, "completed")}
          >
            <Progress
              type="dashboard"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              percent={completionRate}
              strokeColor={{ "0%": "#38bdf8", "100%": "#818cf8" }}
              size={140}
            />
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>
              <Text type="secondary">
                {stats.completed} / {stats.total} {t("dashboard.completed")}
              </Text>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            </div>
          </Card>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} lg={8}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <Card className="af-glass" title={t("dashboard.statusDist")} styles={{ body: { height: 220 } }}>
            {pieData.length === 0 ? (
              <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={t("dashboard.noData")} />
            ) : (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <PieChart>
                  <Pie
                    data={pieData}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    dataKey="value"
                    nameKey="name"
                    innerRadius={48}
                    outerRadius={72}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    paddingAngle={3}
                    style={{ cursor: "pointer" }}
                    onClick={(_, index) => {
                      const slice = pieData[index];
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      if (slice?.name) goTasks(navigate, slice.name);
                    }}
                  >
                    {pieData.map((_, i) => (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                      <Cell key={i} fill={PIE_COLORS[i % PIE_COLORS.length]} />
                    ))}
                  </Pie>
                  <RTooltip />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                </PieChart>
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            )}
          </Card>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} lg={8}>
          <Card className="af-glass" title={t("dashboard.trend")} styles={{ body: { height: 220 } }}>
            {barData.length === 0 ? (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              <Empty image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} description={t("dashboard.noData")} />
            ) : (
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <BarChart data={barData}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  <CartesianGrid strokeDasharray="3 3" stroke="var(--af-border)" />
                  <XAxis dataKey="day" tick={{ fill: "var(--af-text-muted)", fontSize: 11 }} />
                  <YAxis allowDecimals={false} tick={{ fill: "var(--af-text-muted)", fontSize: 11 }} />
                  <RTooltip />
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  <Bar
                    dataKey="count"
                    fill="#38bdf8"
                    radius={[6, 6, 0, 0]}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    cursor="pointer"
                    onClick={() => goTasks(navigate)}
                  />
                </BarChart>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            )}
          </Card>
        </Col>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      </Row>
      <Card
        className="af-glass"
        title={
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Space>
            <span>{t("dashboard.recent")}</span>
            <Tag>{Math.min(tasks.length, 8)}</Tag>
          </Space>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        }
        extra={
          <Button type="link" onClick={() => goTasks(navigate)} icon={<ArrowRightOutlined />}>
            {t("dashboard.allTasks")}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          </Button>
        }
      >
        {tasks.length === 0 ? (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          <Empty description={t("dashboard.noTasks")} />
        ) : (
          <Space direction="vertical" size={10} style={{ width: "100%" }}>
            {tasks.slice(0, 8).map((t: Task) => (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <div
                key={t.id}
                onClick={() => navigate(`/tasks/${t.id}`)}
                className="af-card-hover"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                style={{
                  display: "flex",
                  justifyContent: "space-between",
                  alignItems: "center",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  gap: 12,
                  padding: "12px 14px",
                  borderRadius: 12,
                  border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
                  cursor: "pointer",
                  flexWrap: "wrap",
                }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              >
                <div style={{ minWidth: 0, flex: 1 }}>
                  <Text strong ellipsis style={{ display: "block" }}>
                    {t.name}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  </Text>
                  <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                    {formatDateTime(t.created_at)} · {t.test_suite_count} 用例
                  </Text>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                </div>
                <Space>
                  <OwnerBadge owner={t.created_by} />
                  <StatusBadge status={t.status} />
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                </Space>
              </div>
            ))}
          </Space>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        )}
      </Card>
    </div>
  );
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
};
export default Dashboard;

import { Button, Result, Space } from "antd";

export default function NotFound() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return (
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    <div className="af-page" style={{ paddingTop: 48 }}>
      <div className="af-glass" style={{ maxWidth: 560, margin: "0 auto", padding: "32px 24px" }}>
        <Result
          status="404"
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          title={<span className="af-gradient-text">404</span>}
          subTitle="页面不存在，或你没有访问权限。"
          extra={
            <Space wrap>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <Button
                type="primary"
                icon={<HomeOutlined />}
                onClick={() => navigate("/")}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
              >
                回到总览
              </Button>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              <Button icon={<UnorderedListOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate("/tasks")}>
                任务列表
              </Button>
            </Space>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          }
        />
      </div>
    </div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  );
}

// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
import { useI18nStore, type Locale } from "@/i18n";
const { Title, Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
const STORAGE_KEY = "agentflow_settings";
interface LocalSettings {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  apiBaseUrl: string;
  apiKey: [REDACTED_API_KEY]
  pollIntervalSec: number;
  showArchived: boolean;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  preferredActorLabel: string;
}
const defaults: LocalSettings = {
  apiBaseUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "/api/v1",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  apiKey: "",
  pollIntervalSec: 3,
  showArchived: false,
  preferredActorLabel: "",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
};
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function loadSettings(): LocalSettings {
  try {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (!raw) return { ...defaults };
    return { ...defaults, ...JSON.parse(raw) };
  } catch {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    return { ...defaults };
  }
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function maskKey(key: string): string {
  if (!key) return "未配置";
  if (key.length <= 8) return "••••••••";
  return `${key.slice(0, 4)}••••${key.slice(-4)}`;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
export default function Settings() {
  const { token } = theme.useToken();
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const { mode, setMode } = useThemeStore();
  const locale = useI18nStore((s) => s.locale);
  const setLocale = useI18nStore((s) => s.setLocale);
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const [form] = Form.useForm<LocalSettings>();
  const [saved, setSaved] = useState<LocalSettings>(loadSettings);
  const [actorInfo, setActorInfo] = useState<ActorInfo | null>(null);
  const [actorLoading, setActorLoading] = useState(false);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const [actorError, setActorError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [showKey, setShowKey] = useState(false);
  const [saving, setSaving] = useState(false);
  const fetchActor = useCallback(async () => {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    setActorLoading(true);
    setActorError(null);
    try {
      const data = await settingsApi.getActor();
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      setActorInfo(data);
    } catch (err: any) {
      const msg =
        err?.response?.data?.error?.message ||
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        err?.response?.data?.detail ||
        err?.message ||
        "无法获取当前身份";
      setActorError(typeof msg === "string" ? msg : "无法获取当前身份");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      setActorInfo(null);
    } finally {
      setActorLoading(false);
    }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
    form.setFieldsValue(saved);
  }, [form, saved]);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  useEffect(() => {
    fetchActor();
  }, [fetchActor, saved.apiKey]);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const onSave = async () => {
    try {
      setSaving(true);
      const values = await form.validateFields();
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      const next: LocalSettings = {
        apiBaseUrl: values.apiBaseUrl?.trim() || defaults.apiBaseUrl,
        apiKey: [REDACTED_API_KEY] || "").trim(),
        pollIntervalSec: Number(values.pollIntervalSec) || 3,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        showArchived: !!values.showArchived,
        preferredActorLabel: (values.preferredActorLabel || "").trim(),
      };
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(next));
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      setSaved(next);
      message.success("设置已保存，正在刷新身份…");
      setTimeout(() => fetchActor(), 80);
    } finally {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      setSaving(false);
    }
  };
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const onReset = () => {
    localStorage.removeItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    setSaved({ ...defaults });
    form.setFieldsValue(defaults);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    message.info("已恢复默认设置");
    setTimeout(() => fetchActor(), 80);
  };
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const copyActor = async () => {
    const a = actorInfo?.current_actor || "anonymous";
    try {
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(a);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      message.success("Actor 已复制");
    } catch {
      message.error("复制失败");
    }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  };
  const displayActor = actorInfo?.current_actor || "anonymous";
  const isAdmin = !!actorInfo?.is_admin;
  const actorOptions = useMemo(
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    () =>
      (actorInfo?.available_actors || []).map((a) => ({
        value: a,
        label: (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Space>
            <Tag color={(actorInfo?.admin_actors || []).includes(a) ? "gold" : "blue"}>{a}</Tag>
            {(actorInfo?.admin_actors || []).includes(a) && (
              <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                admin
              </Text>
            )}
          </Space>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        ),
      })),
    [actorInfo]
  );
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  return (
    <div className="af-page">
      {/* Hero */}
      <div
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        style={{
          marginBottom: 24,
          padding: "22px 24px",
          borderRadius: 16,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
          background: "var(--af-gradient-soft)",
          boxShadow: "var(--af-shadow-sm)",
          position: "relative",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求

        }}
      >
        <div
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          style={{
            position: "absolute",
            right: -40,
            top: -40,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            width: 180,
            height: 180,
            borderRadius: "50%",
            background: "var(--af-gradient)",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            opacity: 0.12,
            filter: "blur(8px)",
          }}
        />
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        <Space align="start" size={14}>
          <div
            style={{
              width: 48,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              height: 48,
              borderRadius: 14,
              background: "var(--af-gradient)",
              display: "grid",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              placeItems: "center",
              color: "#fff",
              boxShadow: "var(--af-shadow-glow)",
            }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          >
            <SettingOutlined style={{ fontSize: 22 }} />
          </div>
          <div>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            <Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0, letterSpacing: "-0.02em" }}>
              设置中心
            </Title>
            <Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: "6px 0 0", maxWidth: 560 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              管理 API Key、当前身份与工作区偏好。敏感密钥仅存本机浏览器，不会上传到前端代码仓库。
            </Paragraph>

// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      </div>

            }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          >
            {actorLoading && !actorInfo ? (
              <div style={{ padding: 24 }}>
                <Spin style={{ display: "block", margin: "24px auto" }} />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                <Text type="secondary" style={{ display: "block", textAlign: "center" }}>
                  正在同步身份…

              </div>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            ) : actorError ? (
              <Alert type="warning" showIcon message="身份服务不可用" description={actorError} />

              <>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥

// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测

                </Row>
                <Form.Item
                  label="默认显示已归档任务"
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  name="showArchived"
                  valuePropName="checked"
                  style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
                >
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  <Switch checkedChildren="显示" unCheckedChildren="隐藏" />
                </Form.Item>
              </Form>
              <Divider style={{ margin: "12px 0 20px" }} />
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              <Space wrap>
                <Button
                  type="primary"
                  size="large"
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  icon={<SaveOutlined />}
                  loading={saving}
                  onClick={onSave}
                  style={{
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    background: "var(--af-gradient)",
                    border: "none",
                    minWidth: 120,
                  }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                >
                  保存设置
                </Button>
                <Button size="large" icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={onReset}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  恢复默认
                </Button>
                <Tooltip title="重新向后端请求当前 actor">
                  <Button size="large" onClick={fetchActor} loading={actorLoading}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    校验身份
                  </Button>
                </Tooltip>
              </Space>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            </Card>
            <Card
              className="af-glass"
              size="small"
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              styles={{ body: { padding: 16 } }}
              style={{ borderStyle: "dashed" }}
            >
              <Descriptions column={1} size="small">
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <Descriptions.Item label="前端版本">0.1.0</Descriptions.Item>
                <Descriptions.Item label="构建模式">{import.meta.env.MODE}</Descriptions.Item>
                <Descriptions.Item label="主题 token">
                  <Text code style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                    primary={token.colorPrimary}
                  </Text>
                </Descriptions.Item>
              </Descriptions>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            </Card>
          </Space>
        </Col>
      </Row>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    </div>
  );
}

// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
const DIMENSION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  tool_accuracy: "工具准确率",
  answer_correctness: "答案正确性",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  reasoning_coherence: "推理连贯性",
};
const DIMENSION_MAX: Record<string, number> = {
  tool_accuracy: 40,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  answer_correctness: 40,
  reasoning_coherence: 20,
};
const DIM_COLORS = ["#38bdf8", "#34d399", "#818cf8"];
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
function exportJson(data: TaskReport, filename: string) {
  const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(data, null, 2)], { type: "application/json" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const a = document.createElement("a");
  a.href = url;
  a.download = filename;
  a.click();
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function exportCsv(report: TaskReport) {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const rows: string[] = [
    '"suite","status","tool_accuracy","answer_correctness","reasoning_coherence","total","tokens","time_ms"',
  ];
  for (const d of report.details || []) {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    for (const t of d.traces || []) {
      const scores = t.scores || {};
      const total = Object.values(scores).reduce((a: number, b: number) => a + b, 0);
      rows.push(
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        [
          `"${(d.user_query || "").replace(/"/g, '""')}"`,
          t.status,
          scores.tool_accuracy ?? "",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          scores.answer_correctness ?? "",
          scores.reasoning_coherence ?? "",
          total,
          t.total_tokens,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          t.response_time_ms,
        ].join(",")
      );
    }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  }
  const blob = new Blob([rows.join("\n")], { type: "text/csv;charset=utf-8" });
  const url = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a = document.createElement("a");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  a.href = url;
  a.download = "evaluation-report.csv";
  a.click();
  URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export default function ReportDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { data: report, isLoading, error, refetch } = useTaskReport(id);
  const chartData = useMemo(() => {
    if (!report?.summary?.dimension_scores) return [];
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    const ds = report.summary.dimension_scores;
    return Object.entries(DIMENSION_LABELS).map(([key, label]) => ({
      dimension: label,
      key,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      value: Math.round(((ds[key] || 0) / (DIMENSION_MAX[key] || 1)) * 100),
      raw: ds[key] || 0,
      max: DIMENSION_MAX[key] || 0,
    }));
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  }, [report]);
  const handleExportJson = useCallback(() => {
    if (!report) return;
    exportJson(report, `report-${id}.json`);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    message.success("JSON 已导出");
  }, [report, id]);
  const handleExportCsv = useCallback(() => {
    if (!report) return;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    exportCsv(report);
    message.success("CSV 已导出");
  }, [report]);
  const handleExportPdf = useCallback(async () => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    if (!report) return;
    try {
      message.loading({ content: "正在生成 PDF…", key: "pdf" });
      await exportReportPdf(report, `report-${id || report.task?.id}.pdf`);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      message.success({ content: "PDF 已下载", key: "pdf" });
    } catch (e) {
      message.error({ content: "PDF 生成失败", key: "pdf" });
    }
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  }, [report, id]);
  const handleCopyJson = useCallback(async () => {
    if (!report) return;
    try {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(JSON.stringify(report, null, 2));
      message.success("报告 JSON 已复制到剪贴板");
    } catch {
      message.error("复制失败");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    }
  }, [report]);
  const tableData = useMemo(() => {
    const result: Array<Record<string, unknown>> = [];
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    for (const d of report?.details || []) {
      for (const t of d.traces || []) {
        const scores = t.scores || {};
        result.push({
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          key: t.trace_id,
          user_query: d.user_query,
          status: t.status,
          tool_accuracy: scores.tool_accuracy,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          answer_correctness: scores.answer_correctness,
          reasoning_coherence: scores.reasoning_coherence,
          total: (Object.values(scores) as number[]).reduce((a: number, b: number) => a + b, 0),
          total_tokens: t.total_tokens,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          response_time_ms: t.response_time_ms,
          trace_id: t.trace_id,
        });
      }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    }
    return result;
  }, [report]);
  const columns = [
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    {
      title: "用例",
      dataIndex: "user_query",
      key: "user_query",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      ellipsis: true,
      width: 260,
    },
    {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      title: "状态",
      dataIndex: "status",
      key: "status",
      width: 100,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      render: (s: string) => <StatusBadge status={s} />,
    },
    {
      title: "工具",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      dataIndex: "tool_accuracy",
      key: "tool_accuracy",
      width: 80,
      render: (v: number) => v ?? "-",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    },
    {
      title: "答案",
      dataIndex: "answer_correctness",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      key: "answer_correctness",
      width: 80,
      render: (v: number) => v ?? "-",
    },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    {
      title: "推理",
      dataIndex: "reasoning_coherence",
      key: "reasoning_coherence",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      width: 80,
      render: (v: number) => v ?? "-",
    },
    {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      title: "总分",
      dataIndex: "total",
      key: "total",
      width: 80,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      render: (v: number) => <Text strong>{typeof v === "number" ? Math.round(v) : v}</Text>,
    },
    {
      title: "Tokens",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      dataIndex: "total_tokens",
      key: "total_tokens",
      width: 90,
    },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    {
      title: "耗时",
      dataIndex: "response_time_ms",
      key: "response_time_ms",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      width: 90,
      render: (v: number) => (v != null ? `${v}ms` : "-"),
    },
    {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      title: "操作",
      key: "actions",
      width: 110,
      render: (_: unknown, record: { trace_id?: string }) =>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        record.trace_id ? (
          <Button
            size="small"
            type="link"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            icon={<EyeOutlined />}
            onClick={() => id && navigate(`/tasks/${id}`)}
          >
            轨迹
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          </Button>
        ) : null,
    },
  ];
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  if (isLoading) {
    return <PageSkeleton variant="report" />;
  }
  if (error) {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    return (
      <Alert
        type="error"
        message="报告加载失败"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        description={(error as Error)?.message || "发生错误"}
        showIcon
        action={<Button onClick={() => refetch()}>重试</Button>}
      />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    );
  }
  if (!report) {
    return (
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      <div className="af-glass" style={{ padding: 48, textAlign: "center" }}>
        <Empty description="暂无报告数据">
          <Button onClick={() => id && navigate(`/tasks/${id}`)}>返回任务</Button>
        </Empty>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      </div>
    );
  }
  const summary = report.summary;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const overall = summary?.overall_score ?? 0;
  const passRate = summary?.total_traces
    ? Math.round((summary.success_count / summary.total_traces) * 100)
    : 0;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  return (
    <div className="af-page af-print-report">
      <div className="af-no-print">
        <PageHeader
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          title="评测报告"
          subtitle={report.task?.name || id}
          icon={<BarChartOutlined />}
          extra={
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            <Space wrap>
              <Button icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate(`/tasks/${id}`)}>
                返回任务
              </Button>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              <Button icon={<CopyOutlined />} onClick={handleCopyJson}>
                复制 JSON
              </Button>
              <Dropdown
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                menu={{
                  items: [
                    {
                      key: "json",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                      icon: <FileTextOutlined />,
                      label: "导出 JSON",
                      onClick: handleExportJson,
                    },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    {
                      key: "csv",
                      icon: <DownloadOutlined />,
                      label: "导出 CSV",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                      onClick: handleExportCsv,
                    },
                    {
                      key: "pdf",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                      icon: <FilePdfOutlined />,
                      label: "导出 PDF（正式）",
                      onClick: () => {
                        void handleExportPdf();
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      },
                    },
                    {
                      key: "print",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      icon: <PrinterOutlined />,
                      label: "浏览器打印",
                      onClick: () => {
                        message.info("请在打印对话框中选择「另存为 PDF」");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                        setTimeout(() => window.print(), 200);
                      },
                    },
                  ],
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                }}
              >
                <Button
                  type="primary"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  icon={<ShareAltOutlined />}
                  style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
                >
                  导出
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                </Button>
              </Dropdown>
              <Button
                icon={<FilePdfOutlined />}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                onClick={() => {
                  void handleExportPdf();
                }}
              >
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                下载 PDF
              </Button>
            </Space>
          }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        />
      </div>
      {/* Hero score */}
      <Card
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        className="af-glass"
        styles={{ body: { padding: 22 } }}
        style={{
          marginBottom: 16,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          background: "var(--af-gradient-soft)",
        }}
      >
        <Row gutter={[24, 16]} align="middle">
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Col xs={24} md={8} style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
            <Progress
              type="dashboard"
              percent={Math.min(100, Math.round(overall))}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              size={150}
              strokeColor={{ "0%": "#38bdf8", "100%": "#818cf8" }}
              format={() => (
                <div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 32, fontWeight: 800 }}>{Math.round(overall)}</div>
                  <div style={{ fontSize: 12, color: "var(--af-text-muted)" }}>综合分</div>
                </div>
              )}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            />
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} md={16}>
            <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <Col xs={12} sm={6}>
                <Statistic title="用例数" value={summary?.total_suites ?? 0} />
              </Col>
              <Col xs={12} sm={6}>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                <Statistic title="轨迹数" value={summary?.total_traces ?? 0} />
              </Col>
              <Col xs={12} sm={6}>
                <Statistic title="通过率" value={passRate} suffix="%" />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              </Col>
              <Col xs={12} sm={6}>
                <Statistic title="总 Tokens" value={summary?.total_tokens ?? 0} />
              </Col>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            </Row>
            <Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: "16px 0 0" }}>
              成功 {summary?.success_count ?? 0} · 失败 {summary?.failed_count ?? 0} · 均耗时{" "}
              {summary?.avg_time_per_trace_ms ?? 0}ms · 总耗时 {summary?.total_time_ms ?? 0}ms
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            </Paragraph>
          </Col>
        </Row>
      </Card>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      {chartData.length > 0 && (
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
            <Card className="af-glass" title="维度雷达图" styles={{ body: { height: 320 } }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <ResponsiveContainer width="100%" height="100%">
                <RadarChart data={chartData}>
                  <PolarGrid stroke="var(--af-border-strong)" />
                  <PolarAngleAxis
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    dataKey="dimension"
                    tick={{ fill: "var(--af-text-secondary)", fontSize: 12 }}
                  />
                  <PolarRadiusAxis domain={[0, 100]} tick={false} axisLine={false} />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  <Radar
                    dataKey="value"
                    stroke="#38bdf8"
                    fill="#818cf8"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    fillOpacity={0.35}
                    strokeWidth={2}
                  />
                </RadarChart>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              </ResponsiveContainer>
            </Card>
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            <Card className="af-glass" title="维度得分明细">
              {chartData.map((d, i) => (
                <div key={d.dimension} style={{ marginBottom: 18 }}>
                  <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 6 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    <Text>{d.dimension}</Text>
                    <Text strong style={{ color: DIM_COLORS[i % DIM_COLORS.length] }}>
                      {d.raw} / {d.max}
                    </Text>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  </div>
                  <Progress
                    percent={d.value}
                    showInfo={false}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    strokeColor={DIM_COLORS[i % DIM_COLORS.length]}
                    trailColor="var(--af-bg-muted)"
                    size="small"
                  />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
                    占满分 {d.value}%
                  </Text>
                </div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              ))}
            </Card>
          </Col>
        </Row>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      )}
      <Card className="af-glass" title="明细评分表" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        {tableData.length > 0 ? (
          <Table
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            dataSource={tableData}
            columns={columns}
            rowKey="key"
            pagination={false}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            size="middle"
            scroll={{ x: 960 }}
          />
        ) : (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Empty description="暂无评分明细" image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} />
        )}
      </Card>
      {summary && (
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        <Card className="af-glass" title="汇总信息">
          <Descriptions column={{ xs: 1, sm: 2, md: 3 }} size="small">
            <Descriptions.Item label="任务状态">
              <Tag>{report.task?.status}</Tag>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            </Descriptions.Item>
            <Descriptions.Item label="成功轨迹">{summary.success_count}</Descriptions.Item>
            <Descriptions.Item label="失败轨迹">{summary.failed_count}</Descriptions.Item>
            <Descriptions.Item label="平均耗时">{summary.avg_time_per_trace_ms}ms</Descriptions.Item>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            <Descriptions.Item label="总耗时">{summary.total_time_ms}ms</Descriptions.Item>
            <Descriptions.Item label="总 Tokens">{summary.total_tokens}</Descriptions.Item>
          </Descriptions>
        </Card>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

/* Reports list — card style */
import { useState } from "react";

import {

  PlusOutlined,
} from "@ant-design/icons";

// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求

const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
const STATUS_OPTIONS = [
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  { value: "completed", label: "已完成" },
  { value: "failed", label: "失败" },
  { value: "cancelled", label: "已取消" },
  { value: "timeout", label: "超时" },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  { value: "", label: "全部终态" },
];
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export default function ReportsPage() {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("completed");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useTasks({
    page,
    page_size: 12,
    status: status || undefined,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  });
  const terminal = new Set(["completed", "failed", "cancelled", "timeout"]);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const items = (data?.items ?? []).filter((t) => (status ? true : terminal.has(t.status)));
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  if (error) {
    return (
      <Alert
        type="error"
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        message="加载报告列表失败"
        description={(error as Error).message}
        showIcon
        action={
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <Button size="small" onClick={() => refetch()}>
            重试
          </Button>
        }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      />
    );
  }
  return (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    <div className="af-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={t("reports.title")}
        subtitle={t("reports.subtitle")}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        icon={<BarChartOutlined />}
        extra={
          <Space>
            <Select
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              style={{ width: 140 }}
              value={status}
              options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
              onChange={(v) => {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                setStatus(v);
                setPage(1);
              }}
            />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            <Button icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={() => refetch()}>
              {t("common.refresh")}
            </Button>
          </Space>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        }
      />
      {isLoading ? (
        <PageSkeleton variant="cards" rows={6} />
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={t("reports.empty")}
          description={t("reports.emptyDesc")}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          action={
            <Space>
              <Button type="primary" icon={<PlusOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate("/tasks/create")}>
                {t("tasks.create")}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              </Button>
              <Button onClick={() => navigate("/tasks")}>{t("dashboard.allTasks")}</Button>
            </Space>
          }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        />
      ) : (
        <>
          <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            {items.map((record: Task) => (
              <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={8} key={record.id}>
                <Card
                  className="af-glass af-card-hover"
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  styles={{ body: { padding: 18 } }}
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/reports/${record.id}`)}
                  style={{ cursor: "pointer", height: "100%" }}
                >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  <Space style={{ marginBottom: 12 }} wrap>
                    <FileTextOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-primary)" }} />
                    <StatusBadge status={record.status} />
                    <OwnerBadge owner={record.created_by} />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    {record.is_archived && <Tag>已归档</Tag>}
                  </Space>
                  <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16, display: "block", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                    {record.name}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  </Text>
                  <Paragraph type="secondary" ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }} style={{ minHeight: 40 }}>
                    {record.description || "无描述"}
                  </Paragraph>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  <div
                    style={{
                      display: "flex",
                      justifyContent: "space-between",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      alignItems: "center",
                      marginTop: 12,
                      paddingTop: 12,
                      borderTop: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                    }}
                  >
                    <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                      {record.test_suite_count} 用例 · {formatDateTime(record.created_at)}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    </Text>
                    <Button
                      type="link"
                      size="small"
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                      icon={<EyeOutlined />}
                      onClick={(e) => {
                        e.stopPropagation();
                        navigate(`/reports/${record.id}`);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                      }}
                    >
                      {t("reports.view")}
                    </Button>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  </div>
                </Card>
              </Col>
            ))}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          </Row>
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 20 }}>
            <Pagination
              current={page}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              pageSize={12}
              total={data?.total ?? 0}
              onChange={setPage}
              showSizeChanger={false}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    </div>
  );
}

import { Controller, useForm } from "react-hook-form";

// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
const MODEL_OPTIONS = [
  { value: "gpt-4o", label: "GPT-4o", hint: "高质量通用" },
  { value: "gpt-4o-mini", label: "GPT-4o-mini", hint: "性价比首选" },
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  { value: "gpt-4-turbo", label: "GPT-4 Turbo", hint: "长上下文" },
  { value: "gpt-3.5-turbo", label: "GPT-3.5 Turbo", hint: "快速试跑" },
  { value: "claude-3-opus", label: "Claude 3 Opus", hint: "复杂推理" },
  { value: "claude-3-sonnet", label: "Claude 3 Sonnet", hint: "均衡表现" },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
];
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function tempLabel(t: number) {
  if (t <= 0.2) return "确定性强";
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  if (t <= 0.7) return "平衡";
  if (t <= 1.2) return "有创意";
  return "高随机";
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export default function CreateTaskPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const createTask = useCreateTask();
  const {
    control,
    handleSubmit,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    watch,
    setValue,
    reset,
    formState: { errors, isSubmitting },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  } = useForm<TaskCreateInput>({
    resolver: zodResolver(taskCreateSchema),
    defaultValues: {
      name: "",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      description: "",
      model: "gpt-4o",
      temperature: 0,
      max_tokens: 4096,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    },
  });
  const formValues = watch();
  const nameOk = Boolean(formValues.name?.trim());
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const step = nameOk ? 1 : 0;
  const payloadPreview = useMemo(() => {
    return JSON.stringify(
      {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        name: formValues.name || "",
        description: formValues.description || "",
        agent_config: {
          model: formValues.model || "gpt-4o",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          temperature: formValues.temperature ?? 0,
          max_tokens: formValues.max_tokens ?? 4096,
        },
      },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      null,
      2
    );
  }, [formValues]);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const fillSample = useCallback(() => {
    reset({
      name: "Customer Support Agent Evaluation",
      description:
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        "Evaluate the performance of a customer support agent on common business queries including weather,...
      model: "gpt-4o-mini",
      temperature: 0,
      max_tokens: 4096,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    });
  }, [reset]);
  const onSubmit = handleSubmit(async (data) => {
    try {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      const result = await createTask.mutateAsync({
        name: data.name,
        description: data.description || "",
        agent_config: {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          model: data.model || "gpt-4o",
          temperature: data.temperature ?? 0,
          max_tokens: data.max_tokens ?? 4096,
        },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      });
      message.success("任务创建成功");
      navigate(`/tasks/${result.id}`);
    } catch (err: any) {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      const msg = err?.response?.data?.error?.message || err?.message || "创建失败";
      message.error(msg);
    }
  });
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  return (
    <form onSubmit={onSubmit} className="af-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={t("create.title")}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        subtitle={t("create.subtitle")}
        icon={<ExperimentOutlined />}
        extra={
          <Space wrap>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            <Button icon={<ArrowLeftOutlined />} onClick={() => navigate("/tasks")}>
              {t("create.back")}
            </Button>
            <Button icon={<ClearOutlined />} onClick={fillSample}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              {t("create.sample")}
            </Button>
          </Space>
        }
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      />
      <Card className="af-glass" styles={{ body: { padding: "16px 20px" } }} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <Steps
          size="small"
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          current={step}
          items={[
            { title: "基本信息", description: "名称与描述" },
            { title: "模型参数", description: "Agent 配置" },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            { title: "创建", description: "提交并进入详情" },
          ]}
        />
      </Card>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      <Row gutter={[20, 20]}>
        <Col xs={24} lg={14}>
          <Card
            className="af-glass"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            title={
              <Space>
                <ThunderboltOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-primary)" }} />
                <span>基本信息</span>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              </Space>
            }
          >
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              <Text strong>
                任务名称 <Text type="danger">*</Text>
              </Text>
              {/* Ant Design Input 必须用 Controller，不能用 register，否则输入不会写入表单状态 */}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              <Controller
                name="name"
                control={control}
                render={({ field }) => (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  <Input
                    {...field}
                    value={field.value ?? ""}
                    placeholder="例如：客服 Agent 业务评测"
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    status={errors.name ? "error" : undefined}
                    maxLength={100}
                    showCount
                    size="large"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    style={{ marginTop: 6 }}
                  />
                )}
              />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              {errors.name && (
                <Text type="danger" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                  {errors.name.message === "Task name is required"
                    ? "任务名称是必需的"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    : errors.name.message}
                </Text>
              )}
            </div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
              <Text strong>描述</Text>
              <Controller
                name="description"
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                control={control}
                render={({ field }) => (
                  <TextArea
                    {...field}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                    value={field.value ?? ""}
                    rows={3}
                    placeholder="可选：评测目标、业务场景、关注指标等"
                    style={{ marginTop: 6 }}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  />
                )}
              />
            </div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          </Card>
          <Card
            className="af-glass"
            style={{ marginTop: 16 }}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            title={
              <Space>
                <span>Agent 配置</span>
                <Tag color="blue">{formValues.model}</Tag>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              </Space>
            }
          >
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              <Text strong style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 8 }}>
                模型
              </Text>
              <Select
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                value={formValues.model}
                onChange={(val) => setValue("model", val, { shouldValidate: true })}
                style={{ width: "100%" }}
                size="large"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                optionLabelProp="label"
                options={MODEL_OPTIONS.map((m) => ({
                  value: m.value,
                  label: m.label,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  m,
                }))}
                optionRender={(opt) => {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  const m = (opt.data as { m: (typeof MODEL_OPTIONS)[0] }).m;
                  return (
                    <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 12 }}>
                      <span>{m.label}</span>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                        {m.hint}
                      </Text>
                    </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  );
                }}
              />
            </div>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", marginBottom: 4 }}>
                <Text strong>
                  Temperature: {(formValues.temperature ?? 0).toFixed(1)}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                </Text>
                <Tag>{tempLabel(formValues.temperature ?? 0)}</Tag>
              </div>
              <Slider
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                min={0}
                max={2}
                step={0.1}
                value={formValues.temperature ?? 0}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                onChange={(val) => setValue("temperature", val, { shouldValidate: true })}
                marks={{ 0: "0", 0.5: "0.5", 1: "1.0", 1.5: "1.5", 2: "2.0" }}
              />
              <Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12, marginBottom: 0 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                评测场景通常建议 0–0.3，保证可复现性。
              </Paragraph>
            </div>
            <div style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              <Text strong style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 6 }}>
                Max Tokens
              </Text>
              <InputNumber
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                value={formValues.max_tokens ?? 4096}
                onChange={(val) =>
                  setValue("max_tokens", val ?? 4096, { shouldValidate: true })
                }
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                min={256}
                max={16384}
                step={256}
                style={{ width: "100%" }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                size="large"
              />
            </div>
          </Card>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <div className="af-form-actions af-no-print">
            <Space wrap>
              <Button
                type="primary"
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                htmlType="submit"
                icon={<PlusOutlined />}
                size="large"
                loading={createTask.isPending || isSubmitting}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
              >
                {t("create.submit")}
              </Button>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <Button size="large" onClick={() => navigate("/tasks")}>
                {t("create.cancel")}
              </Button>
            </Space>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          </div>
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} lg={10}>
          <Card
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            className="af-glass af-sticky-preview"
            title={
              <Space>
                <CodeOutlined />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <span>请求预览</span>
              </Space>
            }
            extra={
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              <Tag color="processing" style={{ margin: 0 }}>
                Live
              </Tag>
            }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          >
            <pre className="af-code-block">{payloadPreview}</pre>
            <Divider style={{ margin: "12px 0" }} />
            <Space direction="vertical" size={6} style={{ width: "100%" }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
                <Text type="secondary">模型</Text>
                <Text>{formValues.model}</Text>
              </div>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
                <Text type="secondary">Temperature</Text>
                <Text>{(formValues.temperature ?? 0).toFixed(1)}</Text>
              </div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between" }}>
                <Text type="secondary">Max Tokens</Text>
                <Text>{formValues.max_tokens ?? 4096}</Text>
              </div>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            </Space>
            <Alert
              message="右侧预览即为实际提交的 JSON 载荷。"
              type="info"
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              showIcon
              style={{ marginTop: 14, fontSize: 12, borderRadius: 10 }}
            />
          </Card>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </Col>
      </Row>
    </form>
  );
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
}

// [功能] 任务详情：状态进度、用例、轨迹与报告入口
import { useState } from "react";

// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥

  Tag,
  Button,
  Space,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  Spin,
  Alert,
  Collapse,
  Descriptions,

  DeleteOutlined,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求

  InboxOutlined,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥

  useTaskReport,

  useDeleteTask,
  useUploadTestSuites,

  useArchiveTask,
} from "@/hooks";

// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
function formatDate(date: string | null): string {
  if (!date) return "-";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  return new Date(date).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
}
const STATUS_COLORS: Record<string, string> = {
  created: "default",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  queued: "warning",
  running: "processing",
  waiting_tool: "warning",
  judging: "processing",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  completed: "success",
  failed: "error",
  cancelled: "default",
  timeout: "error",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
};
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export default function TaskDetailPage() {
  const { id } = useParams<{ id: string }>();
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const [selectedTrace, setSelectedTrace] = useState<Trace | null>(null);
  const [traceLoading, setTraceLoading] = useState(false);
  const [uploadOpen, setUploadOpen] = useState(false);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const [traceView, setTraceView] = useState<"flow" | "timeline">("flow");
  // ---- Query task & report with polling when running ----
  // First load basic data to inspect status
  const tempTaskQuery = useTaskDetail(id, { enabled: !!id });
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const isRunning = tempTaskQuery.data && ["running", "queued", "judging"].includes(tempTaskQuery.data.stat...
  const taskQuery = useTaskDetail(id, {
    refetchInterval: isRunning ? 3000 : false,
  });
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const reportQuery = useTaskReport(id, {
    refetchInterval: isRunning ? 3000 : false,
  });
  const executeMutation = useExecuteTask();
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const cancelMutation = useCancelTask();
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteTask();
  const uploadMutation = useUploadTestSuites(id || "");
  const archiveMutation = useArchiveTask();
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const task = taskQuery.data;
  const report = reportQuery.data;
  // ---- Actions ----
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const handleRefresh = () => {
    taskQuery.refetch();
    reportQuery.refetch();
  };
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const handleExecute = () => {
    if (!id) return;
    executeMutation.mutate(id, {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      onSuccess: () => {
        message.success("Task submitted for execution");
        taskQuery.refetch();
      },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      onError: () => message.error("Failed to execute task"),
    });
  };
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const handleCancel = () => {
    if (!id) return;
    cancelMutation.mutate(id, {
      onSuccess: () => {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        message.success("Cancellation request submitted");
        taskQuery.refetch();
      },
      onError: () => message.error("Failed to cancel task"),
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    });
  };
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const handleDelete = () => {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    if (!id) return;
    Modal.confirm({
      title: "Delete Task",
      content: "Are you sure you want to delete this task? This action cannot be undone.",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      okText: "Delete",
      okType: "danger",
      onOk: () => {
        deleteMutation.mutate(id, {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          onSuccess: () => {
            message.success("Task deleted");
            navigate("/tasks");
          },
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          onError: () => message.error("Failed to delete task"),
        });
      },
    });
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const handleViewTrace = async (traceId: string) => {
    setTraceLoading(true);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    try {
      const trace = await traceApi.get(traceId);
      setSelectedTrace(trace);
    } catch {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      message.error("Failed to load trace");
    } finally {
      setTraceLoading(false);
    }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  };
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const handleUpload = (file: File) => {
    if (!id) return false;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    uploadMutation.mutate(file, {
      onSuccess: (res) => {
        message.success(res.message || `Imported ${res.created} suite(s)`);
        setUploadOpen(false);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        handleRefresh();
      },
      onError: (err: any) => {
        const msg =
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          err?.response?.data?.detail ||
          err?.response?.data?.error?.message ||
          err?.message ||
          "Upload failed";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        message.error(typeof msg === "string" ? msg : "Upload failed");
      },
    });
    return false;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  };
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const handleArchive = () => {
    if (!id) return;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    Modal.confirm({
      title: "Archive Task",
      content: "Archived tasks are hidden from the default list. Continue?",
      onOk: () =>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        archiveMutation.mutateAsync(id).then(() => {
          message.success("Task archived");
          navigate("/tasks");
        }),
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    });
  };
  // ---- Loading ----
  if (taskQuery.isLoading) {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    return <PageSkeleton variant="detail" />;
  }
  // ---- Error ----
  if (taskQuery.error || !task) {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    return (
      <Alert
        type="error"
        message="Failed to load task"
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        description={(taskQuery.error as Error)?.message || "Task not found"}
        showIcon
        action={<Button onClick={() => taskQuery.refetch()}>Retry</Button>}
      />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    );
  }
  // ---- Stats ----
  const totalSuites = report?.summary?.total_suites ?? task.test_suite_count ?? 0;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const executed = report?.summary?.total_traces ?? 0;
  const avgScore = report?.summary?.overall_score ?? 0;
  const passRate =
    report?.summary?.total_traces && report.summary.total_traces > 0
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      ? Math.round((report.summary.success_count / report.summary.total_traces) * 100)
      : 0;
  // ---- Collapse items ----
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const collapseItems = (report?.details || []).map((detail: { suite_id: string; user_query: string; expect...
    key: detail.suite_id || String(idx),
    label: (
      <Space>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        <Tag color={detail.traces?.[0]?.status === "success" ? "success" : "default"}>
          {detail.traces?.[0]?.status || "N/A"}
        </Tag>
        <Text ellipsis style={{ maxWidth: 400 }}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          {detail.user_query.slice(0, 60)}...
        </Text>
        <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
          Score:{" "}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          {detail.traces?.[0]?.scores
            ? (Object.values(detail.traces[0].scores) as number[]).reduce((a: number, b: number) => a + b, 0)
            : "-"}
        </Text>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      </Space>
    ),
    children: (
      <div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        <Descriptions size="small" column={2} style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
          <Descriptions.Item label="Query">{detail.user_query}</Descriptions.Item>
          <Descriptions.Item label="Expected Output">{detail.expected_output || "-"}</Descriptions.Item>
          <Descriptions.Item label="Expected Tools">
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            {detail.expected_tools?.join(", ") || "-"}
          </Descriptions.Item>
        </Descriptions>
        {(detail.traces || []).map((t: { trace_id: string; status: string; total_tokens: number; response_t...
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <Row key={t.trace_id} gutter={8} style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}>
            <Col span={3}>
              <Tag color={t.status === "success" ? "success" : "error"}>{t.status}</Tag>
            </Col>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            <Col span={3}>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{t.total_tokens} tokens</Text>
            </Col>
            <Col span={3}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{t.response_time_ms}ms</Text>
            </Col>
            <Col span={5}>
              <Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                Score:{" "}
                {(Object.values(t.scores || {}) as number[]).reduce((a: number, b: number) => a + b, 0)}
              </Text>
            </Col>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            <Col span={4}>
              <Button
                size="small"
                icon={<EyeOutlined />}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                onClick={() => handleViewTrace(t.trace_id)}
              >
                View Flow
              </Button>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            </Col>
          </Row>
        ))}
      </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    ),
  }));
  return (
    <div className="af-page">
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      {/* ---- Header ---- */}
      <Card
        className="af-glass"
        styles={{ body: { padding: 20 } }}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
      >
        <Row justify="space-between" align="middle" gutter={[12, 12]}>
          <Col flex="auto">
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            <Space size="middle" wrap>
              <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 20, letterSpacing: "-0.02em" }}>
                {task.name}
              </Text>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              <Tag color={STATUS_COLORS[task.status] || "default"}>{task.status}</Tag>
              <Tag color={task.created_by === "admin" ? "gold" : "processing"}>
                Owner: {task.created_by || "anonymous"}
              </Tag>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              {isRunning && (
                <Space size={6}>
                  <span className="af-live-dot" />
                  <Spin size="small" />
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                </Space>
              )}
              <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                Created: {formatDate(task.created_at)}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              </Text>
            </Space>
            {task.description && (
              <Text type="secondary" style={{ display: "block", marginTop: 8 }}>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                {task.description}
              </Text>
            )}
          </Col>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Col>
            <Space wrap>
              <Button icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={handleRefresh}>
                刷新
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              </Button>
              {task.status === "created" && (
                <Button icon={<UploadOutlined />} onClick={() => setUploadOpen(true)}>
                  导入用例
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                </Button>
              )}
              {isRunning ? (
                <Button
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  danger
                  icon={<CloseCircleOutlined />}
                  loading={cancelMutation.isPending}
                  onClick={handleCancel}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                >
                  取消
                </Button>
              ) : (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                <Button
                  type="primary"
                  icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />}
                  loading={executeMutation.isPending}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  onClick={handleExecute}
                  disabled={task.status !== "created"}
                  style={
                    task.status === "created"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      ? { background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }
                      : undefined
                  }
                >
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  执行
                </Button>
              )}
              {["completed", "failed", "cancelled", "timeout", "partial"].includes(task.status) && (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                <Button
                  icon={<BarChartOutlined />}
                  onClick={() => navigate(`/reports/${id}`)}
                >
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  报告
                </Button>
              )}
              {["completed", "failed", "cancelled", "timeout"].includes(task.status) &&
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                !task.is_archived && (
                  <Button loading={archiveMutation.isPending} onClick={handleArchive}>
                    归档
                  </Button>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                )}
              <Button danger icon={<DeleteOutlined />} onClick={handleDelete}>
                删除
              </Button>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            </Space>
          </Col>
        </Row>
      </Card>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      <Modal
        title="Import Test Suites (CSV / JSON)"
        open={uploadOpen}
        onCancel={() => setUploadOpen(false)}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        footer={null}
        destroyOnClose
      >
        <Alert
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          type="info"
          showIcon
          style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}
          message="CSV columns: user_query, expected_output, expected_tools"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          description="expected_tools can be comma/pipe-separated or a JSON array. JSON file should be an a...
        />
        <Upload.Dragger
          accept=".csv,.json,text/csv,application/json"
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          multiple={false}
          showUploadList={false}
          beforeUpload={handleUpload}
          disabled={uploadMutation.isPending}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        >
          <p className="ant-upload-drag-icon">
            <InboxOutlined />
          </p>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <p className="ant-upload-text">
            {uploadMutation.isPending ? "Uploading..." : "Click or drag file to this area"}
          </p>
          <p className="ant-upload-hint">Supports .csv and .json (UTF-8)</p>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </Upload.Dragger>
      </Modal>
      {/* ---- Stats Cards ---- */}
      <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        {[
          { title: "用例总数", value: totalSuites },
          { title: "已执行", value: `${executed} / ${totalSuites}` },
          { title: "平均分", value: avgScore, suffix: "/ 100" },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          { title: "通过率", value: passRate, suffix: "%" },
        ].map((s) => (
          <Col xs={12} md={6} key={s.title}>
            <Card className="af-glass" size="small" styles={{ body: { padding: 16 } }}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              <Statistic title={s.title} value={s.value as number | string} suffix={s.suffix} />
            </Card>
          </Col>
        ))}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      </Row>
      {/* ---- Test Suites (Collapse) ---- */}
      {report?.details && report.details.length > 0 && (
        <Card className="af-glass" title="测试用例" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <Collapse items={collapseItems} />
        </Card>
      )}
      {/* ---- Trace visualization ---- */}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      {selectedTrace && (
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          <Col xs={24} lg={16}>
            <Card
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              className="af-glass"
              title="执行轨迹"
              extra={
                <Space wrap>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                  <Segmented
                    size="small"
                    value={traceView}
                    onChange={(v) => setTraceView(v as "flow" | "timeline")}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    options={[
                      { label: "流程图", value: "flow" },
                      { label: "时间线", value: "timeline" },
                    ]}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  />
                  <Tag>Tokens: {selectedTrace.total_tokens}</Tag>
                  <Tag>{selectedTrace.response_time_ms}ms</Tag>
                  <Button size="small" onClick={() => setSelectedTrace(null)}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                    关闭
                  </Button>
                </Space>
              }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            >
              {traceLoading ? (
                <div style={{ textAlign: "center", padding: 40 }}>
                  <Spin />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                </div>
              ) : traceView === "flow" ? (
                <TraceFlowChart steps={selectedTrace.steps} />
              ) : (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                <StepLogPanel steps={selectedTrace.steps} />
              )}
            </Card>
          </Col>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Col xs={24} lg={8}>
            <ScoreCard metricScores={selectedTrace.metric_scores || []} />
            {traceView === "flow" && (
              <Card
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                className="af-glass"
                title="步骤摘要"
                size="small"
                style={{ marginTop: 16 }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                styles={{ body: { maxHeight: 320, overflow: "auto" } }}
              >
                <StepLogPanel steps={selectedTrace.steps} />
              </Card>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            )}
          </Col>
        </Row>
      )}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    </div>
  );
}
