function TaskCard({
  task,
  onView,
  onReport,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  onExecute,
  onDelete,
  executing,
  deleting,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
}: {
  task: Task;
  onView: () => void;
  onReport: () => void;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  onExecute: () => void;
  onDelete: () => void;
  executing?: boolean;
  deleting?: boolean;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
}) {
  const isLive = ["running", "queued", "judging"].includes(task.status);
  return (
    <Card
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      className="af-card-hover af-glass"
      styles={{ body: { padding: 18 } }}
      style={{ height: "100%", cursor: "pointer" }}
      onClick={onView}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    >
      <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "space-between", gap: 8, marginBottom: 12 }}>
        <Space size={6} wrap>
          <StatusBadge status={task.status} />
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          {isLive && <span className="af-live-dot" title="进行中" />}
        </Space>
        <OwnerBadge owner={task.created_by} />
      </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16, display: "block", marginBottom: 6, lineHeight: 1.35 }}>
        {task.name}
      </Text>
      <Paragraph
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        type="secondary"
        ellipsis={{ rows: 2 }}
        style={{ minHeight: 44, marginBottom: 14, fontSize: 13 }}
      >
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        {task.description || "暂无描述"}
      </Paragraph>
      <div
        style={{
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "space-between",
          alignItems: "center",
          padding: "10px 12px",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          borderRadius: 10,
          background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
          marginBottom: 14,
        }}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      >
        <div>
          <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11, display: "block" }}>
            用例数
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          </Text>
          <Text strong>{task.test_suite_count ?? 0}</Text>
        </div>
        <div style={{ textAlign: "right" }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11, display: "block" }}>
            创建时间
          </Text>
          <Text style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>{formatDateTime(task.created_at)}</Text>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        </div>
      </div>
      <Space size={6} wrap onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
        <Button size="small" icon={<EyeOutlined />} onClick={onView}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          详情
        </Button>
        {["completed", "partial", "failed"].includes(task.status) && (
          <Button size="small" icon={<BarChartOutlined />} onClick={onReport}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            报告
          </Button>
        )}
        {task.status === "created" && (
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <Button
            size="small"
            type="primary"
            ghost
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            icon={<PlayCircleOutlined />}
            loading={executing}
            onClick={onExecute}
          >
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            执行
          </Button>
        )}
        <Tooltip title="删除">
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Button
            size="small"
            danger
            icon={<DeleteOutlined />}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            loading={deleting}
            onClick={onDelete}
          />
        </Tooltip>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      </Space>
    </Card>
  );
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
const TASK_FILTER_KEY = "agentflow_task_filters";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function loadPersistedFilters(): { status?: string; view?: "card" | "table" } {
  try {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(TASK_FILTER_KEY);
    if (!raw) return {};
    return JSON.parse(raw) as { status?: string; view?: "card" | "table" };
  } catch {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    return {};
  }
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
function writeFiltersToUrl(
  setSearchParams: ReturnType<typeof useSearchParams>[1],
  searchParams: URLSearchParams,
  patch: { status?: string; view?: "card" | "table"; q?: string }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
) {
  const next = new URLSearchParams(searchParams);
  if (patch.status !== undefined) {
    if (patch.status) next.set("status", patch.status);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    else next.delete("status");
  }
  if (patch.view !== undefined) {
    if (patch.view && patch.view !== "card") next.set("view", patch.view);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    else next.delete("view");
  }
  if (patch.q !== undefined) {
    if (patch.q) next.set("q", patch.q);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    else next.delete("q");
  }
  if (next.toString() !== searchParams.toString()) {
    setSearchParams(next, { replace: true });
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  }
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(
      TASK_FILTER_KEY,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      JSON.stringify({
        status: next.get("status") || "",
        view: (next.get("view") as "card" | "table") || "card",
      })
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    );
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export default function TaskListPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
  const [searchParams, setSearchParams] = useSearchParams();
  const persisted = useMemo(() => loadPersistedFilters(), []);
  // URL is source of truth; hydrate once from localStorage if URL empty
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!searchParams.get("status") && !searchParams.get("view") && (persisted.status || persisted.view)) {
      writeFiltersToUrl(setSearchParams, searchParams, {
        status: persisted.status || "",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        view: persisted.view || "card",
      });
    }
    // only on mount
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, []);
  const status = searchParams.get("status") || "";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const view = (searchParams.get("view") as "card" | "table") || "card";
  const [page, setPage] = useState(1);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState(() => searchParams.get("q") || "");
  const [debouncedSearch, setDebouncedSearch] = useState(() => searchParams.get("q") || "");
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const timerRef = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  // Sync search box from URL (?q=)
  useEffect(() => {
    const q = searchParams.get("q") || "";
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    setSearch(q);
    setDebouncedSearch(q);
    setPage(1);
  }, [searchParams]);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  useEffect(() => {
    if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
    timerRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      setDebouncedSearch(search);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      setPage(1);
    }, 400);
    return () => {
      if (timerRef.current) clearTimeout(timerRef.current);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    };
  }, [search]);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const setStatus = (s: string) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    setPage(1);
    writeFiltersToUrl(setSearchParams, searchParams, { status: s, view });
  };
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const setView = (v: "card" | "table") => {
    writeFiltersToUrl(setSearchParams, searchParams, { status, view: v });
  };
  const { data, isLoading, error, refetch } = useTasks({
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    page,
    page_size: 12,
    status: status || undefined,
  });
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const executeMutation = useExecuteTask();
  const deleteMutation = useDeleteTask();
  const items = useMemo(() => {
    const list = data?.items || [];
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    if (!debouncedSearch.trim()) return list;
    const q = debouncedSearch.toLowerCase();
    return list.filter(
      (t) =>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        t.name.toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (t.description || "").toLowerCase().includes(q) ||
        (t.created_by || "").toLowerCase().includes(q)
    );
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  }, [data?.items, debouncedSearch]);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const handleExecute = (taskId: string) => {
    executeMutation.mutate(taskId, {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      onSuccess: () => {
        message.success("任务已提交执行");
        refetch();
      },
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      onError: () => message.error("执行失败"),
    });
  };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const handleDelete = (taskId: string) => {
    Modal.confirm({
      title: "删除任务",
      content: "此操作不可撤销，关联用例与轨迹将一并删除。",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      okText: "删除",
      okType: "danger",
      cancelText: "取消",
      onOk: () =>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        deleteMutation.mutateAsync(taskId).then(() => {
          message.success("已删除");
          refetch();
        }),
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    });
  };
  if (error) {
    return (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      <div className="af-page">
        <Alert
          type="error"
          showIcon
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          message="加载任务失败"
          description={(error as Error)?.message}
          action={
            <Button size="small" icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={() => refetch()}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              重试
            </Button>
          }
        />
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      </div>
    );
  }
  return (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    <div className="af-page">
      <PageHeader
        title={t("tasks.title")}
        subtitle={t("tasks.subtitle")}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        icon={<ExperimentOutlined />}
        extra={
          <Space wrap>
            <Button icon={<ReloadOutlined />} onClick={() => refetch()}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              {t("common.refresh")}
            </Button>
            <Button
              type="primary"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              icon={<PlusOutlined />}
              onClick={() => navigate("/tasks/create")}
              style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
            >
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              {t("tasks.create")}
            </Button>
          </Space>
        }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      />
      {/* Filters bar */}
      <Card
        className="af-glass"
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        styles={{ body: { padding: "14px 16px" } }}
        style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}
      >
        <Row gutter={[12, 12]} align="middle">
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Col xs={24} md={8}>
            <Input
              allowClear
              prefix={<SearchOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-text-muted)" }} />}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              placeholder={t("tasks.search")}
              value={search}
              onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
              size="large"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            />
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} md={10}>
            <Segmented
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              block
              value={status || ""}
              onChange={(v) => {
                setStatus(String(v));
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                setPage(1);
              }}
              options={QUICK_FILTERS}
            />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} md={6} style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", gap: 8 }}>
            <Select
              value={status}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              onChange={(v) => {
                setStatus(v);
                setPage(1);
              }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              options={STATUS_OPTIONS}
              style={{ minWidth: 120, flex: 1 }}
            />
            <Segmented
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              value={view}
              onChange={(v) => setView(v as "card" | "table")}
              options={[
                { value: "card", icon: <AppstoreOutlined /> },
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                { value: "table", icon: <UnorderedListOutlined /> },
              ]}
            />
          </Col>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </Row>
      </Card>
      {isLoading ? (
        <PageSkeleton variant="cards" rows={6} />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      ) : items.length === 0 ? (
        <EmptyState
          title={t("tasks.empty")}
          description={status || debouncedSearch ? "试试调整筛选条件" : "创建第一个评测任务开始吧"}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          actionLabel={t("tasks.create")}
          onAction={() => navigate("/tasks/create")}
        />
      ) : view === "card" ? (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        <>
          <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
            {items.map((task) => (
              <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={8} key={task.id}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <TaskCard
                  task={task}
                  onView={() => navigate(`/tasks/${task.id}`)}
                  onReport={() => navigate(`/reports/${task.id}`)}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  onExecute={() => handleExecute(task.id)}
                  onDelete={() => handleDelete(task.id)}
                  executing={executeMutation.isPending}
                  deleting={deleteMutation.isPending}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                />
              </Col>
            ))}
          </Row>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <div style={{ display: "flex", justifyContent: "flex-end", marginTop: 20 }}>
            <Pagination
              current={page}
              pageSize={12}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              total={data?.total || 0}
              onChange={setPage}
              showSizeChanger={false}
              showTotal={(t) => `共 ${t} 个任务`}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            />
          </div>
        </>
      ) : (
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        <Card className="af-glass" styles={{ body: { padding: 0 } }}>
          <div style={{ overflowX: "auto" }}>
            <table style={{ width: "100%", borderCollapse: "collapse" }}>
              <thead>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                <tr style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--af-border)", textAlign: "left" }}>
                  {["名称", "状态", "Owner", "用例", "创建", "操作"].map((h) => (
                    <th
                      key={h}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                      style={{
                        padding: "12px 16px",
                        color: "var(--af-text-secondary)",
                        fontWeight: 600,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                        fontSize: 12,
                        textTransform: "uppercase",
                        letterSpacing: "0.04em",
                      }}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    >
                      {h}
                    </th>
                  ))}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                {items.map((task) => (
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  <tr
                    key={task.id}
                    style={{ borderBottom: "1px solid var(--af-border)", cursor: "pointer" }}
                    onClick={() => navigate(`/tasks/${task.id}`)}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  >
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px" }}>
                      <Text strong>{task.name}</Text>
                    </td>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px" }}>
                      <StatusBadge status={task.status} />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px" }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                      <OwnerBadge owner={task.created_by} />
                    </td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px" }}>{task.test_suite_count}</td>
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px", whiteSpace: "nowrap" }}>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                      <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                        {formatDateTime(task.created_at)}
                      </Text>
                    </td>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    <td style={{ padding: "14px 16px" }} onClick={(e) => e.stopPropagation()}>
                      <Space size={4}>
                        <Button size="small" type="link" onClick={() => navigate(`/tasks/${task.id}`)}>
                          详情
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                        </Button>
                        {task.status === "created" && (
                          <Button size="small" type="link" onClick={() => handleExecute(task.id)}>
                            执行
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                          </Button>
                        )}
                      </Space>
                    </td>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  </tr>
                ))}
              </tbody>
            </table>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          </div>
          <div className="af-table-footer">
            <Pagination
              current={page}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              pageSize={12}
              total={data?.total || 0}
              onChange={setPage}
              showSizeChanger={false}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            />
          </div>
        </Card>
      )}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    </div>
  );
}

// [功能] frontend/src/hooks/useActivityWatcher.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
/* Live activity: WebSocket primary + polling fallback */
import { useEffect, useRef } from "react";
import { useQueryClient } from "@tanstack/react-query";
import { toast } from "sonner";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
import { taskApi } from "@/api/endpoints/tasks";
import { TASKS_QUERY_KEY } from "@/hooks/useTasks";
import { useNotificationStore, LIVE } from "@/stores/useNotificationStore";
import type { Task } from "@/types";
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
const SETTINGS_KEY = "agentflow_settings";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
function readPollIntervalMs(): number {
  try {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return 5000;
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { pollIntervalSec?: number };
    const sec = Number(parsed.pollIntervalSec) || 5;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    return Math.min(60, Math.max(1, sec)) * 1000;
  } catch {
    return 5000;
  }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
function readApiKey(): string {
  try {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return "";
    return ((JSON.parse(raw) as { apiKey?: string }).apiKey || "").trim();
  } catch {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    return "";
  }
}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function toastFor(status: string, message: string) {
  if (status === "completed") toast.success(message);
  else if (status === "failed" || status === "timeout") toast.error(message);
  else if (status === "cancelled") toast.message(message);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  else if (LIVE.has(status)) toast.info(message);
  else toast(message);
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function resolveWsUrl(): string {
  const base = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "/api/v1";
  // Absolute http(s) base → convert to ws(s)
  if (base.startsWith("http://") || base.startsWith("https://")) {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const u = new URL(base.replace(/\/$/, "") + "/ws/activities");
    u.protocol = u.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
    return u.toString();
  }
  // Relative → use current host
  const proto = window.location.protocol === "https:" ? "wss:" : "ws:";
  const path = base.endsWith("/")
    ? `${base}ws/activities`
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    : `${base}/ws/activities`;
  return `${proto}//${window.location.host}${path}`;
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
function eventToSyntheticTask(ev: {
  task_id: string;
  task_name: string;
  status: string;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  at?: string;
}): Task {
  return {
    id: ev.task_id,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    name: ev.task_name || ev.task_id,
    description: "",
    status: ev.status as Task["status"],
    agent_config: {},
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    celery_task_id: null,
    created_at: ev.at || new Date().toISOString(),
    updated_at: ev.at || new Date().toISOString(),
    test_suite_count: 0,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  };
}
/**
 * Mount once under MainLayout.
 * Prefer WebSocket; fall back to HTTP polling when disconnected.
 */
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export function useActivityWatcher(enabled = true) {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  const ingestTasks = useNotificationStore((s) => s.ingestTasks);
  const setTransport = useNotificationStore((s) => s.setTransport);
  const primed = useRef(false);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const wsRef = useRef<WebSocket | null>(null);
  const pollTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  const reconnectTimer = useRef<ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    if (!enabled) return;
    let cancelled = false;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    const applyTasks = (tasks: Task[], seedOnly: boolean) => {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      const fresh = ingestTasks(tasks, seedOnly);
      if (!seedOnly) {
        for (const ev of fresh) {
          if (ev.prevStatus) toastFor(ev.status, ev.message);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        }
        queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: TASKS_QUERY_KEY });
      }
    };
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    const pollOnce = async (seedOnly = false) => {
      try {
        const data = await taskApi.list({ page: 1, page_size: 50 });
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        if (cancelled) return;
        applyTasks(data.items || [], seedOnly);
      } catch {
        /* silent */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      }
    };
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    const schedulePoll = (delay?: number) => {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      if (pollTimer.current) clearTimeout(pollTimer.current);
      pollTimer.current = setTimeout(async () => {
        if (cancelled) return;
        // When WS is open, poll less aggressively as soft sync
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        const wsOpen = wsRef.current?.readyState === WebSocket.OPEN;
        await pollOnce(false);
        if (!cancelled) {
          schedulePoll(wsOpen ? Math.max(readPollIntervalMs() * 3, 15000) : readPollIntervalMs());
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        }
      }, delay ?? readPollIntervalMs());
    };
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    const connectWs = () => {
      if (cancelled) return;
      try {
        const url = resolveWsUrl();
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        const key = readApiKey();
        // API key as query if needed (WS can't always set headers)
        const full = key ? `${url}${url.includes("?") ? "&" : "?"}api_key=[REDACTED_API_KEY])}` : url;
        const ws = new WebSocket(full);
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        wsRef.current = ws;
        ws.onopen = () => {
          if (cancelled) return;
          setTransport("ws");
          // Prime once via HTTP so statusMap is warm
          if (!primed.current) {
            void pollOnce(true).then(() => {
              primed.current = true;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            });
          }
          // Soft poll backup while WS is up
          schedulePoll(20000);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        };
        ws.onmessage = (msg) => {
          try {
            const data = JSON.parse(msg.data as string) as {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              type?: string;
              task_id?: string;
              task_name?: string;
              status?: string;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              prev_status?: string;
              at?: string;
            };
            if (data.type === "ping") {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              ws.send(JSON.stringify({ type: "pong" }));
              return;
            }
            if (data.type === "hello" || data.type === "pong") return;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            if (data.type === "task_status" && data.task_id && data.status) {
              if (!primed.current) {
                // ensure map exists
                primed.current = true;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              }
              const synthetic = eventToSyntheticTask({
                task_id: data.task_id,
                task_name: data.task_name || data.task_id,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                status: data.status,
                at: data.at,
              });
              // Force prev status into map so ingest detects change
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              const store = useNotificationStore.getState();
              if (data.prev_status && !store.statusMap[data.task_id]) {
                useNotificationStore.setState({
                  statusMap: {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    ...store.statusMap,
                    [data.task_id]: data.prev_status,
                  },
                });
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              } else if (data.prev_status) {
                useNotificationStore.setState({
                  statusMap: {
                    ...store.statusMap,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    [data.task_id]: data.prev_status,
                  },
                });
              }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              applyTasks([synthetic], false);
            }
          } catch {
            /* ignore bad frames */
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          }
        };
        ws.onerror = () => {
          /* onclose will handle */
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        };
        ws.onclose = () => {
          wsRef.current = null;
          if (cancelled) return;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          setTransport("poll");
          // Fallback to faster polling
          if (!primed.current) {
            void pollOnce(true).then(() => {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              primed.current = true;
              schedulePoll(800);
            });
          } else {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            schedulePoll(1000);
          }
          reconnectTimer.current = setTimeout(connectWs, 4000);
        };
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      } catch {
        setTransport("poll");
        void pollOnce(!primed.current).then(() => {
          primed.current = true;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          schedulePoll();
        });
      }
    };
    // Start WS; poll as fallback
    connectWs();
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    const onVisibility = () => {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      if (document.visibilityState === "visible" && !cancelled) {
        void pollOnce(false);
      }
    };
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    document.addEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
    return () => {
      cancelled = true;
      document.removeEventListener("visibilitychange", onVisibility);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      if (pollTimer.current) clearTimeout(pollTimer.current);
      if (reconnectTimer.current) clearTimeout(reconnectTimer.current);
      try {
        wsRef.current?.close();
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      } catch {
        /* ignore */
      }
      wsRef.current = null;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    };
  }, [enabled, ingestTasks, queryClient, setTransport]);
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/hooks/useDocumentTitle.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/hooks/useDocumentTitle.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import { useEffect } from "react";
import { useLocation } from "react-router-dom";
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
const TITLE_MAP: Array<{ match: RegExp | string; title: string }> = [
  { match: /^\/$/, title: "总览" },
  { match: /^\/tasks\/create$/, title: "创建任务" },
  { match: /^\/tasks\/[^/]+$/, title: "任务详情" },
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  { match: /^\/tasks$/, title: "任务列表" },
  { match: /^\/reports\/[^/]+$/, title: "评测报告" },
  { match: /^\/reports$/, title: "报告中心" },
  { match: /^\/settings$/, title: "设置" },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  { match: /^\/404$/, title: "页面未找到" },
];
const APP_NAME = "AgentFlow-Eval";
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export function resolvePageTitle(pathname: string): string {
  for (const item of TITLE_MAP) {
    if (typeof item.match === "string" ? pathname === item.match : item.match.test(pathname)) {
      return `${item.title} · ${APP_NAME}`;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    }
  }
  return APP_NAME;
}
/** Call inside router tree to keep document.title in sync. */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export function useDocumentTitle(override?: string) {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  useEffect(() => {
    document.title = override || resolvePageTitle(pathname);
  }, [pathname, override]);
}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
export function DocumentTitle() {
  useDocumentTitle();
  return null;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
}

// === 模块：frontend/src/stores/useNotificationStore.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/stores/useNotificationStore.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
/* Global activity / notification store with status-change detection */
import { create } from "zustand";
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
import { persist } from "zustand/middleware";
import type { Task } from "@/types";
export interface ActivityEvent {
  id: string;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  taskId: string;
  taskName: string;
  status: string;
  prevStatus?: string;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  message: string;
  at: string;
  read: boolean;
}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
interface NotificationState {
  events: ActivityEvent[];
  /** last known status per task id (for change detection) */
  statusMap: Record<string, string>;
  /** last successful poll ISO time */
  lastPolledAt: string | null;
  /** transport mode for UI badge */
  transport: "ws" | "poll" | "idle";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  setTransport: (t: "ws" | "poll" | "idle") => void;
  /** @param seedOnly when true, only fill statusMap (no events / first poll) */
  ingestTasks: (tasks: Task[], seedOnly?: boolean) => ActivityEvent[];
  markRead: (id: string) => void;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  markAllRead: () => void;
  clear: () => void;
  unreadCount: () => number;
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
function headline(task: Task, prev?: string): string {
  const name = task.name;
  switch (task.status) {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    case "completed":
      return prev ? `任务「${name}」已完成` : `任务「${name}」已完成`;
    case "failed":
      return `任务「${name}」执行失败`;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    case "timeout":
      return `任务「${name}」超时`;
    case "cancelled":
      return `任务「${name}」已取消`;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    case "running":
      return prev === "queued" || prev === "created"
        ? `任务「${name}」开始运行`
        : `任务「${name}」进行中`;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    case "queued":
      return `任务「${name}」已入队`;
    case "judging":
      return `任务「${name}」正在评分`;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    case "created":
      return `新任务「${name}」待执行`;
    default:
      return `任务「${name}」状态 → ${task.status}`;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  }
}
const TERMINAL_TOAST = new Set(["completed", "failed", "timeout", "cancelled"]);
const LIVE = new Set(["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"]);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export const useNotificationStore = create<NotificationState>()(
  persist(
    (set, get) => ({
      events: [],
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      statusMap: {},
      lastPolledAt: null,
      transport: "idle",
      setTransport: (t) => set({ transport: t }),
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      ingestTasks: (tasks, seedOnly = false) => {
        const prevMap = { ...get().statusMap };
        const nextMap = { ...prevMap };
        const fresh: ActivityEvent[] = [];
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        const now = new Date().toISOString();
        for (const task of tasks) {
          const prev = prevMap[task.id];
          nextMap[task.id] = task.status;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          if (seedOnly || prev === undefined) {
            // first sighting or cold start — only snapshot, no flood
            continue;
          }
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          if (prev !== task.status) {
            const ev: ActivityEvent = {
              id: `${task.id}:${task.status}:${task.updated_at || now}`,
              taskId: task.id,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
              taskName: task.name,
              status: task.status,
              prevStatus: prev,
              message: headline(task, prev),
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              at: task.updated_at || now,
              read: false,
            };
            fresh.push(ev);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          }
        }
        if (fresh.length === 0) {
          set({ statusMap: nextMap, lastPolledAt: now });
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          return [];
        }
        set((state) => {
          const ids = new Set(state.events.map((e) => e.id));
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          const merged = [...fresh.filter((e) => !ids.has(e.id)), ...state.events].slice(
            0,
            80
          );
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          return {
            events: merged,
            statusMap: nextMap,
            lastPolledAt: now,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          };
        });
        return fresh;
      },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      markRead: (id) =>
        set((s) => ({
          events: s.events.map((e) => (e.id === id ? { ...e, read: true } : e)),
        })),
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      markAllRead: () =>
        set((s) => ({
          events: s.events.map((e) => ({ ...e, read: true })),
        })),
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      clear: () => set({ events: [] }),
      unreadCount: () => get().events.filter((e) => !e.read).length,
    }),
    {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      name: "agentflow_notifications",
      partialize: (s) => ({
        events: s.events.slice(0, 40),
        statusMap: s.statusMap,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        lastPolledAt: s.lastPolledAt,
        // transport is runtime-only
      }),
    }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  )
);
export { TERMINAL_TOAST, LIVE };
// === 模块：frontend/src/stores/useTaskStore.ts ===
// [功能] 任务前端状态：筛选条件与当前任务缓存
// Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
import { create } from "zustand";
import type { Task, TaskCreatePayload, TaskListResponse, TaskReport } from "../types";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
import { taskApi } from "@/api/endpoints/tasks";
interface TaskState {
  tasks: Task[];
  total: number;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  currentPage: number;
  loading: boolean;
  currentTask: Task | null;
  currentReport: TaskReport | null;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  fetchTasks: (page?: number, status?: string) => Promise<void>;
  createTask: (payload: TaskCreatePayload) => Promise<Task>;
  fetchTask: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  deleteTask: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  executeTask: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  fetchReport: (id: string) => Promise<void>;
  reset: () => void;
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
export const useTaskStore = create<TaskState>((set) => ({
  tasks: [],
  total: 0,
  currentPage: 1,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  loading: false,
  currentTask: null,
  currentReport: null,
  fetchTasks: async (page = 1, status?: string) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    set({ loading: true });
    try {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      const res = (await taskApi.list({
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        page,
        page_size: 20,
        status,
      })) as TaskListResponse;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      set({ tasks: res.items, total: res.total, currentPage: page });
    } finally {
      set({ loading: false });
    }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  },
  createTask: async (payload: TaskCreatePayload) => {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const task = (await taskApi.create(payload as never)) as Task;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    set((state) => ({ tasks: [task, ...state.tasks], total: state.total + 1 }));
    return task;
  },
  fetchTask: async (id: string) => {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    set({ loading: true });
    try {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      const task = (await taskApi.get(id)) as Task;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      set({ currentTask: task });
    } finally {
      set({ loading: false });
    }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  },
  deleteTask: async (id: string) => {
    await taskApi.delete(id);
    set((state) => ({
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      tasks: state.tasks.filter((t) => t.id !== id),
      total: state.total - 1,
    }));
  },
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  executeTask: async (id: string) => {
    await taskApi.execute(id);
    set((state) => ({
      tasks: state.tasks.map((t) =>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        t.id === id ? { ...t, status: "running" as const } : t,
      ),
    }));
  },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  fetchReport: async (id: string) => {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    const report = (await taskApi.getReport(id)) as TaskReport;
    set({ currentReport: report });
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  },
  reset: () => set({ tasks: [], total: 0, currentPage: 1, loading: false, currentTask: null, currentReport:...
}));
// === 模块：frontend/src/stores/useThemeStore.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/stores/useThemeStore.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import { create } from "zustand";
/** Available UI color themes */
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
export type ThemeMode =
  | "dark"
  | "light"
  | "midnight"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  | "emerald"
  | "ocean"
  | "sunset";
export const THEME_OPTIONS: ReadonlyArray<{
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  value: ThemeMode;
  label: string;
  labelEn: string;
  /** Whether Ant Design / OS should treat this as a dark scheme */
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  isDark: boolean;
  /** Swatch color for option previews */
  swatch: string;
}> = [
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  {
    value: "light",
    label: "明亮浅色（默认）",

    isDark: false,
    swatch: "#0284c7",
  },
  {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    value: "dark",

    labelEn: "Deep Space Dark",
    isDark: true,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    swatch: "#38bdf8",
  },
  {
    value: "midnight",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    label: "午夜紫",
    labelEn: "Midnight Violet",
    isDark: true,
    swatch: "#a78bfa",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  },

    value: "emerald",
    label: "翡翠绿",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    labelEn: "Emerald Night",
    isDark: true,
    swatch: "#34d399",
  },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  {
    value: "ocean",
    label: "深海蓝",
    labelEn: "Deep Ocean",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    isDark: true,
    swatch: "#22d3ee",
  },
  {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    value: "sunset",
    label: "落日暖橙",
    labelEn: "Sunset Warm",
    isDark: true,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    swatch: "#fb923c",
  },
] as const;
export const THEME_MODE_SET = new Set<string>(THEME_OPTIONS.map((o) => o.value));
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
export function isThemeMode(v: string | null | undefined): v is ThemeMode {
  return !!v && THEME_MODE_SET.has(v);
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export function isDarkTheme(mode: ThemeMode): boolean {
  return THEME_OPTIONS.find((o) => o.value === mode)?.isDark ?? true;
}
// v2: default switched from dark → light; new key so first open after upgrade uses light
const STORAGE_KEY = "agentflow_theme_v2";
const LAST_DARK_KEY = "agentflow_theme_last_dark";
/** App default when user has never chosen a theme */
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
export const DEFAULT_THEME: ThemeMode = "light";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function readInitial(): ThemeMode {
  try {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const v = localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY);
    if (isThemeMode(v)) return v;
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  }
  // First visit: always bright light (do not follow OS dark preference)
  return DEFAULT_THEME;
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
function readLastDark(): ThemeMode {
  try {
    const v = localStorage.getItem(LAST_DARK_KEY);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    if (isThemeMode(v) && isDarkTheme(v)) return v;
  } catch {
    /* ignore */
  }
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  return "dark";
}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
function applyDom(mode: ThemeMode) {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  document.documentElement.setAttribute("data-theme", mode);
}
interface ThemeState {
  mode: ThemeMode;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  setMode: (mode: ThemeMode) => void;
  /** Quick toggle between light and the last dark palette */
  toggle: () => void;
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export const useThemeStore = create<ThemeState>((set, get) => {
  const initial = typeof document !== "undefined" ? readInitial() : DEFAULT_THEME;
  if (typeof document !== "undefined") applyDom(initial);
  return {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    mode: initial,
    setMode: (mode) => {
      localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, mode);
      if (isDarkTheme(mode)) {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        try {
          localStorage.setItem(LAST_DARK_KEY, mode);
        } catch {
          /* ignore */
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        }
      }
      applyDom(mode);
      set({ mode });
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    },
    toggle: () => {
      const current = get().mode;
      if (isDarkTheme(current)) {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        get().setMode("light");
      } else {
        get().setMode(readLastDark());
      }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    },
  };
});
// === 模块：frontend/src/stores/useTraceStore.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/stores/useTraceStore.ts — 工作台前端模块

import { create } from "zustand";

import type { Trace, JudgeResult } from "../types";

// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
}
export const useTraceStore = create<TraceState>((set) => ({
  traces: [],
  total: 0,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写

  currentTrace: null,
  currentJudgeResult: null,
  fetchTraces: async (testSuiteId?: string) => {

// === 模块：frontend/src/api/client.ts ===
// [功能] HTTP 客户端：根地址、鉴权头、错误归一化
import axios from "axios";
import type { AxiosInstance } from "axios";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
const BASE_URL = import.meta.env.VITE_API_BASE_URL || "/api/v1";
const SETTINGS_KEY = "agentflow_settings";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
function readLocalApiKey(): string {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(SETTINGS_KEY);
    if (!raw) return "";
    const parsed = JSON.parse(raw) as { apiKey?: string };
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    return (parsed.apiKey || "").trim();
  } catch {
    return "";
  }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
}
const apiClient: AxiosInstance = axios.create({
  baseURL: BASE_URL,
  timeout: 30000,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
});
apiClient.interceptors.request.use((config) => {
  const key = readLocalApiKey();
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  if (key) {
    config.headers = config.headers || {};
    config.headers["X-API-Key"] = key;
  }
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  return config;
});
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
function extractErrorMessage(error: any): string {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const data = error?.response?.data;
  const status: number | undefined = error?.response?.status;
  // Structured backend error: { error: { message, detail } }
  const structured =
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    data?.error?.message ||
    (typeof data?.error?.detail === "string" ? data.error.detail : null);
  if (structured && typeof structured === "string") return structured;
  // FastAPI / Starlette style
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  if (typeof data?.detail === "string") return data.detail;
  if (Array.isArray(data?.detail)) {
    return data.detail
      .map((d: any) => d?.msg || d?.message || JSON.stringify(d))
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      .join("; ");
  }
  if (typeof data?.message === "string") return data.message;
  // Vite proxy returns 500 HTML/text when backend is down
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  if (status === 500 || status === 502 || status === 503 || status === 504) {
    const body = typeof data === "string" ? data : "";
    if (
      !data?.error &&
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      (body.includes("ECONNREFUSED") ||
        body.includes("proxy") ||
        body.includes("Error occurred while trying to proxy") ||
        !body)
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    ) {
      return "后端服务不可用（代理返回 " + status + "）。请先启动 API：https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]";
    }
    return `服务器错误 (${status})。请检查后端日志，或确认 API 已在 8000 端口运行。`;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  }
  if (!error?.response) {
    if (error?.code === "ECONNABORTED") return "请求超时，请稍后重试";
    return "无法连接后端，请确认 API 已启动（默认 https://[YOUR_DOMAIN]";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  }
  return error?.message || "Request failed";
}
apiClient.interceptors.response.use(
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  (response) => response,
  (error) => {
    const message = extractErrorMessage(error);
    // Reject with Error so React Query / UI show a clear message
    // instead of axios default "Request failed with status code 500"
    const enriched = new Error(message) as Error & {
      response?: unknown;
      status?: number;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      original?: unknown;
    };
    enriched.response = error?.response;
    enriched.status = error?.response?.status;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    enriched.original = error;
    return Promise.reject(enriched);
  },
);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
export { apiClient };

// === 模块：frontend/src/api/endpoints/tasks.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/api/endpoints/tasks.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import { apiClient } from "../client";
import type {
  Task,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  TaskCreate,
  PaginatedResponse,
  TaskReport,
  ExecuteTaskResponse,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  CreateTestSuitesResponse,
  CreateTestSuiteInput,
} from "@/types";
export const taskApi = {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  list: (params?: {
    page?: number;
    page_size?: number;
    status?: string;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    include_archived?: boolean;
  }) =>
    apiClient.get<PaginatedResponse<Task>>("/tasks", { params }).then((r) => r.data),
  get: (id: string) =>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    apiClient.get<Task>(`/tasks/${id}`).then((r) => r.data),
  create: (data: TaskCreate) =>
    apiClient.post<Task>("/tasks", data).then((r) => r.data),
  delete: (id: string) =>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    apiClient.delete(`/tasks/${id}`).then((r) => r.data),
  execute: (id: string) =>
    apiClient.post<ExecuteTaskResponse>(`/tasks/${id}/execute`).then((r) => r.data),
  createTestSuites: (taskId: string, suites: CreateTestSuiteInput[]) =>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    apiClient
      .post<CreateTestSuitesResponse>(`/tasks/${taskId}/test-suites`, suites)
      .then((r) => r.data),
  uploadTestSuites: (taskId: string, file: File) => {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const form = new FormData();
    form.append("file", file);
    return apiClient
      .post<CreateTestSuitesResponse & { filename?: string; message?: string }>(
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        `/tasks/${taskId}/test-suites/upload`,
        form,
        { headers: { "Content-Type": "multipart/form-data" } }
      )
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      .then((r) => r.data);
  },
  getReport: (id: string) =>
    apiClient.get<TaskReport>(`/reports/${id}`).then((r) => r.data),
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  cancel: (id: string) =>
    apiClient.post(`/tasks/${id}/cancel`).then((r) => r.data),
  archive: (id: string) =>
    apiClient.post(`/tasks/${id}/archive`).then((r) => r.data),
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  unarchive: (id: string) =>
    apiClient.post(`/tasks/${id}/unarchive`).then((r) => r.data),
};

// [功能] frontend/src/utils/exportPdf.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
/* Client-side PDF export with Chinese text support via html2canvas */
import { jsPDF } from "jspdf";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
import type { TaskReport } from "@/types";
const DIMENSION_LABELS: Record<string, string> = {
  tool_accuracy: "工具准确率",
  answer_correctness: "答案正确性",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  reasoning_coherence: "推理连贯性",
};
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function escapeHtml(s: string) {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  return String(s)
    .replace(/&/g, "&amp;")
    .replace(/</g, "&lt;")
    .replace(/>/g, "&gt;")
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    .replace(/"/g, "&quot;");
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
function buildReportHtml(report: TaskReport): string {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const task = report.task;
  const summary = report.summary;
  const passRate =
    summary && summary.total_traces > 0
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      ? Math.round((summary.success_count / summary.total_traces) * 100)
      : null;
  const dimRows = Object.entries(summary?.dimension_scores || {})
    .map(
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      ([k, v]) =>
        `<tr><td>${escapeHtml(DIMENSION_LABELS[k] || k)}</td><td>${escapeHtml(String(v))}</td></tr>`
    )
    .join("");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const detailRows: string[] = [];
  for (const d of report.details || []) {
    for (const t of d.traces || []) {
      const scores = t.scores || {};
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      const total = Object.values(scores).reduce((a, b) => a + Number(b || 0), 0);
      detailRows.push(
        `<tr>
          <td>${escapeHtml((d.user_query || "").slice(0, 80))}</td>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <td>${escapeHtml(t.status || "")}</td>
          <td>${scores.tool_accuracy ?? ""}</td>
          <td>${scores.answer_correctness ?? ""}</td>
          <td>${scores.reasoning_coherence ?? ""}</td>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <td>${Math.round(total)}</td>
          <td>${t.total_tokens ?? ""}</td>
          <td>${t.response_time_ms ?? ""}</td>
        </tr>`
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      );
    }
  }
  return `
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  <div id="af-pdf-root" style="
    width: 794px;
    padding: 36px 40px;
    background: #fff;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    color: #111;
    font-family: 'Microsoft YaHei','PingFang SC','Noto Sans SC',system-ui,sans-serif;
    font-size: 12px;
    line-height: 1.5;
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    box-sizing: border-box;
  ">
    <div style="font-size: 20px; font-weight: 700; margin-bottom: 4px; color: #0ea5e9;">
      AgentFlow-Eval 评测报告
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    </div>
    <div style="font-size: 16px; font-weight: 600; margin-bottom: 12px;">
      ${escapeHtml(task?.name || task?.id || "未命名任务")}
    </div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    <div style="color: #555; margin-bottom: 18px;">
      <div>任务 ID：${escapeHtml(task?.id || "-")}</div>
      <div>状态：${escapeHtml(task?.status || "-")}</div>
      <div>生成时间：${escapeHtml(new Date().toLocaleString())}</div>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    </div>
    <div style="font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; margin: 16px 0 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #0ea5e9; pa...
      汇总
    </div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    <table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;">
      <tr><td style="padding:4px 0;color:#555;">综合得分</td><td>${summary?.overall_score ?? "-"}</td></tr>
      <tr><td style="padding:4px 0;color:#555;">成功 / 总 Trace</td>
        <td>${summary?.success_count ?? "-"} / ${summary?.total_traces ?? "-"}${
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          passRate != null ? `（${passRate}%）` : ""
        }</td></tr>
      <tr><td style="padding:4px 0;color:#555;">用例数</td><td>${summary?.total_suites ?? report.details?.leng...
      <tr><td style="padding:4px 0;color:#555;">总 Tokens</td><td>${summary?.total_tokens ?? "-"}</td></tr>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      <tr><td style="padding:4px 0;color:#555;">总耗时</td><td>${
        summary?.total_time_ms != null ? `${summary.total_time_ms} ms` : "-"
      }</td></tr>
    </table>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    ${
      dimRows
        ? `<div style="font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; margin: 16px 0 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #636...
    <table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; margin-bottom: 12px;">
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      <thead><tr style="background:#eef2ff;">
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:6px;border:1px solid #ddd;">维度</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:6px;border:1px solid #ddd;">得分</th>
      </tr></thead>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      <tbody>${dimRows}</tbody>
    </table>`
        : ""
    }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    ${
      detailRows.length
        ? `<div style="font-size: 14px; font-weight: 700; margin: 16px 0 8px; border-bottom: 2px solid #636...
    <table style="width:100%; border-collapse: collapse; font-size: 11px;">
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      <thead><tr style="background:#eef2ff;">
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">用例</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">状态</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">工具</th>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">答案</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">推理</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">总分</th>
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">Tokens</th>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        <th style="text-align:left;padding:5px;border:1px solid #ddd;">ms</th>
      </tr></thead>
      <tbody>${detailRows.join("")}</tbody>
    </table>`
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        : ""
    }
    <div style="margin-top: 24px; color: #999; font-size: 10px;">
      Generated by AgentFlow-Eval · ${escapeHtml(new Date().toISOString())}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    </div>
  </div>`;
}
/**
 * Export report as multi-page A4 PDF with full Chinese rendering
 * (DOM + system CJK fonts → canvas → jsPDF images).
 */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export async function exportReportPdf(report: TaskReport, filename?: string) {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const html2canvas = (await import("html2canvas")).default;
  const host = document.createElement("div");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  host.style.cssText =
    "position:fixed;left:-10000px;top:0;z-index:-1;pointer-events:none;background:#fff;";
  host.innerHTML = buildReportHtml(report);
  document.body.appendChild(host);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  try {
    const root = host.querySelector("#af-pdf-root") as HTMLElement;
    const canvas = await html2canvas(root, {
      scale: 2,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      useCORS: true,
      backgroundColor: "#ffffff",
      logging: false,
    });
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    const pdf = new jsPDF({ unit: "pt", format: "a4", orientation: "portrait" });
    const pageWidth = pdf.internal.pageSize.getWidth();
    const pageHeight = pdf.internal.pageSize.getHeight();
    const margin = 24;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    const imgWidth = pageWidth - margin * 2;
    // Slice canvas into pages
    const pageCanvas = document.createElement("canvas");
    const pageCtx = pageCanvas.getContext("2d")!;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    const sliceHeightPx = Math.floor((pageHeight - margin * 2) * (canvas.width / imgWidth));
    pageCanvas.width = canvas.width;
    let offsetY = 0;
    let pageIndex = 0;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    while (offsetY < canvas.height) {
      const h = Math.min(sliceHeightPx, canvas.height - offsetY);
      pageCanvas.height = h;
      pageCtx.clearRect(0, 0, pageCanvas.width, h);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      pageCtx.drawImage(canvas, 0, offsetY, canvas.width, h, 0, 0, canvas.width, h);
      const imgData = pageCanvas.toDataURL("image/jpeg", 0.92);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      const sliceDisplayHeight = (h * imgWidth) / canvas.width;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      if (pageIndex > 0) pdf.addPage();
      pdf.addImage(imgData, "JPEG", margin, margin, imgWidth, sliceDisplayHeight);
      offsetY += h;
      pageIndex += 1;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    }
    const name = filename || `report-${report.task?.id || "export"}.pdf`;
    pdf.save(name);
  } finally {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    document.body.removeChild(host);
  }
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/utils/format.ts ===
// [功能] frontend/src/utils/format.ts — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
// Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
import dayjs from "dayjs";
/** 格式化日期时间 */
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export function formatDateTime(date: string | null | undefined): string {
  if (!date) return "-";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  return dayjs(date).format("YYYY-MM-DD HH:mm:ss");
}
/** 格式化 Token 数量 */
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
export function formatTokens(count: number): string {
  if (count >= 1000) {
    return `${(count / 1000).toFixed(1)}k`;
  }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  return String(count);
}
/** 格式化响应时间 */
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export function formatDuration(ms: number): string {
  if (ms >= 1000) {
    return `${(ms / 1000).toFixed(1)}s`;
  }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  return `${ms}ms`;
}
/** 获取状态对应的颜色 */
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
export function getStatusColor(status: string): string {
  const map: Record<string, string> = {
    pending: "default",
    created: "default",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    queued: "warning",
    running: "processing",
    waiting_tool: "warning",
    judging: "processing",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    completed: "success",
    failed: "error",
    cancelled: "default",
    timeout: "error",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    success: "success",
  };
  return map[status] || "default";
}

  Component: React.LazyExoticComponent<React.ComponentType>,
  variant: SkeletonVariant = "cards"
) => (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败

  </Suspense>

// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export function CommandPalette() {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false);
  const [q, setQ] = useState("");
  const [active, setActive] = useState(0);
  const [showHelp, setShowHelp] = useState(false);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { mode, toggle } = useThemeStore();
  const { data } = useTasks({ page: 1, page_size: 20 });
  const openWith = useCallback((query = "") => {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    setQ(query);
    setActive(0);
    setShowHelp(false);
    setOpen(true);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  }, []);
  useEffect(() => {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      if ((e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey) && e.key.toLowerCase() === "k") {
        e.preventDefault();
        setOpen((v) => {
          if (!v) {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            setQ("");
            setActive(0);
            setShowHelp(false);
          }
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          return !v;
        });
      }
    };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const onOpen = (e: Event) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      const detail = (e as CustomEvent<{ query?: string }>).detail;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      openWith(detail?.query ?? "");
    };
    window.addEventListener("keydown", onKey);
    window.addEventListener(OPEN_COMMAND_PALETTE, onOpen);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener("keydown", onKey);
      window.removeEventListener(OPEN_COMMAND_PALETTE, onOpen);
    };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  }, [openWith]);
  const actionCommands = useMemo<CommandItem[]>(
    () => [
      {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        key: "act-theme",
        label: isDarkTheme(mode) ? "切换到亮色主题" : "切换到深色主题",
        icon: isDarkTheme(mode) ? <SunOutlined /> : <MoonOutlined />,
        group: "操作",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        keywords: "theme dark light 主题 暗色 亮色 midnight emerald ocean sunset",
        action: () => toggle(),
      },
      {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        key: "act-help",
        label: "查看键盘快捷键",
        icon: <QuestionCircleOutlined />,
        group: "操作",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        keywords: "help shortcuts 快捷键 帮助",
        action: () => setShowHelp(true),
      },
    ],
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    [mode, toggle]
  );
  const taskCommands = useMemo<CommandItem[]>(() => {
    return (data?.items ?? []).slice(0, 12).map((t) => ({
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      key: `task-${t.id}`,
      label: t.name,
      icon: <ExperimentOutlined />,
      group: "最近任务",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      path: `/tasks/${t.id}`,
      keywords: `${t.status} ${t.created_by ?? ""} ${t.id}`,
    }));
  }, [data?.items]);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const reportCommands = useMemo<CommandItem[]>(() => {
    return (data?.items ?? [])
      .filter((t) => ["completed", "failed", "timeout", "cancelled"].includes(t.status))
      .slice(0, 6)
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      .map((t) => ({
        key: `report-${t.id}`,
        label: `报告 · ${t.name}`,
        icon: <FileTextOutlined />,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        group: "报告",
        path: `/reports/${t.id}`,
        keywords: `report ${t.status} ${t.id}`,
      }));
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  }, [data?.items]);
  const allCommands = useMemo(
    () => [...NAV_COMMANDS, ...actionCommands, ...taskCommands, ...reportCommands],
    [actionCommands, taskCommands, reportCommands]
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  );
  const filtered = useMemo(() => {
    const s = q.trim().toLowerCase();
    if (!s) return allCommands;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    return allCommands.filter((c) => {
      const hay = `${c.label} ${c.key} ${c.path ?? ""} ${c.group} ${c.keywords ?? ""}`.toLowerCase();
      return hay.includes(s);
    });
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  }, [allCommands, q]);
  useEffect(() => {
    setActive(0);
  }, [q, open]);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  const run = useCallback(
    (item: CommandItem) => {
      if (item.action) {
        item.action();
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        if (item.key !== "act-help") setOpen(false);
        return;
      }
      if (item.path) {
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        setOpen(false);
        navigate(item.path);
      }
    },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    [navigate]
  );
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const onListKeyDown = (e: React.KeyboardEvent) => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    if (!filtered.length) return;
    if (e.key === "ArrowDown") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActive((i) => (i + 1) % filtered.length);
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    } else if (e.key === "ArrowUp") {
      e.preventDefault();
      setActive((i) => (i - 1 + filtered.length) % filtered.length);
    } else if (e.key === "Enter") {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      e.preventDefault();
      run(filtered[active]);
    }
  };
  // Group labels for visual sectioning (preserve order of first appearance)
  const groups = useMemo(() => {
    const order: string[] = [];
    const map = new Map<string, CommandItem[]>();
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    filtered.forEach((c) => {
      if (!map.has(c.group)) {
        map.set(c.group, []);
        order.push(c.group);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      }
      map.get(c.group)!.push(c);
    });
    return order.map((g) => ({ group: g, items: map.get(g)! }));
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  }, [filtered]);
  let flatIndex = -1;
  return (
    <Modal
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      open={open}
      onCancel={() => setOpen(false)}
      footer={null}
      closable={false}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      width={560}
      styles={{
        content: {
          padding: 12,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          borderRadius: 16,
          background: "var(--af-bg-surface)",
          border: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
          boxShadow: "var(--af-shadow-md)",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        },
      }}
      destroyOnClose
    >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      <Input
        autoFocus
        size="large"
        prefix={<SearchOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-text-muted)" }} />}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        placeholder="搜索页面、任务、报告或操作…"
        value={q}
        onChange={(e) => setQ(e.target.value)}
        onKeyDown={onListKeyDown}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
        suffix={<span className="af-kbd">Esc</span>}
      />
      {showHelp ? (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        <div style={{ padding: "8px 4px 4px" }}>
          <Text strong style={{ display: "block", marginBottom: 10 }}>
            键盘快捷键
          </Text>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          {SHORTCUTS.map((s) => (
            <div
              key={s.keys}
              style={{
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                display: "flex",
                justifyContent: "space-between",
                alignItems: "center",
                padding: "8px 4px",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                borderBottom: "1px solid var(--af-border)",
              }}
            >
              <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                {s.desc}
              </Text>
              <span className="af-kbd">{s.keys}</span>
            </div>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          ))}
          <div style={{ marginTop: 12, textAlign: "right" }}>
            <Text
              style={{ color: "var(--af-primary)", cursor: "pointer", fontSize: 13 }}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              onClick={() => setShowHelp(false)}
            >
              返回命令列表
            </Text>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <div style={{ maxHeight: 380, overflow: "auto" }}>
            {groups.length === 0 ? (
              <div style={{ padding: 24, textAlign: "center" }}>
                <Text type="secondary">无匹配结果</Text>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              </div>
            ) : (
              groups.map(({ group, items }) => (
                <div key={group} style={{ marginBottom: 6 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  <Text
                    type="secondary"
                    style={{
                      fontSize: 11,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                      letterSpacing: "0.04em",
                      textTransform: "uppercase",
                      padding: "6px 10px 4px",
                      display: "block",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                    }}
                  >
                    {group}
                  </Text>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                  <List
                    size="small"
                    dataSource={items}
                    split={false}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                    renderItem={(item) => {
                      flatIndex += 1;
                      const idx = flatIndex;
                      const selected = idx === active;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                      return (
                        <List.Item
                          style={{
                            cursor: "pointer",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                            borderRadius: 10,
                            padding: "10px 12px",
                            border: "none",
                            background: selected ? "var(--af-primary-soft)" : "transparent",
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                            outline: selected ? "1px solid var(--af-border-strong)" : "none",
                          }}
                          className="af-card-hover"
                          onClick={() => run(item)}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                          onMouseEnter={() => setActive(idx)}
                        >
                          <List.Item.Meta
                            avatar={
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                              <span style={{ color: "var(--af-primary)", fontSize: 16 }}>
                                {item.icon}
                              </span>
                            }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                            title={<Text>{item.label}</Text>}
                            description={
                              item.path ? (
                                <Tag style={{ marginTop: 2, fontSize: 11 }}>{item.path}</Tag>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                              ) : (
                                <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 12 }}>
                                  操作
                                </Text>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                              )
                            }
                          />
                        </List.Item>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                      );
                    }}
                  />
                </div>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              ))
            )}
          </div>
          <Divider style={{ margin: "8px 0" }} />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <div
            style={{
              display: "flex",
              justifyContent: "space-between",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              alignItems: "center",
              padding: "0 4px",
            }}
          >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            <Space size={8}>
              <span className="af-kbd">↑↓</span>
              <span className="af-kbd">Enter</span>
              <Text
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                type="secondary"
                style={{ fontSize: 11, cursor: "pointer" }}
                onClick={() => setShowHelp(true)}
              >
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                快捷键
              </Text>
            </Space>
            <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              Ctrl / ⌘ + K
            </Text>
          </div>
        </>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/common/ErrorBoundary.tsx ===
// [功能] frontend/src/components/common/ErrorBoundary.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
// Copyright 2026 AgentFlow-Eval
/* Global error boundary — Deep Space styled */
import React from "react";
import { Button, Result, Typography, Space } from "antd";
import { ReloadOutlined, HomeOutlined } from "@ant-design/icons";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
const { Paragraph, Text } = Typography;
interface ErrorBoundaryState {
  hasError: boolean;
  error: Error | null;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
class ErrorBoundary extends React.Component<
  React.PropsWithChildren,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  ErrorBoundaryState
> {
  state: ErrorBoundaryState = { hasError: false, error: null };
  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error): ErrorBoundaryState {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    return { hasError: true, error };
  }
  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: React.ErrorInfo) {
  }
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  handleReset = () => {
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
  };
  handleHome = () => {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    this.setState({ hasError: false, error: null });
    window.location.href = "/";
  };
  render() {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    if (this.state.hasError) {
      return (
        <div
          style={{
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
            minHeight: "100vh",
            display: "grid",
            placeItems: "center",
            padding: 24,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            background: "var(--af-bg-base, #07090f)",
          }}
        >
          <div
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            className="af-glass"
            style={{ maxWidth: 520, width: "100%", padding: "28px 20px" }}
          >
            <Result
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
              status="error"
              title={<span className="af-gradient-text">页面出现异常</span>}
              subTitle="已捕获前端运行时错误，可重试或返回首页。"
              extra={
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <Space wrap>
                  <Button
                    type="primary"
                    icon={<ReloadOutlined />}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                    onClick={this.handleReset}
                    style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none" }}
                  >
                    重试
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  </Button>
                  <Button icon={<HomeOutlined />} onClick={this.handleHome}>
                    回首页
                  </Button>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                </Space>
              }
            />
            {this.state.error?.message && (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              <Paragraph
                code
                copyable
                style={{
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  margin: "0 16px 8px",
                  fontSize: 12,
                  maxHeight: 120,
                  overflow: "auto",
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                }}
              >
                <Text type="secondary">{this.state.error.message}</Text>
              </Paragraph>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
            )}
          </div>
        </div>
      );
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    }
    return this.props.children;
  }
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export default ErrorBoundary;

export const Header: React.FC<HeaderProps> = ({ collapsed, onToggle }) => {

  const [notifyOpen, setNotifyOpen] = useState(false);
  const unread = useNotificationStore((s) => s.events.filter((e) => !e.read).length);
  const liveCount = useNotificationStore(
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    (s) =>
      s.events.filter((e) =>
        ["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(e.status)
      ).length
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  );
  const badgeCount = unread > 0 ? unread : liveCount;
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
  const locale = useI18nStore((s) => s.locale);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const setLocale = useI18nStore((s) => s.setLocale);
  return (
    <>
      <AntHeader
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        className="af-no-print"
        style={{
          background: "var(--af-header)",
          backdropFilter: "blur(14px)",
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          padding: "0 20px",
          display: "flex",

    </>
  );

// [功能] frontend/src/components/layout/MainLayout.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import React, { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Layout, Grid, Drawer } from "antd";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
import { Outlet } from "react-router-dom";
import { Sidebar } from "./Sidebar";
import { Header } from "./Header";
import { CommandPalette } from "@/components/CommandPalette";
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
import { DocumentTitle } from "@/hooks/useDocumentTitle";
import { AppBreadcrumb } from "@/components/ui/AppBreadcrumb";
import { ActivityWatcher } from "@/components/layout/ActivityWatcher";
const { Content } = Layout;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
const { useBreakpoint } = Grid;
export const MainLayout: React.FC = () => {
  const screens = useBreakpoint();
  const isMobile = !screens.lg;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  const [collapsed, setCollapsed] = useState(false);
  const [drawerOpen, setDrawerOpen] = useState(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isMobile) {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      setCollapsed(true);
      setDrawerOpen(false);
    }
  }, [isMobile]);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const toggleNav = () => {
    if (isMobile) setDrawerOpen((o) => !o);
    else setCollapsed((c) => !c);
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  };
  return (
    <Layout className="af-layout" style={{ minHeight: "100vh", background: "transparent" }}>
      <DocumentTitle />
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      <ActivityWatcher />
      {/* Desktop sider */}
      {!isMobile && (
        <Sidebar collapsed={collapsed} onToggle={() => setCollapsed((c) => !c)} />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      )}
      {/* Mobile drawer */}
      <Drawer
        placement="left"
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        open={isMobile && drawerOpen}
        onClose={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
        width={260}
        styles={{
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          body: { padding: 0, background: "var(--af-sidebar)" },
          header: { display: "none" },
        }}
        destroyOnClose={false}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      >
        <Sidebar
          collapsed={false}
          onToggle={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          mode="inline"
          onNavigate={() => setDrawerOpen(false)}
        />
      </Drawer>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      <Layout style={{ background: "transparent", minWidth: 0 }}>
        <Header collapsed={isMobile ? !drawerOpen : collapsed} onToggle={toggleNav} />
        <Content className="af-content-shell">
          <div className="af-page" style={{ minHeight: 280 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            <AppBreadcrumb />
            <Outlet />
          </div>
        </Content>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      </Layout>
      <CommandPalette />
    </Layout>
  );
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
# [设计] 评测闭环原创逻辑，保证可审查与可复现
};
