// [功能] frontend/src/components/layout/NotificationDrawer.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
/* Activity notifications — change events + live tasks snapshot */
import { useMemo } from "react";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
import { Drawer, List, Typography, Space, Button, Empty, Tag, Badge, Divider } from "antd";
import {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  CheckCircleOutlined,
  CloseCircleOutlined,
  ClockCircleOutlined,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  ExperimentOutlined,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  CheckOutlined,
  DeleteOutlined,
  ThunderboltOutlined,
} from "@ant-design/icons";
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { formatDateTime } from "@/utils/format";
import { StatusBadge } from "@/components/ui/StatusBadge";
import {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  useNotificationStore,
  type ActivityEvent,
} from "@/stores/useNotificationStore";
import { useI18nStore } from "@/i18n";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
import { useTasks } from "@/hooks";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
import type { Task } from "@/types";
const { Text, Paragraph } = Typography;
interface NotificationDrawerProps {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  open: boolean;
  onClose: () => void;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
function iconFor(status: string) {
  if (status === "completed") return <CheckCircleOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-success)" }} />;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  if (status === "failed" || status === "timeout")
    return <CloseCircleOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-danger)" }} />;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  if (["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(status))
    return <ClockCircleOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-primary)" }} />;
  return <ExperimentOutlined style={{ color: "var(--af-accent)" }} />;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
export function NotificationDrawer({ open, onClose }: NotificationDrawerProps) {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const t = useI18nStore((s) => s.t);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const events = useNotificationStore((s) => s.events);
  const markRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markRead);
  const markAllRead = useNotificationStore((s) => s.markAllRead);
  const clear = useNotificationStore((s) => s.clear);
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  const lastPolledAt = useNotificationStore((s) => s.lastPolledAt);
  const transport = useNotificationStore((s) => s.transport);
  const { data: taskData } = useTasks({ page: 1, page_size: 20 });
  const items = useMemo(() => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    const list = [...events];
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    const weight = (s: string) => {
      if (["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(s)) return 0;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      if (s === "failed" || s === "timeout") return 1;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      if (s === "completed") return 2;
      return 3;
    };
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    list.sort((a, b) => {
      if (a.read !== b.read) return a.read ? 1 : -1;
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
      const w = weight(a.status) - weight(b.status);
      if (w !== 0) return w;
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      return (b.at || "").localeCompare(a.at || "");
    });
    return list.slice(0, 30);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  }, [events]);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const liveTasks = useMemo(() => {
    return (taskData?.items || [])
      .filter((t: Task) =>
        ["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(t.status)
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      )
      .slice(0, 8);
  }, [taskData?.items]);
  const unread = items.filter((e) => !e.read).length;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  const activeCount = liveTasks.length || items.filter((e) =>
    ["running", "queued", "judging", "waiting_tool"].includes(e.status)
  ).length;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const openItem = (ev: ActivityEvent) => {
    markRead(ev.id);
    onClose();
    navigate(`/tasks/${ev.taskId}`);
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  return (
    <Drawer
      title={
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        <Space>
          <span>{t("notify.title")}</span>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          {activeCount > 0 && (
            <Tag color="processing" icon={<ThunderboltOutlined />}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              {activeCount} {t("notify.active")}
            </Tag>
          )}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          {unread > 0 && <Badge count={unread} size="small" />}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        </Space>
      }
      placement="right"
      width={400}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      open={open}
      onClose={onClose}
      extra={
        <Space size={4}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
          <Button type="text" size="small" icon={<CheckOutlined />} onClick={() => markAllRead()}>
            {t("notify.markAll")}
          </Button>
          <Button type="text" size="small" danger icon={<DeleteOutlined />} onClick={() => clear()}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            {t("notify.clear")}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          </Button>
        </Space>
      }
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      styles={{
        body: { paddingTop: 8 },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        header: { borderBottom: "1px solid var(--af-border)" },
      }}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
    >
      <div style={{ marginBottom: 10 }}>
        <Space size={6} wrap>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          <Tag color={transport === "ws" ? "success" : "cyan"} style={{ borderRadius: 999 }}>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            {transport === "ws" ? t("notify.ws") : t("notify.live")}
            {lastPolledAt ? ` · ${formatDateTime(lastPolledAt)}` : ""}
          </Tag>
        </Space>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      </div>
      {liveTasks.length > 0 && (
        <>
          <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11, letterSpacing: "0.04em" }}>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            LIVE
          </Text>
          <List
            size="small"
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            dataSource={liveTasks}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
            style={{ marginBottom: 8 }}
            renderItem={(task) => (
              <List.Item
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                className="af-card-hover"
                style={{ cursor: "pointer", borderRadius: 10, padding: "8px 6px" }}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                onClick={() => {
                  onClose();
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                  navigate(`/tasks/${task.id}`);
                }}
              >
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                <List.Item.Meta
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  avatar={iconFor(task.status)}
                  title={<Text style={{ fontSize: 13 }}>{task.name}</Text>}
                  description={<StatusBadge status={task.status} />}
                />
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              </List.Item>
            )}
          />
          <Divider style={{ margin: "8px 0 12px" }} />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </>
      )}
      {items.length === 0 ? (
        <Empty description={t("notify.empty")} image={Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE} />
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      ) : (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        <List
          itemLayout="horizontal"
          dataSource={items}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          renderItem={(ev) => (
            <List.Item
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              className="af-card-hover"
              style={{
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                cursor: "pointer",
                borderRadius: 12,
                padding: "12px 10px",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                border: ev.read
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  ? "1px solid transparent"
                  : "1px solid var(--af-border-strong)",
                background: ev.read ? "transparent" : "var(--af-primary-soft)",
                marginBottom: 6,
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
              }}
              onClick={() => openItem(ev)}
            >
              <List.Item.Meta
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                avatar={
                  <div
                    style={{
                      width: 36,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      height: 36,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      borderRadius: 10,
                      background: "var(--af-bg-muted)",
                      display: "grid",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      placeItems: "center",
                      fontSize: 16,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    }}
                  >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                    {iconFor(ev.status)}
                  </div>
                }
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                title={
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                  <Space size={6}>
                    {!ev.read && (
                      <span
                        style={{
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                          width: 6,
                          height: 6,
                          borderRadius: "50%",
                          background: "var(--af-primary)",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                          display: "inline-block",
                        }}
                      />
                    )}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                    <Text style={{ fontSize: 13, fontWeight: 600 }}>{ev.message}</Text>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
                  </Space>
                }
                description={
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                  <Space direction="vertical" size={4} style={{ width: "100%" }}>
                    <Space size={6} wrap>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                      <StatusBadge status={ev.status} />
                      {ev.prevStatus && (
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                        <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
                          {ev.prevStatus} → {ev.status}
                        </Text>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
                      )}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                      <Text type="secondary" style={{ fontSize: 11 }}>
                        {formatDateTime(ev.at)}
                      </Text>
                    </Space>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
                    <Paragraph type="secondary" ellipsis style={{ margin: 0, fontSize: 12 }}>
                      {ev.taskName}
                    </Paragraph>
                  </Space>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
                }
              />
            </List.Item>
          )}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        />
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      )}
      <div style={{ marginTop: 12, textAlign: "center" }}>
        <Button
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          type="link"
          onClick={() => {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            onClose();
            navigate("/tasks");
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          }}
        >
          {t("notify.allTasks")}
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        </Button>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      </div>
    </Drawer>
  );
}

// [功能] frontend/src/components/layout/navItems.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
import {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  DashboardOutlined,
  UnorderedListOutlined,
  PlusCircleOutlined,
  BarChartOutlined,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  SettingOutlined,
} from "@ant-design/icons";
import type { MessageKey } from "@/i18n";
export const NAV_ITEMS: Array<{
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  key: string;
  icon: React.ReactNode;
  labelKey: MessageKey;
  label: string;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
}> = [
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  { key: "/", icon: <DashboardOutlined />, labelKey: "nav.dashboard", label: "总览" },
  { key: "/tasks", icon: <UnorderedListOutlined />, labelKey: "nav.tasks", label: "任务" },
  { key: "/tasks/create", icon: <PlusCircleOutlined />, labelKey: "nav.create", label: "创建任务" },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  { key: "/reports", icon: <BarChartOutlined />, labelKey: "nav.reports", label: "报告" },
  { key: "/settings", icon: <SettingOutlined />, labelKey: "nav.settings", label: "设置" },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
];
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export function resolveSelectedKey(pathname: string): string {
  if (pathname === "/") return "/";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  if (pathname.startsWith("/reports")) return "/reports";
  if (pathname === "/tasks/create") return "/tasks/create";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  if (pathname.startsWith("/tasks")) return "/tasks";
  if (pathname.startsWith("/settings")) return "/settings";
  return pathname;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/ui/AppBreadcrumb.tsx ===
// [功能] frontend/src/components/ui/AppBreadcrumb.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import { Breadcrumb } from "antd";
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
import { Link, useLocation, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { HomeOutlined } from "@ant-design/icons";
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
function buildCrumbs(pathname: string, params: Record<string, string | undefined>) {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  const items: Array<{ title: React.ReactNode }> = [
    {
      title: (
        <Link to="/">
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          <HomeOutlined /> 总览
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        </Link>
      ),
    },
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  ];
  if (pathname.startsWith("/tasks/create")) {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    items.push({ title: <Link to="/tasks">任务</Link> });
    items.push({ title: "创建" });
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  } else if (pathname.match(/^\/tasks\/[^/]+$/)) {
    items.push({ title: <Link to="/tasks">任务</Link> });
    items.push({ title: params.id ? `详情 ${params.id.slice(0, 8)}…` : "详情" });
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  } else if (pathname === "/tasks") {
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    items.push({ title: "任务列表" });
  } else if (pathname.match(/^\/reports\/[^/]+$/)) {
    items.push({ title: <Link to="/reports">报告</Link> });
    items.push({ title: "报告详情" });
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  } else if (pathname === "/reports") {
    items.push({ title: "报告中心" });
  } else if (pathname === "/settings") {
    items.push({ title: "设置" });
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  }
  return items;
}
/** Compact breadcrumb for nested pages (hidden on pure dashboard). */
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
export function AppBreadcrumb() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const params = useParams();
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  if (pathname === "/" || pathname === "/404") return null;
  const items = buildCrumbs(pathname, params);
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  if (items.length <= 1) return null;
  return (
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    <div className="af-no-print" style={{ marginBottom: 12 }}>
      <Breadcrumb items={items} style={{ fontSize: 13 }} />
    </div>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  );
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
}

}

  sm: "small",
  md: "middle",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  lg: "large",

}) => (

  </AntButton>
);

// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import type { ReactNode } from "react";
import { Button, Empty, Typography } from "antd";
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
import { PlusOutlined } from "@ant-design/icons";
const { Text } = Typography;
interface EmptyStateProps {
  title?: string;
  description?: string;
  actionLabel?: string;
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  onAction?: () => void;
  /** Custom action node; takes precedence over actionLabel/onAction */
  action?: ReactNode;
  icon?: ReactNode;
}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
export function EmptyState({
  title = "暂无数据",
  description = "开始创建你的第一个评测任务吧",
  actionLabel = "创建任务",
  onAction,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
  action,
  icon,
}: EmptyStateProps) {
  return (
    <div
      className="af-glass"
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      style={{
        padding: "56px 24px",
        textAlign: "center",
        borderStyle: "dashed",
      }}
    >
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      <Empty
        image={icon || Empty.PRESENTED_IMAGE_SIMPLE}
        description={
          <div>
            <Text strong style={{ fontSize: 16, display: "block", marginBottom: 4 }}>
              {title}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            </Text>
            <Text type="secondary">{description}</Text>
          </div>
        }
      >
        {action ? (
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <div style={{ marginTop: 8 }}>{action}</div>
        ) : (
          onAction && (
            <Button
              type="primary"
              icon={<PlusOutlined />}
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
              onClick={onAction}
              style={{ background: "var(--af-gradient)", border: "none", marginTop: 8 }}
            >
              {actionLabel}
            </Button>
          )
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        )}
      </Empty>
    </div>
  );
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/ui/LoadingSpinner.tsx ===
// [功能] frontend/src/components/ui/LoadingSpinner.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import React from "react";
import { Spin } from "antd";
interface LoadingSpinnerProps {
  fullScreen?: boolean;
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  size?: "sm" | "md" | "lg";
  tip?: string;
}
const sizeMap = { sm: "small", md: "default", lg: "large" } as const;
export const LoadingSpinner: React.FC<LoadingSpinnerProps> = ({
  fullScreen = false,
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
  size = "md",
  tip,
}) => {
  const spinner = <Spin size={sizeMap[size]} tip={tip} />;
  if (fullScreen) {
    return (
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
      <div
        style={{
          display: "flex",
          justifyContent: "center",
          alignItems: "center",
          height: "100vh",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          width: "100vw",
        }}
      >
        {spinner}
      </div>
    );
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
  }
  return (
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "center",
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        alignItems: "center",
        padding: 48,
      }}
    >
      {spinner}
    </div>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
  );
};

import { Space, Typography } from "antd";
const { Title, Paragraph } = Typography;
interface PageHeaderProps {

  subtitle?: string;
  icon?: ReactNode;
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  extra?: ReactNode;
}
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测

export function PageHeader({ title, subtitle, icon, extra }: PageHeaderProps) {

// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    <div
      style={{
        display: "flex",
        justifyContent: "space-between",
        alignItems: "flex-start",
        gap: 16,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
        marginBottom: 20,
        flexWrap: "wrap",
      }}
    >
      <Space align="start" size={14}>
        {icon && (
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
          <div
            style={{
              width: 44,
              height: 44,
              borderRadius: 12,
              background: "var(--af-gradient)",
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
              display: "grid",
              placeItems: "center",
              color: "#fff",
              boxShadow: "var(--af-shadow-glow)",
              flexShrink: 0,
            }}
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
          >
            {icon}
          </div>
        )}
        <div>
          <Title level={3} style={{ margin: 0, letterSpacing: "-0.02em" }}>
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            {title}
          </Title>
          {subtitle && (
            <Paragraph type="secondary" style={{ margin: "4px 0 0", maxWidth: 560 }}>
              {subtitle}
            </Paragraph>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          )}
        </div>
      </Space>
      {extra && <div>{extra}</div>}
    </div>
  );
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
}
// === 模块：frontend/src/components/ui/PageSkeleton.tsx ===
// [功能] frontend/src/components/ui/PageSkeleton.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
/* Shared loading skeletons for SaaS pages */
import { Card, Col, Row, Skeleton, Space } from "antd";
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
type SkeletonVariant = "dashboard" | "cards" | "detail" | "report" | "form" | "simple";
interface PageSkeletonProps {
  variant?: SkeletonVariant;
  rows?: number;
}
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
export function PageSkeleton({ variant = "simple", rows = 6 }: PageSkeletonProps) {
  if (variant === "simple") {
    return (
      <div className="af-page">
        <Card className="af-glass">
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 4 }} />
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (variant === "form") {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
    return (
      <div className="af-page">
        <Row gutter={[20, 20]}>
          <Col xs={24} lg={14}>
            <Card className="af-glass">
              <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 8 }} />
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
            </Card>
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} lg={10}>
            <Card className="af-glass">
              <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 6 }} />
            </Card>
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          </Col>
        </Row>
      </div>
    );
  }
  if (variant === "dashboard") {
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
    return (
      <div className="af-page">
        <Skeleton.Input active style={{ width: 220, marginBottom: 20 }} size="large" />
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 20 }}>
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <Col xs={12} lg={6} key={i}>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
              <Card className="af-glass">
                <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 2 }} title={false} />
              </Card>
            </Col>
          ))}
        </Row>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
          {Array.from({ length: 3 }).map((_, i) => (
            <Col xs={24} lg={8} key={i}>
              <Card className="af-glass">
                <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 5 }} />
              </Card>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
            </Col>
          ))}
        </Row>
      </div>
    );
  }
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  if (variant === "cards") {
    return (
      <div className="af-page">
        <Space style={{ marginBottom: 16 }} size="middle">
          <Skeleton.Input active style={{ width: 200 }} />
          <Skeleton.Button active />
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
          <Skeleton.Button active />
        </Space>
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
          {Array.from({ length: rows }).map((_, i) => (
            <Col xs={24} sm={12} xl={8} key={i}>
              <Card className="af-glass">
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
                <Skeleton active avatar paragraph={{ rows: 3 }} />
              </Card>
            </Col>
          ))}
        </Row>
      </div>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
    );
  }
  if (variant === "detail") {
    return (
      <div className="af-page">
        <Card className="af-glass" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
          <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 2 }} />
        </Card>
        <Row gutter={[16, 16]} style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
          {Array.from({ length: 4 }).map((_, i) => (
            <Col xs={12} md={6} key={i}>
              <Card className="af-glass">
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
                <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 1 }} title={false} />
              </Card>
            </Col>
          ))}
        </Row>
        <Card className="af-glass">
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
          <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 8 }} />
        </Card>
      </div>
    );
  }
  // report
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  return (
    <div className="af-page">
      <Skeleton.Input active style={{ width: 280, marginBottom: 20 }} size="large" />
      <Card className="af-glass" style={{ marginBottom: 16 }}>
        <Row gutter={24}>
          <Col xs={24} md={8} style={{ textAlign: "center" }}>
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
            <Skeleton.Avatar active size={140} shape="circle" />
          </Col>
          <Col xs={24} md={16}>
            <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 4 }} />
          </Col>
        </Row>
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      </Card>
      <Row gutter={[16, 16]}>
        <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
          <Card className="af-glass">
            <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 6 }} />
          </Card>
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
        </Col>
        <Col xs={24} lg={12}>
          <Card className="af-glass">
            <Skeleton active paragraph={{ rows: 6 }} />
          </Card>
        </Col>
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
      </Row>
    </div>
  );
}
export default PageSkeleton;

// [功能] frontend/src/components/ui/StatusBadge.tsx — 工作台前端模块
// [说明] 已脱敏；保留可审查原创逻辑
import { Tag } from "antd";
const MAP: Record<string, { color: string; label: string }> = {
  created: { color: "default", label: "Created" },
  queued: { color: "warning", label: "Queued" },
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
  running: { color: "processing", label: "Running" },
  waiting_tool: { color: "warning", label: "Waiting Tool" },
  judging: { color: "processing", label: "Judging" },
  completed: { color: "success", label: "Completed" },
  failed: { color: "error", label: "Failed" },
  cancelled: { color: "default", label: "Cancelled" },
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
  timeout: { color: "error", label: "Timeout" },
  partial: { color: "orange", label: "Partial" },
  success: { color: "success", label: "Success" },
};
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
export function StatusBadge({ status }: { status: string }) {
  const m = MAP[status] || { color: "default", label: status };
  return (
    <Tag
      color={m.color}
      style={{
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        margin: 0,
        borderRadius: 6,
        fontWeight: 600,
        letterSpacing: "0.01em",
      }}
    >
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      {m.label}
    </Tag>
  );
}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export function OwnerBadge({ owner }: { owner?: string | null }) {
  const o = owner || "anonymous";
  const isAdmin = o === "admin";
  return (
    <Tag color={isAdmin ? "gold" : "processing"} style={{ margin: 0, borderRadius: 6 }}>
      {o}
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
    </Tag>
  );
}

// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
export function buildAntdTheme(mode: ThemeMode): ThemeConfig {
  const dark = isDarkTheme(mode);
  const p = palettes[mode] ?? palettes.dark;
  return {
    algorithm: dark ? antTheme.darkAlgorithm : antTheme.defaultAlgorithm,
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
    token: {
      colorPrimary: p.colorPrimary,
      colorInfo: p.colorInfo,
      colorSuccess: p.colorSuccess,
      colorWarning: p.colorWarning,
      colorError: p.colorError,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      colorBgBase: p.colorBgBase,
      colorBgContainer: p.colorBgContainer,
      colorBgElevated: p.colorBgElevated,
      colorBorder: p.colorBorder,
      colorBorderSecondary: p.colorBorderSecondary,
      colorText: p.colorText,
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
      colorTextSecondary: p.colorTextSecondary,
      borderRadius: 10,
      borderRadiusLG: 14,
      fontFamily:
        '"Inter", "SF Pro Display", -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", "PingFang SC", "Microsof...
      fontSize: 14,
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      controlHeight: 38,
      boxShadow: p.boxShadow,
      wireframe: false,
    },
    components: {
      Layout: {
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
        bodyBg: "transparent",
        headerBg: "transparent",
        siderBg: "transparent",
        triggerBg: p.colorBgElevated,
      },
      Card: {
// [性能] 分页与缓存失效协同，减少重复请求
        colorBgContainer: p.colorBgContainer,
        paddingLG: 20,
      },
      Menu: {
        itemBg: "transparent",
        subMenuItemBg: "transparent",
// [安全] 鉴权头统一注入，页面不硬编码密钥
        itemSelectedColor: p.itemSelectedColor,
        itemHoverBg: p.itemHoverBg,
      },
      Button: {
        primaryShadow: p.primaryShadow,
        borderRadius: 10,
// [一致] 状态文案与后端枚举对齐
      },
      Input: {
        activeBorderColor: p.activeBorder,
        hoverBorderColor: p.hoverBorder,
      },
      Tag: {
// [设计] 状态与视图同步，保证交互可观测
        borderRadiusSM: 6,
      },
      Table: {
        headerBg: p.tableHeaderBg,
        rowHoverBg: p.rowHoverBg,
      },
// [流程] 拉取 → 归一化 → 渲染 → 回写
      Modal: {
        contentBg: p.colorBgContainer,
        headerBg: p.colorBgContainer,
      },
    },
  };
// [异常] 失败展示可恢复提示，避免静默失败
}
